theorem liouville10_common_field (a : Fin 10 → Fin 10 → ℝ)
    (ha : ∀ s i, IsAlgebraic ℚ (a s i)) :
    ∃ K : IntermediateField ℚ ℝ, FiniteDimensional ℚ K ∧
      ∀ s i, a s i ∈ K := by
  let S : Set ℝ := Set.range (fun p : Fin 10 × Fin 10 => a p.1 p.2)
  let K := IntermediateField.adjoin ℚ S
  haveI : Finite S := Set.finite_range _
  have hfin : FiniteDimensional ℚ K := IntermediateField.finiteDimensional_adjoin (by
    rintro x ⟨⟨s,i⟩,rfl⟩
    exact (ha s i).isIntegral)
  exact ⟨K,hfin,fun s i => IntermediateField.subset_adjoin ℚ S ⟨(s,i),rfl⟩⟩

theorem liouville10_common_denominator (K : IntermediateField ℚ ℝ) [FiniteDimensional ℚ K]
    (b : Fin 10 → Fin 10 → K) :
    ∃ M : ℤ, M ≠ 0 ∧ ∀ s i, IsIntegral ℤ ((M : K) * b s i) := by
  haveI : Algebra.IsAlgebraic ℚ K := Algebra.IsAlgebraic.of_finite ℚ K
  haveI : Algebra.IsAlgebraic ℤ K := (IsFractionRing.comap_isAlgebraic_iff (A := ℤ) (K := ℚ) (C := K)).mpr inferInstance
  obtain ⟨M,hM,hi⟩ := Algebra.IsAlgebraic.exists_integral_multiples ℤ
    (Finset.univ.image (fun p : Fin 10 × Fin 10 => b p.1 p.2))
  refine ⟨M,hM,fun s i => ?_⟩
  simpa only [Algebra.smul_def,eq_intCast] using hi (b s i) (Finset.mem_image.mpr ⟨(s,i),Finset.mem_univ _,rfl⟩)

theorem liouville10_conjugate_height (K : IntermediateField ℚ ℝ) [FiniteDimensional ℚ K]
    (b : Fin 10 → Fin 10 → K) :
    ∃ B : ℝ, 0 < B ∧ ∀ (sigma : K →ₐ[ℚ] ℂ) s (z : Fin 10 → ℤ),
      ‖sigma (∑ i, b s i * (z i : K))‖ ≤
        B * max 1 ‖(fun i => (z i : ℝ))‖ := by
  classical
  let B := max 1 (∑ sigma : K →ₐ[ℚ] ℂ, ∑ s : Fin 10, ∑ i : Fin 10, ‖sigma (b s i)‖)
  refine ⟨B,lt_of_lt_of_le zero_lt_one (le_max_left _ _),?_⟩
  intro sigma s z
  have hrow : (∑ i : Fin 10, ‖sigma (b s i)‖) ≤ B := by
    apply le_trans _ (le_max_right _ _)
    have hs : (∑ i : Fin 10, ‖sigma (b s i)‖) ≤ ∑ t : Fin 10, ∑ i : Fin 10, ‖sigma (b t i)‖ :=
      Finset.single_le_sum (s := Finset.univ) (f := fun t : Fin 10 => ∑ i : Fin 10, ‖sigma (b t i)‖) (fun t _ => Finset.sum_nonneg (fun i _ => norm_nonneg _)) (Finset.mem_univ s)
    have ht : (∑ t : Fin 10, ∑ i : Fin 10, ‖sigma (b t i)‖) ≤ ∑ tau : K →ₐ[ℚ] ℂ, ∑ t : Fin 10, ∑ i : Fin 10, ‖tau (b t i)‖ :=
      Finset.single_le_sum (s := Finset.univ) (f := fun tau : K →ₐ[ℚ] ℂ => ∑ t : Fin 10, ∑ i : Fin 10, ‖tau (b t i)‖) (fun t _ => Finset.sum_nonneg (fun j _ => Finset.sum_nonneg (fun i _ => norm_nonneg _))) (Finset.mem_univ sigma)
    exact hs.trans ht
  have hi (i : Fin 10) : |(z i : ℝ)| ≤ max 1 ‖(fun i => (z i : ℝ))‖ := by
    have hh : |(z i : ℝ)| ≤ ‖(fun i => (z i : ℝ))‖ := by
      simpa only [Real.norm_eq_abs] using norm_le_pi_norm (fun i => (z i : ℝ)) i
    exact hh.trans (le_max_right _ _)
  calc
    ‖sigma (∑ i, b s i * (z i : K))‖ = ‖∑ i, sigma (b s i) * (z i : ℂ)‖ := by simp only [map_sum,map_mul,map_intCast]
    _ ≤ ∑ i : Fin 10, ‖sigma (b s i)‖ * |(z i : ℝ)| := by simpa only [norm_mul,Complex.norm_intCast] using norm_sum_le (s := Finset.univ) (f := fun i => sigma (b s i) * (z i : ℂ))
    _ ≤ ∑ i : Fin 10, ‖sigma (b s i)‖ * max 1 ‖(fun i => (z i : ℝ))‖ := Finset.sum_le_sum fun i _ => mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (hi i) (norm_nonneg _)
    _ = (∑ i : Fin 10, ‖sigma (b s i)‖) * max 1 ‖(fun i => (z i : ℝ))‖ := (Finset.sum_mul _ _ _).symm
    _ ≤ B * max 1 ‖(fun i => (z i : ℝ))‖ := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hrow (le_trans zero_le_one (le_max_left _ _))

theorem liouville10_field_liouville (K : IntermediateField ℚ ℝ) [FiniteDimensional ℚ K]
    (b : Fin 10 → Fin 10 → K) :
    ∃ C : ℝ, 0 < C ∧ ∃ D : ℕ, ∀ s (z : Fin 10 → ℤ),
      (∑ i, (b s i : ℝ) * (z i : ℝ)) ≠ 0 →
      C ≤ |∑ i, (b s i : ℝ) * (z i : ℝ)| * (max 1 ‖(fun i => (z i : ℝ))‖)^D := by
  classical
  obtain ⟨M,hM,hint⟩ := liouville10_common_denominator K b
  let c : Fin 10 → Fin 10 → K := fun s i => (M : K) * b s i
  obtain ⟨B,hB,hheight⟩ := liouville10_conjugate_height K c
  let sigma0 : K →ₐ[ℚ] ℂ := (IsScalarTower.toAlgHom ℚ ℝ ℂ).comp K.val
  let D := Fintype.card (K →ₐ[ℚ] ℂ) - 1
  have hMr : (M : ℝ) ≠ 0 := by exact_mod_cast hM
  have hden : 0 < |(M : ℝ)| * B^D := mul_pos (abs_pos.mpr hMr) (pow_pos hB _)
  refine ⟨(|(M : ℝ)| * B^D)⁻¹,inv_pos.mpr hden,D,?_⟩
  intro s z hz
  let beta : K := ∑ i, c s i * (z i : K)
  have hb : IsIntegral ℤ beta := IsIntegral.sum _ (fun i _ =>
    (hint s i).mul (isIntegral_algebraMap : IsIntegral ℤ ((algebraMap ℤ K) (z i))))
  have he : sigma0 beta = ((M : ℝ) * (∑ i, (b s i : ℝ) * (z i : ℝ)) : ℝ) := by
    simp [beta,c,sigma0,Finset.mul_sum,mul_assoc]
  have hb0 : beta ≠ 0 := by
    intro h
    have hh := he
    rw [h,map_zero] at hh
    have hh' : (M : ℝ) * (∑ i, (b s i : ℝ) * (z i : ℝ)) = 0 := by
      exact_mod_cast hh.symm
    exact (mul_ne_zero hMr hz) hh'
  have hp := liouville6_integral_norm_lower K beta hb hb0
  rw [liouville6_norm_product] at hp
  rw [← Finset.mul_prod_erase Finset.univ (fun sigma : K →ₐ[ℚ] ℂ => ‖sigma beta‖)
    (Finset.mem_univ sigma0)] at hp
  have hrest : (∏ sigma ∈ Finset.univ.erase sigma0, ‖sigma beta‖) ≤
      (B * max 1 ‖(fun i => (z i : ℝ))‖)^D := by
    calc
      _ ≤ ∏ _sigma ∈ Finset.univ.erase sigma0, B * max 1 ‖(fun i => (z i : ℝ))‖ :=
        Finset.prod_le_prod (fun _ _ => norm_nonneg _) (fun sigma _ => hheight sigma s z)
      _ = _ := by rw [Finset.prod_const,Finset.card_erase_of_mem (Finset.mem_univ sigma0)]; rfl
  have htotal : 1 ≤ (|(M : ℝ)| * B^D) *
      (|∑ i, (b s i : ℝ) * (z i : ℝ)| * (max 1 ‖(fun i => (z i : ℝ))‖)^D) := by
    calc
      1 ≤ ‖sigma0 beta‖ * (∏ sigma ∈ Finset.univ.erase sigma0, ‖sigma beta‖) := hp
      _ ≤ ‖sigma0 beta‖ * (B * max 1 ‖(fun i => (z i : ℝ))‖)^D :=
        mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hrest (norm_nonneg _)
      _ = _ := by rw [he,Complex.norm_real,Real.norm_eq_abs,abs_mul,mul_pow]; ring
  rw [← one_div]
  exact (div_le_iff₀ hden).mpr (by simpa only [mul_comm] using htotal)

theorem algebraic_fin10_liouville_lower
    (a : Fin 10 → Fin 10 → ℝ) (ha : ∀ s i, IsAlgebraic ℚ (a s i)) :
    ∃ C : ℝ, 0 < C ∧ ∃ D : ℕ, ∀ s : Fin 10, ∀ z : Fin 10 → ℤ,
      (∑ i, a s i * (z i : ℝ)) ≠ 0 →
      C ≤ |∑ i, a s i * (z i : ℝ)| * (max 1 ‖integerReal z‖)^D := by
  obtain ⟨K,hK,hmem⟩ := liouville10_common_field a ha
  letI : FiniteDimensional ℚ K := hK
  let b : Fin 10 → Fin 10 → K := fun s i => ⟨a s i,hmem s i⟩
  exact liouville10_field_liouville K b

end CompoundFeasibility

end
end M197Literal

namespace CompoundFeasibility.SourceHermite10
open SourceHermite
variable {K : Type*} [Field K]

def frameChart10 (w : Fin 9 → Fin 10 → K) (i : Fin 10) : MvPolynomial (Fin 9) K :=
  ∑ j, MvPolynomial.C (w j i)*MvPolynomial.X j

def frameSubstitution10 (w : Fin 9 → Fin 10 → K) :
    MvPolynomial (Fin 10) K →+* MvPolynomial (Fin 9) K :=
  MvPolynomial.eval₂Hom MvPolynomial.C (frameChart10 w)

theorem frameChart10_eval (w : Fin 9 → Fin 10 → K) (x : Fin 9 → K) (i : Fin 10) :
    MvPolynomial.eval x (frameChart10 w i) = ∑ j, x j*w j i := by
  simp only [frameChart10,map_sum,map_mul,MvPolynomial.eval_C,MvPolynomial.eval_X]
  apply Finset.sum_congr rfl
  intro j _
  ring

theorem frameSubstitution10_eval (w : Fin 9 → Fin 10 → K)
    (P : MvPolynomial (Fin 10) K) (x : Fin 9 → K) :
    MvPolynomial.eval x (frameSubstitution10 w P) =
      MvPolynomial.eval (fun i => ∑ j, x j*w j i) P := by
  have he : (MvPolynomial.eval x).comp (frameSubstitution10 w) =
      MvPolynomial.eval (fun i => ∑ j, x j*w j i) := by
    apply MvPolynomial.ringHom_ext
    · intro c
      simp [frameSubstitution10]
    · intro i
      simp only [RingHom.comp_apply,frameSubstitution10,MvPolynomial.eval₂Hom_X']
      simpa only [MvPolynomial.eval_X] using frameChart10_eval w x i
  exact congrArg (fun f : MvPolynomial (Fin 10) K →+* K => f P) he

theorem frameChart10_pderiv (w : Fin 9 → Fin 10 → K) (j : Fin 9) (i : Fin 10) :
    MvPolynomial.pderiv j (frameChart10 w i) = MvPolynomial.C (w j i) := by
  simp [frameChart10,map_sum,MvPolynomial.pderiv_C_mul,MvPolynomial.pderiv_X,Pi.single_apply]

theorem frameSubstitution10_chain (w : Fin 9 → Fin 10 → K)
    (P : MvPolynomial (Fin 10) K) (j : Fin 9) :
    MvPolynomial.pderiv j (frameSubstitution10 w P) =
      ∑ i : Fin 10, MvPolynomial.C (w j i)*frameSubstitution10 w (MvPolynomial.pderiv i P) := by
  classical
  induction P using MvPolynomial.induction_on with
  | C c => simp [frameSubstitution10,MvPolynomial.pderiv_C]
  | add P Q hP hQ =>
    simp only [map_add,hP,hQ,mul_add,Finset.sum_add_distrib]
  | mul_X P i hP =>
    have hX : frameSubstitution10 w (MvPolynomial.X i) = frameChart10 w i := by
      simp [frameSubstitution10]
    have hdelta : (∑ k : Fin 10, (MvPolynomial.C (w j k)*frameSubstitution10 w P)*
        (if k = i then 1 else 0)) = frameSubstitution10 w P*MvPolynomial.C (w j i) := by
      simp [mul_ite,Finset.sum_ite_eq',Finset.sum_ite_eq,mul_comm]
    have hD (k : Fin 10) :
        frameSubstitution10 w (MvPolynomial.pderiv k (P*MvPolynomial.X i)) =
          frameSubstitution10 w (MvPolynomial.pderiv k P)*frameChart10 w i +
          frameSubstitution10 w P*(if k = i then 1 else 0) := by
      by_cases he : k = i <;>
        simp [MvPolynomial.pderiv_mul,hX,MvPolynomial.pderiv_X,Pi.single_apply,he,eq_comm] <;> ring
    rw [map_mul,MvPolynomial.pderiv_mul,hP,hX,frameChart10_pderiv]
    simp_rw [hD,mul_add,← mul_assoc]
    rw [Finset.sum_add_distrib,← Finset.sum_mul,hdelta]



theorem frame_coordinates_exist10 (w : Fin 9 → Fin 10 → K) (y : Fin 10 → K)
    (hy : y ∈ Submodule.span K (Set.range w)) :
    ∃ x : Fin 9 → K, ∀ i, y i = ∑ j, x j*w j i := by
  obtain ⟨x,hx⟩ := (Submodule.mem_span_range_iff_exists_fun K).mp hy
  refine ⟨x,?_⟩
  intro i
  have he := congrFun hx i
  simpa only [Finset.sum_apply,Pi.smul_apply,smul_eq_mul] using he.symm


theorem frame_hasse_zero_transport10 [CharZero K] (w : Fin 9 → Fin 10 → K)
    (P : MvPolynomial (Fin 10) K) (x : Fin 9 → K) (T : ℕ) (A : Fin 9 → ℕ)
    (hA : (∑ j, A j) ≤ T)
    (hz : ∀ J : Fin 10 → ℕ, (∑ i, J i) ≤ T →
      gridJet6 P J (fun i => ∑ j, x j*w j i) = 0) :
    gridJet6 (frameSubstitution10 w P) A x = 0 := by
  classical
  induction T generalizing P A with
  | zero =>
    have hAz : A = 0 := by
      funext j
      have hj := Finset.single_le_sum (fun i _ => Nat.zero_le (A i)) (Finset.mem_univ j)
      have ha : A j = 0 := by omega
      exact ha
    rw [hAz]
    change gridJet6 (frameSubstitution10 w P) (fun _ => 0) x = 0
    rw [gridJet6_zero,frameSubstitution10_eval]
    simpa only [gridJet6_zero] using hz (fun _ => 0) (by simp)
  | succ T ih =>
    by_cases hAz : A = 0
    · rw [hAz]
      change gridJet6 (frameSubstitution10 w P) (fun _ => 0) x = 0
      rw [gridJet6_zero,frameSubstitution10_eval]
      simpa only [gridJet6_zero] using hz (fun _ => 0) (by simp)
    · obtain ⟨j,hjn⟩ := Function.ne_iff.mp hAz
      have hj : 0 < A j := Nat.pos_of_ne_zero hjn
      let B := Function.update A j (A j-1)
      have hBsum : (∑ k, B k)+1 = ∑ k, A k := by
        have he := jet_order_update6 A j (A j-1)
        dsimp [B]
        omega
      have hB : (∑ k, B k) ≤ T := by omega
      have hBA : Function.update B j (B j+1) = A := by
        funext k
        by_cases hk : k = j
        · subst k; simp [B,show A j-1+1 = A j by omega]
        · simp [B,hk]
      have hzD : gridJet6 (MvPolynomial.pderiv j (frameSubstitution10 w P)) B x = 0 := by
        rw [frameSubstitution10_chain,gridJet6_sum]
        apply Finset.sum_eq_zero
        intro i _
        rw [gridJet6_C_mul]
        have hi : gridJet6 (frameSubstitution10 w (MvPolynomial.pderiv i P)) B x = 0 := by
          apply ih _ B hB
          intro J hJ
          rw [gridJet6_pderiv]
          have he := jet_order_update6 J i (J i+1)
          have hinc : (∑ k, Function.update J i (J i+1) k) ≤ T+1 := by omega
          rw [hz _ hinc,mul_zero]
        rw [hi,mul_zero]
      rw [gridJet6_pderiv,hBA] at hzD
      exact (mul_eq_zero.mp hzD).resolve_left (by exact_mod_cast Nat.succ_ne_zero (B j))


theorem frameChart10_degree (w : Fin 9 → Fin 10 → K) (i : Fin 10) :
    (frameChart10 w i).totalDegree ≤ 1 := by
  apply MvPolynomial.totalDegree_finsetSum_le
  intro j _
  have h := MvPolynomial.totalDegree_mul (MvPolynomial.C (w j i)) (MvPolynomial.X j)
  simpa using h

theorem frameSubstitution10_degree (w : Fin 9 → Fin 10 → K)
    (P : MvPolynomial (Fin 10) K) :
    (frameSubstitution10 w P).totalDegree ≤ P.totalDegree := by
  classical
  conv_lhs => rw [P.as_sum]
  rw [map_sum]
  apply MvPolynomial.totalDegree_finsetSum_le
  intro d hd
  rw [frameSubstitution10,MvPolynomial.eval₂Hom_monomial]
  have hp : (d.prod fun i e => frameChart10 w i^e).totalDegree ≤ d.sum (fun _ e => e) := by
    unfold Finsupp.prod Finsupp.sum
    apply (MvPolynomial.totalDegree_finset_prod _ _).trans
    apply Finset.sum_le_sum
    intro i _
    exact (MvPolynomial.totalDegree_pow _ _).trans (by
      have h := frameChart10_degree w i
      nlinarith)
  calc
    _ ≤ (d.prod fun i e => frameChart10 w i^e).totalDegree := by
      simpa only [MvPolynomial.smul_eq_C_mul] using
        MvPolynomial.totalDegree_smul_le (P.coeff d) (d.prod fun i e => frameChart10 w i^e)
    _ ≤ d.sum (fun _ e => e) := hp
    _ ≤ P.totalDegree := MvPolynomial.le_totalDegree hd

theorem frame_hermite10 [CharZero K] (w : Fin 9 → Fin 10 → K)
    (P : MvPolynomial (Fin 10) K) (S : Fin 9 → Finset K) (s t r : ℕ)
    (hcard : ∀ j, (S j).card = s) (hdeg : P.totalDegree ≤ r)
    (hsize : r < s*(t+1))
    (hz : ∀ a : Fin 9 → K, (∀ j, a j ∈ S j) →
      ∀ J : Fin 10 → ℕ, (∑ i, J i) ≤ t →
      gridJet6 P J (fun i => ∑ j, a j*w j i) = 0) :
    ∀ y : Fin 10 → K, y ∈ Submodule.span K (Set.range w) → MvPolynomial.eval y P = 0 := by
  have hQ : frameSubstitution10 w P = 0 := by
    apply hermite_multivariate6 _ S s t r hcard ((frameSubstitution10_degree w P).trans hdeg) hsize
    intro a ha J hJ
    exact frame_hasse_zero_transport10 w P a t J hJ (hz a ha)
  intro y hy
  obtain ⟨a,ha⟩ := frame_coordinates_exist10 w y hy
  have hey : y = fun i => ∑ j, a j*w j i := funext ha
  rw [hey,← frameSubstitution10_eval,hQ,map_zero]


end CompoundFeasibility.SourceHermite10

end
end M197Literal

namespace M197Literal
open scoped BigOperators Classical Matrix
noncomputable section
namespace CompoundFeasibility.SourceMultiblock10
open SourceHermite SourceHermite10
variable {K : Type*} [Field K]
abbrev Vars10 (m : ℕ) := Fin m × Fin 10

noncomputable def derivative10 {m : ℕ} {R : Type*} [CommRing R]
    (J : Vars10 m →₀ ℕ) (P : MvPolynomial (Vars10 m) R) :
    MvPolynomial (Vars10 m) R :=
  P.sum fun b c => MvPolynomial.monomial (b - J)
    (c * ∏ u : Vars10 m, (Nat.choose (b u) (J u) : R))

theorem derivative10_coeff {m : ℕ} {R : Type*} [CommRing R]
    (a gamma : Vars10 m →₀ ℕ) (P : MvPolynomial (Vars10 m) R) :
    (derivative10 a P).coeff gamma =
      P.coeff (gamma + a) *
        ∏ k : Vars10 m, (Nat.choose ((gamma + a) k) (a k) : R) := by
  classical
  rw [derivative10, MvPolynomial.sum_def, MvPolynomial.coeff_sum]
  calc
    _ = (MvPolynomial.monomial ((gamma + a) - a)
        (P.coeff (gamma + a) * ∏ k : Vars10 m,
          (Nat.choose ((gamma + a) k) (a k) : R))).coeff gamma := by
      apply Finset.sum_eq_single (gamma + a)
      · intro b hb hne
        by_cases hab : a ≤ b
        · have hd : b - a ≠ gamma := by
            intro hz
            apply hne
            calc
              b = (b - a) + a := (tsub_add_cancel_of_le hab).symm
              _ = gamma + a := by rw [hz]
          simp [MvPolynomial.coeff_monomial, hd]
        · change ¬ ∀ k, a k ≤ b k at hab
          push_neg at hab
          obtain ⟨k, hk⟩ := hab
          have hz : (∏ k : Vars10 m, (Nat.choose (b k) (a k) : R)) = 0 := by
            apply Finset.prod_eq_zero (Finset.mem_univ k)
            simp [Nat.choose_eq_zero_of_lt hk]
          simp [hz]
      · intro hn
        have hz : P.coeff (gamma + a) = 0 := MvPolynomial.not_mem_support_iff.mp hn
        simp [hz]
    _ = _ := by simp



variable {R : Type*} [CommRing R]

theorem derivative10_monomial {m : ℕ} (J b : Vars10 m →₀ ℕ) (c : R) :
    derivative10 J (MvPolynomial.monomial b c) = MvPolynomial.monomial (b-J)
      (c * ∏ u : Vars10 m, (Nat.choose (b u) (J u) : R)) := by
  unfold derivative10
  exact MvPolynomial.sum_monomial_eq (by simp)


variable {𝕂 : Type*} [Field 𝕂] [CharZero 𝕂]

theorem derivative10_add {m : ℕ} (J : Vars10 m →₀ ℕ)
    (P Q : MvPolynomial (Vars10 m) 𝕂) :
    derivative10 J (P + Q) = derivative10 J P + derivative10 J Q := by
  apply MvPolynomial.ext
  intro d
  simp only [derivative10_coeff, MvPolynomial.coeff_add, add_mul]

theorem source10_pderiv_eq_single {m : ℕ} (u : Vars10 m)
    (P : MvPolynomial (Vars10 m) 𝕂) :
    MvPolynomial.pderiv u P = derivative10 (Finsupp.single u 1) P := by
  classical
  induction P using MvPolynomial.induction_on' with
  | add P Q hP hQ => simp only [map_add, derivative10_add, hP, hQ]
  | monomial b c =>
    rw [MvPolynomial.pderiv_monomial, derivative10_monomial]
    congr 1
    congr 1
    rw [Finset.prod_eq_single u]
    · simp [Finsupp.single_apply, add_comm]
    · intro x hx hxu
      simp [Finsupp.single_apply, Ne.symm hxu]
    · simp

theorem source10_derivative_comp {m : ℕ} (a b : Vars10 m →₀ ℕ)
    (P : MvPolynomial (Vars10 m) 𝕂) :
    derivative10 a (derivative10 b P) =
      (∏ k : Vars10 m, (Nat.choose ((a + b) k) (a k) : 𝕂)) •
        derivative10 (a + b) P := by
  classical
  apply MvPolynomial.ext
  intro c
  simp only [derivative10_coeff, MvPolynomial.coeff_smul, smul_eq_mul]
  have hch (k : Vars10 m) :
      Nat.choose ((c + a + b) k) (b k) * Nat.choose ((c + a) k) (a k) =
        Nat.choose ((a + b) k) (a k) * Nat.choose ((c + a + b) k) ((a + b) k) := by
    simp only [Finsupp.add_apply]
    have h := Nat.choose_mul (n := c k + a k + b k) (k := a k + b k) (s := b k)
      (by omega) (by omega)
    have hn : c k + a k + b k - b k = c k + a k := by omega
    have ha : a k + b k - b k = a k := by omega
    rw [hn, ha] at h
    have hs : Nat.choose (a k + b k) (b k) = Nat.choose (a k + b k) (a k) :=
      Nat.choose_symm_add.symm
    rw [hs] at h
    nlinarith
  calc
    _ = P.coeff (c + (a + b)) *
        ∏ k : Vars10 m, ((Nat.choose ((c + a + b) k) (b k) : 𝕂) *
          (Nat.choose ((c + a) k) (a k) : 𝕂)) := by
      rw [Finset.prod_mul_distrib]
      simp only [add_assoc]
      ring
    _ = P.coeff (c + (a + b)) *
        ∏ k : Vars10 m, ((Nat.choose ((a + b) k) (a k) : 𝕂) *
          (Nat.choose ((c + a + b) k) ((a + b) k) : 𝕂)) := by
      congr 1
      apply Finset.prod_congr rfl
      intro k _
      exact_mod_cast hch k
    _ = _ := by rw [Finset.prod_mul_distrib]; simp only [add_assoc]; ring

theorem source10_pderiv_hasse {m : ℕ} (u : Vars10 m)
    (J : Vars10 m →₀ ℕ) (P : MvPolynomial (Vars10 m) 𝕂) :
    MvPolynomial.pderiv u (derivative10 J P) =
      (J u + 1) • derivative10 (J + Finsupp.single u 1) P := by
  rw [source10_pderiv_eq_single, source10_derivative_comp, add_comm J]
  have he : (∏ v : Vars10 m, (Nat.choose (((Finsupp.single u 1 : Vars10 m →₀ ℕ) + J) v)
      ((Finsupp.single u 1) v) : 𝕂)) = ((J u + 1 : ℕ) : 𝕂) := by
    rw [Finset.prod_eq_single u]
    · simp [Finsupp.single_apply, add_comm]
    · intro x hx hxu
      simp [Finsupp.single_apply, Ne.symm hxu]
    · simp
  rw [he]
  simp [nsmul_eq_mul, MvPolynomial.smul_eq_C_mul]


def blockPartialEval10 {m : ℕ} (h : Fin m) (x : Vars10 m → K) :
    MvPolynomial (Vars10 m) K →+* MvPolynomial (Fin 10) K :=
  MvPolynomial.eval₂Hom MvPolynomial.C
    (fun u => if u.1 = h then MvPolynomial.X u.2 else MvPolynomial.C (x u))

def blockExponent10 {m : ℕ} (h : Fin m) (d : Vars10 m →₀ ℕ) : Fin 10 →₀ ℕ :=
  Finsupp.equivFunOnFinite.symm (fun i => d (h,i))

theorem blockPartialEval10_eval {m : ℕ} (h : Fin m) (x : Vars10 m → K)
    (P : MvPolynomial (Vars10 m) K) (y : Fin 10 → K) :
    MvPolynomial.eval y (blockPartialEval10 h x P) =
      MvPolynomial.eval (fun u => if u.1 = h then y u.2 else x u) P := by
  have he : (MvPolynomial.eval y).comp (blockPartialEval10 h x) =
      MvPolynomial.eval (fun u => if u.1 = h then y u.2 else x u) := by
    apply MvPolynomial.ringHom_ext
    · intro c; simp [blockPartialEval10]
    · intro u
      by_cases hu : u.1 = h <;> simp [blockPartialEval10,hu]
  exact congrArg (fun f : MvPolynomial (Vars10 m) K →+* K => f P) he

theorem blockPartialEval10_monomial {m : ℕ} (h : Fin m) (x : Vars10 m → K)
    (d : Vars10 m →₀ ℕ) (c : K) :
    blockPartialEval10 h x (MvPolynomial.monomial d c) =
      MvPolynomial.monomial (blockExponent10 h d)
        (c*∏ g ∈ Finset.univ.erase h, ∏ i : Fin 10, x (g,i)^d (g,i)) := by
  classical
  rw [blockPartialEval10,MvPolynomial.eval₂Hom_monomial]
  simp only [Finsupp.prod_fintype _ _ (fun _ => pow_zero _),Fintype.prod_prod_type]
  rw [← Finset.mul_prod_erase _ _ (Finset.mem_univ h)]
  simp only [Prod.fst,Prod.snd,if_pos rfl]
  have hout : (∏ g ∈ Finset.univ.erase h, ∏ i : Fin 10,
      (if g = h then MvPolynomial.X i else MvPolynomial.C (x (g,i)))^d (g,i)) =
      MvPolynomial.C (∏ g ∈ Finset.univ.erase h, ∏ i : Fin 10, x (g,i)^d (g,i)) := by
    simp only [map_prod,map_pow]
    apply Finset.prod_congr rfl
    intro g hg
    have hgh := (Finset.mem_erase.mp hg).1
    simp only [if_neg hgh]
  rw [hout]
  have hrow (i : Fin 10) : blockExponent10 h d i = d (h,i) := rfl
  simp only [MvPolynomial.monomial_eq,Finsupp.prod_fintype _ _ (fun _ => pow_zero _),hrow,
    MvPolynomial.C_mul]
  simp only [ite_true]
  ring


def blockJetIndex10 {m : ℕ} (h : Fin m) (A : Fin 10 → ℕ) : Vars10 m →₀ ℕ :=
  Finsupp.equivFunOnFinite.symm (fun u => if u.1 = h then A u.2 else 0)

theorem blockJetIndex10_apply {m : ℕ} (h : Fin m) (A : Fin 10 → ℕ) (u : Vars10 m) :
    blockJetIndex10 h A u = if u.1 = h then A u.2 else 0 := rfl

theorem blockJetIndex10_zero {m : ℕ} (h : Fin m) : blockJetIndex10 h (fun _ => 0) = 0 := by
  ext u
  simp [blockJetIndex10_apply]

theorem blockJetIndex10_update {m : ℕ} (h : Fin m) (A : Fin 10 → ℕ) (i : Fin 10) :
    blockJetIndex10 h (Function.update A i (A i+1)) =
      blockJetIndex10 h A+Finsupp.single (h,i) 1 := by
  classical
  ext ⟨g,k⟩
  by_cases hg : g = h
  · subst g
    by_cases hk : k = i
    · subst k; simp [blockJetIndex10_apply,Finsupp.add_apply,Finsupp.single_apply,Prod.mk.injEq]
    · simp [blockJetIndex10_apply,Finsupp.add_apply,Finsupp.single_apply,Function.update,
        Prod.mk.injEq,hk,Ne.symm hk]
  · simp [blockJetIndex10_apply,Finsupp.add_apply,Finsupp.single_apply,Prod.mk.injEq,hg,Ne.symm hg]

theorem derivative10_zero_index {m : ℕ} (P : MvPolynomial (Vars10 m) K) :
    derivative10 0 P = P := by
  ext d
  simp [derivative10_coeff]

theorem derivative10_commute [CharZero K] {m : ℕ} (A B : Vars10 m →₀ ℕ)
    (P : MvPolynomial (Vars10 m) K) :
    derivative10 A (derivative10 B P) = derivative10 B (derivative10 A P) := by
  rw [source10_derivative_comp,source10_derivative_comp,add_comm B A]
  congr 1
  apply Finset.prod_congr rfl
  intro u _
  simp only [Finsupp.add_apply]
  exact congrArg (fun n : ℕ => (n : K)) Nat.choose_symm_add

theorem blockPartialEval10_pderiv {m : ℕ} (h : Fin m) (x : Vars10 m → K)
    (P : MvPolynomial (Vars10 m) K) (i : Fin 10) :
    MvPolynomial.pderiv i (blockPartialEval10 h x P) =
      blockPartialEval10 h x (MvPolynomial.pderiv (h,i) P) := by
  classical
  induction P using MvPolynomial.induction_on with
  | C c => simp [blockPartialEval10,MvPolynomial.pderiv_C]
  | add P Q hP hQ => simp only [map_add,hP,hQ]
  | mul_X P u hP =>
    have hX : blockPartialEval10 h x (MvPolynomial.X u) =
        if u.1 = h then MvPolynomial.X u.2 else MvPolynomial.C (x u) := by
      simp [blockPartialEval10]
    have hD : MvPolynomial.pderiv i
        (if u.1 = h then MvPolynomial.X u.2 else MvPolynomial.C (x u)) =
        blockPartialEval10 h x (MvPolynomial.pderiv (h,i) (MvPolynomial.X u)) := by
      rcases u with ⟨g,k⟩
      by_cases hg : g = h
      · subst g
        simp [MvPolynomial.pderiv_X,Pi.single_apply,Prod.mk.injEq,eq_comm]
      · simp [hg,MvPolynomial.pderiv_C,MvPolynomial.pderiv_X,Pi.single_apply,
          Prod.mk.injEq,Ne.symm hg]
    simp only [map_mul,MvPolynomial.pderiv_mul,map_add,hP,hX,hD]


theorem blockPartialEval10_jet [CharZero K] {m : ℕ} (h : Fin m) (x : Vars10 m → K)
    (P : MvPolynomial (Vars10 m) K) (A : Fin 10 → ℕ) (y : Fin 10 → K) :
    gridJet6 (blockPartialEval10 h x P) A y =
      MvPolynomial.eval (fun u => if u.1 = h then y u.2 else x u)
        (derivative10 (blockJetIndex10 h A) P) := by
  classical
  have hall : ∀ T : ℕ, ∀ (P : MvPolynomial (Vars10 m) K) (A : Fin 10 → ℕ),
      (∑ i, A i) ≤ T →
      gridJet6 (blockPartialEval10 h x P) A y =
        MvPolynomial.eval (fun u => if u.1 = h then y u.2 else x u)
          (derivative10 (blockJetIndex10 h A) P) := by
    intro T
    induction T with
    | zero =>
      intro P A hA
      have hAz : A = 0 := by
        funext i
        have hi := Finset.single_le_sum (fun j _ => Nat.zero_le (A j)) (Finset.mem_univ i)
        have hz : A i = 0 := by omega
        exact hz
      rw [hAz]
      change gridJet6 (blockPartialEval10 h x P) (fun _ => 0) y =
        MvPolynomial.eval _ (derivative10 (blockJetIndex10 h (fun _ => 0)) P)
      rw [gridJet6_zero,blockJetIndex10_zero,derivative10_zero_index,blockPartialEval10_eval]
    | succ T ih =>
      intro P A hA
      by_cases hAz : A = 0
      · rw [hAz]
        change gridJet6 (blockPartialEval10 h x P) (fun _ => 0) y =
          MvPolynomial.eval _ (derivative10 (blockJetIndex10 h (fun _ => 0)) P)
        rw [gridJet6_zero,blockJetIndex10_zero,derivative10_zero_index,blockPartialEval10_eval]
      · obtain ⟨i,hin⟩ := Function.ne_iff.mp hAz
        have hi : 0 < A i := Nat.pos_of_ne_zero hin
        let B := Function.update A i (A i-1)
        have hB : (∑ j, B j) ≤ T := by
          have he := jet_order_update6 A i (A i-1)
          dsimp [B]
          omega
        have hBA : Function.update B i (B i+1) = A := by
          funext j
          by_cases hj : j = i
          · subst j; simp [B,show A i-1+1 = A i by omega]
          · simp [B,hj]
        have hidx : blockJetIndex10 h B+Finsupp.single (h,i) 1 = blockJetIndex10 h A := by
          rw [← blockJetIndex10_update,hBA]
        have hright : MvPolynomial.eval (fun u => if u.1 = h then y u.2 else x u)
            (derivative10 (blockJetIndex10 h B) (MvPolynomial.pderiv (h,i) P)) =
            ((B i+1 : ℕ) : K)*MvPolynomial.eval (fun u => if u.1 = h then y u.2 else x u)
              (derivative10 (blockJetIndex10 h A) P) := by
          rw [source10_pderiv_eq_single,derivative10_commute,← source10_pderiv_eq_single,
            source10_pderiv_hasse,hidx]
          simp only [blockJetIndex10_apply,Prod.fst,Prod.snd,if_pos rfl,ite_true,map_nsmul,nsmul_eq_mul,map_mul,map_natCast]
        apply mul_left_cancel₀ (show ((B i+1 : ℕ) : K) ≠ 0 by exact_mod_cast Nat.succ_ne_zero (B i))
        calc
          _ = gridJet6 (MvPolynomial.pderiv i (blockPartialEval10 h x P)) B y := by
            rw [gridJet6_pderiv,hBA]
          _ = gridJet6 (blockPartialEval10 h x (MvPolynomial.pderiv (h,i) P)) B y := by
            rw [blockPartialEval10_pderiv]
          _ = _ := (ih _ B hB).trans hright
  exact hall (∑ i, A i) P A le_rfl



theorem blockPartialEval10_degree {m : ℕ} (h : Fin m) (x : Vars10 m → K)
    (P : MvPolynomial (Vars10 m) K) (r : ℕ)
    (hdeg : ∀ d ∈ P.support, (∑ i : Fin 10, d (h,i)) ≤ r) :
    (blockPartialEval10 h x P).totalDegree ≤ r := by
  classical
  conv_lhs => rw [P.as_sum]
  rw [map_sum]
  apply MvPolynomial.totalDegree_finsetSum_le
  intro d hd
  rw [blockPartialEval10_monomial]
  apply (MvPolynomial.totalDegree_monomial_le _ _).trans
  have he : (blockExponent10 h d).sum (fun _ e => e) = ∑ i : Fin 10, d (h,i) := by
    rw [Finsupp.sum_fintype _ _ (fun _ => rfl)]
    rfl
  exact he.le.trans (hdeg d hd)


def blockFramePoint10 {m : ℕ} (w : Fin m → Fin 9 → Fin 10 → K)
    (a : Fin m → Fin 9 → K) (u : Vars10 m) : K := ∑ j, a u.1 j*w u.1 j u.2

theorem derivative10_block_degree_bound {m : ℕ} (r : Fin m → ℕ)
    (P : MvPolynomial (Vars10 m) K)
    (hP : ∀ d ∈ P.support, ∀ h, (∑ i : Fin 10, d (h,i)) ≤ r h) (J : Vars10 m →₀ ℕ) :
    ∀ d ∈ (derivative10 J P).support, ∀ h, (∑ i : Fin 10, d (h,i)) ≤ r h := by
  intro d hd h
  have hn := MvPolynomial.mem_support_iff.mp hd
  rw [derivative10_coeff] at hn
  have he := hP (d+J) (MvPolynomial.mem_support_iff.mpr (mul_ne_zero_iff.mp hn).1) h
  simp only [Finsupp.add_apply,Finset.sum_add_distrib] at he
  omega

theorem multiblock_frame_hermite10 [CharZero K] {m : ℕ} (P : MvPolynomial (Vars10 m) K)
    (w : Fin m → Fin 9 → Fin 10 → K) (S : Fin m → Fin 9 → Finset K)
    (s t r : Fin m → ℕ) (hcard : ∀ h j, (S h j).card = s h)
    (hdeg : ∀ d ∈ P.support, ∀ h, (∑ i : Fin 10, d (h,i)) ≤ r h)
    (hsize : ∀ h, r h < s h*(t h+1))
    (hgrid : ∀ a : Fin m → Fin 9 → K, (∀ h j, a h j ∈ S h j) →
      ∀ J : Vars10 m →₀ ℕ, (∀ h, (∑ i : Fin 10, J (h,i)) ≤ t h) →
      MvPolynomial.eval (blockFramePoint10 w a) (derivative10 J P) = 0) :
    ∀ a : Fin m → Fin 9 → K, MvPolynomial.eval (blockFramePoint10 w a) P = 0 := by
  classical
  let Stage (F : Finset (Fin m)) : Prop :=
    ∀ a : Fin m → Fin 9 → K, (∀ g, g ∉ F → ∀ j, a g j ∈ S g j) →
    ∀ J : Vars10 m →₀ ℕ, (∀ g, g ∈ F → ∀ i, J (g,i) = 0) →
      (∀ g, g ∉ F → (∑ i : Fin 10, J (g,i)) ≤ t g) →
      MvPolynomial.eval (blockFramePoint10 w a) (derivative10 J P) = 0
  have hstage : ∀ F, Stage F := by
    intro F
    induction F using Finset.induction_on with
    | empty =>
      intro a ha J hzero horder
      exact hgrid a (fun g j => ha g (Finset.not_mem_empty g) j) J
        (fun g => horder g (Finset.not_mem_empty g))
    | @insert h F hh ih =>
      intro a ha J hzero horder
      let x := blockFramePoint10 w a
      have hD : (blockPartialEval10 h x (derivative10 J P)).totalDegree ≤ r h :=
        blockPartialEval10_degree h x _ _ (fun d hd => derivative10_block_degree_bound r P hdeg J d hd h)
      have hH := frame_hermite10 (w h) (blockPartialEval10 h x (derivative10 J P))
        (S h) (s h) (t h) (r h) (hcard h) hD (hsize h)
      have hzeros : ∀ b : Fin 9 → K, (∀ j, b j ∈ S h j) →
          ∀ B : Fin 10 → ℕ, (∑ i, B i) ≤ t h →
          gridJet6 (blockPartialEval10 h x (derivative10 J P)) B
            (fun i => ∑ j, b j*w h j i) = 0 := by
        intro b hb B hB
        let a' := Function.update a h b
        let C := blockJetIndex10 h B+J
        have ha' : ∀ g, g ∉ F → ∀ j, a' g j ∈ S g j := by
          intro g hg j
          by_cases hgh : g = h
          · subst g; simpa [a'] using hb j
          · simpa [a',hgh] using ha g (by simp [hgh,hg]) j
        have hCzero : ∀ g, g ∈ F → ∀ i, C (g,i) = 0 := by
          intro g hg i
          have hgh : g ≠ h := by intro he; subst g; exact hh hg
          simp [C,Finsupp.add_apply,blockJetIndex10_apply,hgh,hzero g (Finset.mem_insert_of_mem hg) i]
        have hCorder : ∀ g, g ∉ F → (∑ i : Fin 10, C (g,i)) ≤ t g := by
          intro g hg
          by_cases hgh : g = h
          · subst g
            simpa [C,Finsupp.add_apply,blockJetIndex10_apply,hzero h (Finset.mem_insert_self h F)] using hB
          · simpa [C,Finsupp.add_apply,blockJetIndex10_apply,hgh] using
              horder g (by simp [hgh,hg])
        have hz := ih a' ha' C hCzero hCorder
        rw [blockPartialEval10_jet,source10_derivative_comp,MvPolynomial.smul_eval]
        have he : (fun u : Vars10 m => if u.1 = h then ∑ j, b j*w h j u.2 else x u) =
            blockFramePoint10 w a' := by
          funext u
          rcases u with ⟨g,i⟩
          by_cases hg : g = h <;> simp [blockFramePoint10,a',x,hg]
        rw [he]
        change _ * MvPolynomial.eval (blockFramePoint10 w a') (derivative10 C P) = 0
        rw [hz,mul_zero]
      have hspan : (fun i => ∑ j, a h j*w h j i) ∈ Submodule.span K (Set.range (w h)) := by
        have he : (fun i => ∑ j, a h j*w h j i) = ∑ j, a h j • w h j := by
          ext i
          simp [Finset.sum_apply,Pi.smul_apply,smul_eq_mul]
        rw [he]
        apply Submodule.sum_mem
        intro j _
        exact Submodule.smul_mem _ _ (Submodule.subset_span (Set.mem_range_self j))
      have hz := hH hzeros (fun i => ∑ j, a h j*w h j i) hspan
      rw [blockPartialEval10_eval] at hz
      have he : (fun u : Vars10 m => if u.1 = h then ∑ j, a h j*w h j u.2 else x u) = x := by
        funext u
        rcases u with ⟨g,i⟩
        by_cases hg : g = h <;> simp [x,blockFramePoint10,hg]
      rw [he] at hz
      exact hz
  intro a
  have hz := hstage Finset.univ a (by intro g hg; simp at hg) 0
    (by intros; rfl) (by intro g hg; simp at hg)
  simpa only [derivative10_zero_index] using hz


theorem multiblock_span_hermite10 [CharZero K] {m : ℕ} (P : MvPolynomial (Vars10 m) K)
    (w : Fin m → Fin 9 → Fin 10 → K) (S : Fin m → Fin 9 → Finset K)
    (s t r : Fin m → ℕ) (hcard : ∀ h j, (S h j).card = s h)
    (hdeg : ∀ d ∈ P.support, ∀ h, (∑ i : Fin 10, d (h,i)) ≤ r h)
    (hsize : ∀ h, r h < s h*(t h+1))
    (hgrid : ∀ a : Fin m → Fin 9 → K, (∀ h j, a h j ∈ S h j) →
      ∀ J : Vars10 m →₀ ℕ, (∀ h, (∑ i : Fin 10, J (h,i)) ≤ t h) →
      MvPolynomial.eval (blockFramePoint10 w a) (derivative10 J P) = 0) :
    ∀ y : Fin m → Fin 10 → K, (∀ h, y h ∈ Submodule.span K (Set.range (w h))) →
      MvPolynomial.eval (fun u => y u.1 u.2) P = 0 := by
  intro y hy
  have hex (h : Fin m) := frame_coordinates_exist10 (w h) (y h) (hy h)
  choose a ha using hex
  have he : (fun u : Vars10 m => y u.1 u.2) = blockFramePoint10 w a := by
    funext u
    exact ha u.1 u.2
  rw [he]
  exact multiblock_frame_hermite10 P w S s t r hcard hdeg hsize hgrid a


end CompoundFeasibility.SourceMultiblock10

end
end M197Literal

namespace M197Literal
open scoped BigOperators Matrix Classical
noncomputable section
namespace CompoundFeasibility.Auxiliary10

abbrev Block10 (r : ℕ) := {b : Fin 10 → Fin (r + 1) // ∑ i, (b i).val = r}

def blockNatEquiv10 (r : ℕ) : Block10 r ≃ {b : Fin 10 → ℕ // ∑ i, b i = r} where
  toFun b := ⟨fun i => (b.val i).val,b.property⟩
  invFun b := ⟨fun i => ⟨b.val i,by
    have hh : b.val i ≤ ∑ j, b.val j := Finset.single_le_sum (fun j _ => Nat.zero_le _) (Finset.mem_univ i)
    rw [b.property] at hh
    omega⟩,b.property⟩
  left_inv b := by rfl
  right_inv b := by rfl

theorem block10_card (r : ℕ) : Fintype.card (Block10 r) = (r + 9).choose 9 := by
  letI : Fintype {b : Fin 10 → ℕ // ∑ i, b i = r} := Fintype.ofEquiv (Sym (Fin 10) r) (Sym.equivNatSumOfFintype (Fin 10) r)
  rw [Fintype.card_congr (blockNatEquiv10 r),← Fintype.card_congr (Sym.equivNatSumOfFintype (Fin 10) r),Sym.card_sym_eq_choose]
  simpa only [Fintype.card_fin,show 10 + r - 1 = r + 9 by omega] using (Nat.choose_symm_add (a := r) (b := 9))

noncomputable def rothAuxBlockPerm (r : ℕ) (e : Equiv.Perm (Fin 10)) :
    Block10 r ≃ Block10 r where
  toFun b := ⟨fun i => b.val (e i), by
    change (∑ i, (b.val (e i)).val) = r
    exact (Equiv.sum_comp e (fun i => (b.val i).val)).trans b.property⟩
  invFun b := ⟨fun i => b.val (e.symm i), by
    change (∑ i, (b.val (e.symm i)).val) = r
    exact (Equiv.sum_comp e.symm (fun i => (b.val i).val)).trans b.property⟩
  left_inv b := by ext i; simp
  right_inv b := by ext i; simp

theorem rothAuxBlock_coordinate_sum (r : ℕ) (i : Fin 10) :
    (∑ b : Block10 r, ((b.val i).val : ℝ)) =
      ∑ b : Block10 r, ((b.val 0).val : ℝ) := by
  have h := (rothAuxBlockPerm r (Equiv.swap 0 i)).sum_comp
    (fun b : Block10 r => ((b.val 0).val : ℝ))
  simpa [rothAuxBlockPerm] using h

theorem rothAuxBlock_center_sum (r : ℕ) (hr : 0 < r) :
    (∑ b : Block10 r, (1 / 10 - ((b.val 0).val : ℝ) / r)) = 0 := by
  have htotal : (∑ b : Block10 r, ∑ i : Fin 10, ((b.val i).val : ℝ)) =
      (Fintype.card (Block10 r) : ℝ) * r := by
    calc
      _ = ∑ _b : Block10 r, (r : ℝ) := by
        apply Finset.sum_congr rfl
        intro b _
        exact_mod_cast b.property
      _ = _ := by simp
  rw [Finset.sum_comm] at htotal
  simp_rw [rothAuxBlock_coordinate_sum] at htotal
  simp only [Finset.sum_const, Finset.card_univ, Fintype.card_fin, nsmul_eq_mul] at htotal
  have hsum :
      (∑ b : Block10 r, ((b.val 0).val : ℝ)) =
        (Fintype.card (Block10 r) : ℝ) * r / 10 := by
    calc
      _ = (10 * (∑ b : Block10 r,
          ((b.val 0).val : ℝ))) / 10 := by ring
      _ = _ := by
        simpa only [Nat.cast_ofNat] using
          congrArg (fun x : ℝ => x / 10) htotal
  have hr' : (r : ℝ) ≠ 0 := by positivity
  rw [Finset.sum_sub_distrib]
  simp only [Finset.sum_const, Finset.card_univ, nsmul_eq_mul]
  rw [← Finset.sum_div, hsum]
  field_simp [hr']
  ring

theorem rothAuxBlock_center_bound (r : ℕ) (hr : 0 < r) (b : Block10 r) :
    |1 / 10 - ((b.val 0).val : ℝ) / r| ≤ 1 := by
  have hle : ((b.val 0).val : ℝ) ≤ r := by exact_mod_cast Nat.le_of_lt_succ (b.val 0).isLt
  have hz : 0 ≤ ((b.val 0).val : ℝ) / r := by positivity
  have hu : ((b.val 0).val : ℝ) / r ≤ 1 := (div_le_one (by positivity)).mpr hle
  rw [abs_le]
  constructor <;> linarith

theorem rothAuxBlock_exp_sum (r : ℕ) (hr : 0 < r) (u : ℝ) (hu : 0 ≤ u) (hu' : u ≤ 1) :
    (∑ b : Block10 r, Real.exp (u * (1 / 10 - ((b.val 0).val : ℝ) / r))) ≤
      (Fintype.card (Block10 r) : ℝ) * Real.exp (u ^ 2) := by
  have hpoint (b : Block10 r) :
      Real.exp (u * (1 / 10 - ((b.val 0).val : ℝ) / r)) ≤
        1 + u * (1 / 10 - ((b.val 0).val : ℝ) / r) + u ^ 2 := by
    have hb := rothAuxBlock_center_bound r hr b
    have hmul : |u * (1 / 10 - ((b.val 0).val : ℝ) / r)| ≤ 1 := by
      rw [abs_mul, abs_of_nonneg hu]
      exact (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hb hu).trans (by simpa using hu')
    have he := (abs_le.mp (Real.abs_exp_sub_one_sub_id_le hmul)).2
    have hsq : (u * (1 / 10 - ((b.val 0).val : ℝ) / r)) ^ 2 ≤ u ^ 2 := by
      rw [mul_pow]
      have hs : (1 / 10 - ((b.val 0).val : ℝ) / r) ^ 2 ≤ 1 := by
        have hs := mul_self_le_mul_self (abs_nonneg (1 / 10 - ((b.val 0).val : ℝ) / r)) hb
        simpa only [← sq, sq_abs, one_pow] using hs
      exact (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hs (sq_nonneg u)).trans_eq (mul_one _)
    linarith
  calc
    _ ≤ ∑ b : Block10 r, (1 + u * (1 / 10 - ((b.val 0).val : ℝ) / r) + u ^ 2) :=
      Finset.sum_le_sum (fun b _ => hpoint b)
    _ = (Fintype.card (Block10 r) : ℝ) * (1 + u ^ 2) := by
      simp only [Finset.sum_add_distrib, Finset.sum_const, Finset.card_univ, nsmul_eq_mul,
        ← Finset.mul_sum, rothAuxBlock_center_sum r hr, mul_zero, add_zero]
      ring
    _ ≤ _ := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (by simpa only [add_comm] using Real.add_one_le_exp (u ^ 2)) (by positivity)



open scoped Classical

abbrev RothAuxCoefficients {m : ℕ} (r : Fin m → ℕ) := (h : Fin m) → Block10 (r h)

abbrev RothAuxPivots {m : ℕ} (r : Fin m → ℕ) := (h : Fin m) → Fin (r h + 1)

noncomputable def rothAuxDimension {m : ℕ} (r : Fin m → ℕ) : ℕ :=
  Fintype.card (RothAuxCoefficients r)

theorem rothAuxDimension_eq {m : ℕ} (r : Fin m → ℕ) :
    rothAuxDimension r = ∏ h, (r h + 9).choose 9 := by
  simp [rothAuxDimension, Fintype.card_pi, block10_card]

noncomputable def rothAuxCentered {m : ℕ} (r : Fin m → ℕ) (b : RothAuxCoefficients r) : ℝ :=
  ∑ h, (1 / 10 - ((b h).val 0).val / (r h : ℝ))

abbrev RothAuxBad {m : ℕ} (r : Fin m → ℕ) (eta : ℝ) :=
  {b : RothAuxCoefficients r // eta * m < rothAuxCentered r b}

noncomputable def rothAuxPivotCondition {m : ℕ} (r : Fin m → ℕ) (eta : ℝ)
    (i : RothAuxPivots r) : Prop :=
  (∑ h, ((i h).val : ℝ) / r h) < (1 / 10 - eta) * m

theorem rothAux_exp_moment {m : ℕ} (r : Fin m → ℕ) (hr : ∀ h, 0 < r h)
    (u : ℝ) (hu : 0 ≤ u) (hu' : u ≤ 1) :
    (∑ b : RothAuxCoefficients r, Real.exp (u * rothAuxCentered r b)) ≤
      (rothAuxDimension r : ℝ) * Real.exp ((m : ℝ) * u ^ 2) := by
  have hfactor : (∑ b : RothAuxCoefficients r, Real.exp (u * rothAuxCentered r b)) =
      ∏ h, ∑ b : Block10 (r h),
        Real.exp (u * (1 / 10 - ((b.val 0).val : ℝ) / r h)) := by
    simp only [rothAuxCentered, Finset.mul_sum, Real.exp_sum]
    exact (Fintype.prod_sum (fun (h : Fin m) (b : Block10 (r h)) =>
      Real.exp (u * (1 / 10 - ((b.val 0).val : ℝ) / r h)))).symm
  rw [hfactor]
  calc
    _ ≤ ∏ h, ((Fintype.card (Block10 (r h)) : ℝ) * Real.exp (u ^ 2)) := by
      apply Finset.prod_le_prod
      · intro h _
        exact Finset.sum_nonneg (fun _ _ => (Real.exp_pos _).le)
      · intro h _
        exact rothAuxBlock_exp_sum (r h) (hr h) u hu hu'
    _ = _ := by
      simp [Finset.prod_mul_distrib, rothAuxDimension, Fintype.card_pi,
        Nat.cast_prod, Real.exp_nat_mul]


theorem rothAux_exp_tail_parameter {m : ℕ} (r : Fin m → ℕ)
    (hr : ∀ h, 0 < r h) (eta u : ℝ) (hu : 0 ≤ u) (hu' : u ≤ 1) :
    (Fintype.card (RothAuxBad r eta) : ℝ) ≤
      (rothAuxDimension r : ℝ) * Real.exp ((m : ℝ) * u ^ 2 - u * (eta * m)) := by
  have hlower : (Fintype.card (RothAuxBad r eta) : ℝ) * Real.exp (u * (eta * m)) ≤
      ∑ b : RothAuxBad r eta, Real.exp (u * rothAuxCentered r b.val) := by
    calc
      _ = ∑ _b : RothAuxBad r eta, Real.exp (u * (eta * m)) := by simp
      _ ≤ _ := Finset.sum_le_sum (fun b _ =>
        Real.exp_le_exp.mpr (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left b.property.le hu))
  have hsub : (∑ b : RothAuxBad r eta, Real.exp (u * rothAuxCentered r b.val)) ≤
      ∑ b : RothAuxCoefficients r, Real.exp (u * rothAuxCentered r b) := by
    have hp := Fintype.sum_subtype_add_sum_subtype
      (fun b : RothAuxCoefficients r => eta * m < rothAuxCentered r b)
      (fun b => Real.exp (u * rothAuxCentered r b))
    have hn : 0 ≤ ∑ b : {b : RothAuxCoefficients r //
        ¬ eta * m < rothAuxCentered r b}, Real.exp (u * rothAuxCentered r b.val) :=
      Finset.sum_nonneg (fun _ _ => (Real.exp_pos _).le)
    linarith
  have hb := hlower.trans (hsub.trans (rothAux_exp_moment r hr u hu hu'))
  have heq : Real.exp (u * (eta * m)) * Real.exp (-(u * (eta * m))) = 1 := by
    rw [← Real.exp_add]
    simp
  calc
    _ = ((Fintype.card (RothAuxBad r eta) : ℝ) * Real.exp (u * (eta * m))) *
        Real.exp (-(u * (eta * m))) := by rw [mul_assoc, heq, mul_one]
    _ ≤ ((rothAuxDimension r : ℝ) * Real.exp ((m : ℝ) * u ^ 2)) *
        Real.exp (-(u * (eta * m))) :=
      mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hb (Real.exp_pos _).le
    _ = _ := by rw [mul_assoc, ← Real.exp_add]; rfl

theorem rothAuxBad_card_zero {m : ℕ} (r : Fin m → ℕ) (eta : ℝ) (heta : 1 / 10 ≤ eta) :
    Fintype.card (RothAuxBad r eta) = 0 := by
  have hcenter (b : RothAuxCoefficients r) : rothAuxCentered r b ≤ (m : ℝ) / 10 := by
    calc
      _ ≤ ∑ _h : Fin m, (1 / 10 : ℝ) := by
        apply Finset.sum_le_sum
        intro h _
        have hz : 0 ≤ (((b h).val 0).val : ℝ) / (r h : ℝ) := by positivity
        linarith
      _ = _ := by simp [div_eq_mul_inv]
  have hem : (m : ℝ) / 10 ≤ eta * m := by
    have hm : (0 : ℝ) ≤ m := Nat.cast_nonneg m
    nlinarith
  haveI : IsEmpty (RothAuxBad r eta) := ⟨fun b =>
    (not_lt_of_ge ((hcenter b.val).trans hem)) b.property⟩
  exact Fintype.card_eq_zero

theorem rothAuxBad_exp_bound {m : ℕ} (r : Fin m → ℕ)
    (hr : ∀ h, 0 < r h) (eta : ℝ) (heta : 0 < eta) :
    (Fintype.card (RothAuxBad r eta) : ℝ) ≤
      (rothAuxDimension r : ℝ) * Real.exp (-eta ^ 2 * m / 4) := by
  by_cases he : eta < 1 / 10
  · have hu : 0 ≤ eta / 2 := by positivity
    have hu' : eta / 2 ≤ 1 := by linarith
    have hb := rothAux_exp_tail_parameter r hr eta (eta / 2) hu hu'
    convert hb using 1 <;> congr 2 <;> ring
  · rw [rothAuxBad_card_zero r eta (le_of_not_gt he)]
    simp only [Nat.cast_zero]
    exact mul_nonneg (Nat.cast_nonneg _) (Real.exp_pos _).le




abbrev Equations10 {m : ℕ} (r : Fin m → ℕ) (eta : ℝ) := Fin 30 × RothAuxBad r eta

theorem dimension10_pos {m : ℕ} (r : Fin m → ℕ) : 0 < rothAuxDimension r := by
  rw [rothAuxDimension_eq]
  exact Finset.prod_pos (fun h _ => Nat.choose_pos (by omega))

theorem coordinate_rows10_le_half {m : ℕ} (r : Fin m → ℕ) (hr : ∀ h, 0 < r h)
    (eta : ℝ) (heta : 0 < eta) (d : ℕ) (hd : 0 < d)
    (hm : 4 * (eta^2)⁻¹ * Real.log (60 * (d : ℝ)) ≤ (m : ℝ)) :
    (d * Fintype.card (Equations10 r eta) : ℝ) ≤ (rothAuxDimension r : ℝ) / 2 := by
  have hs : 0 < eta^2 := sq_pos_of_pos heta
  have hdp : (0 : ℝ) < d := by exact_mod_cast hd
  have hmul := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hm hs.le
  have hid : eta^2 * (4 * (eta^2)⁻¹ * Real.log (60 * (d : ℝ))) = 4 * Real.log (60 * (d : ℝ)) := by field_simp
  rw [hid] at hmul
  have he : Real.exp (-eta^2 * m / 4) ≤ 1 / (60 * (d : ℝ)) := by
    calc
      _ ≤ Real.exp (-Real.log (60 * (d : ℝ))) := Real.exp_le_exp.mpr (by nlinarith)
      _ = _ := by rw [Real.exp_neg,Real.exp_log (by positivity),one_div]
  have hb := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (rothAuxBad_exp_bound r hr eta heta) (show (0 : ℝ) ≤ 30 * d by positivity)
  calc
    _ = (30 * (d : ℝ)) * (Fintype.card (RothAuxBad r eta) : ℝ) := by simp only [Equations10,Fintype.card_prod,Fintype.card_fin,Nat.cast_mul,Nat.cast_ofNat]; ring
    _ ≤ (30 * (d : ℝ)) * ((rothAuxDimension r : ℝ) * Real.exp (-eta^2 * m / 4)) := hb
    _ ≤ (30 * (d : ℝ)) * ((rothAuxDimension r : ℝ) * (1 / (60 * (d : ℝ)))) :=
      mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left he (Nat.cast_nonneg _)) (by positivity)
    _ = _ := by field_simp; ring

theorem coordinate_rows10_lt {m : ℕ} (r : Fin m → ℕ) (hr : ∀ h, 0 < r h)
    (eta : ℝ) (heta : 0 < eta) (d : ℕ) (hd : 0 < d)
    (hm : 4 * (eta^2)⁻¹ * Real.log (60 * (d : ℝ)) ≤ (m : ℝ)) :
    d * Fintype.card (Equations10 r eta) < rothAuxDimension r := by
  have hh := coordinate_rows10_le_half r hr eta heta d hd hm
  have hp : (0 : ℝ) < rothAuxDimension r := by exact_mod_cast dimension10_pos r
  exact_mod_cast (show (d * Fintype.card (Equations10 r eta) : ℝ) < (rothAuxDimension r : ℝ) by linarith)

abbrev Block9 (r : ℕ) := {b : Fin 9 → Fin (r + 1) // ∑ i, (b i).val = r}

def blockNatEquiv9 (r : ℕ) : Block9 r ≃ {b : Fin 9 → ℕ // ∑ i, b i = r} where
  toFun b := ⟨fun i => (b.val i).val,b.property⟩
  invFun b := ⟨fun i => ⟨b.val i,by
    have hh : b.val i ≤ ∑ j, b.val j := Finset.single_le_sum (fun j _ => Nat.zero_le _) (Finset.mem_univ i)
    rw [b.property] at hh
    omega⟩,b.property⟩
  left_inv b := by rfl
  right_inv b := by rfl

theorem block9_card (r : ℕ) : Fintype.card (Block9 r) = (r + 8).choose 8 := by
  letI : Fintype {b : Fin 9 → ℕ // ∑ i, b i = r} := Fintype.ofEquiv (Sym (Fin 9) r) (Sym.equivNatSumOfFintype (Fin 9) r)
  rw [Fintype.card_congr (blockNatEquiv9 r),← Fintype.card_congr (Sym.equivNatSumOfFintype (Fin 9) r),Sym.card_sym_eq_choose]
  simpa only [Fintype.card_fin,show 9 + r - 1 = r + 8 by omega] using (Nat.choose_symm_add (a := r) (b := 8))

def blockPivotEquiv10 (r : ℕ) : Block10 r ≃ (i : Fin (r+1)) × Block9 (r-i.val) where
  toFun b := ⟨b.val 0,⟨fun j => ⟨(b.val j.succ).val,by
    have hs := b.property
    rw [Fin.sum_univ_succ] at hs
    have hj : (b.val j.succ).val ≤ ∑ k : Fin 9, (b.val k.succ).val :=
      Finset.single_le_sum (s := Finset.univ) (f := fun k : Fin 9 => (b.val k.succ).val) (fun k _ => Nat.zero_le _) (Finset.mem_univ j)
    omega⟩,by
      have hs := b.property
      rw [Fin.sum_univ_succ] at hs
      change (∑ j : Fin 9, (b.val j.succ).val) = r - (b.val 0).val
      omega⟩⟩
  invFun p := ⟨Fin.cons p.1 (fun j => ⟨(p.2.val j).val,by
    have hh := (p.2.val j).isLt
    omega⟩),by
      rw [Fin.sum_univ_succ]
      change p.1.val + (∑ j : Fin 9, (p.2.val j).val) = r
      rw [p.2.property]
      omega⟩
  left_inv b := by
    apply Subtype.ext
    funext j
    refine Fin.cases ?_ (fun k => ?_) j <;> rfl
  right_inv p := by
    rcases p with ⟨i,b⟩
    rfl

theorem bad_iff_pivot6 {m : ℕ} (r : Fin m → ℕ) (eta : ℝ)
    (b : RothAuxCoefficients r) :
    (eta * m < rothAuxCentered r b) ↔
      rothAuxPivotCondition r eta (fun h => (b h).val 0) := by
  unfold rothAuxCentered rothAuxPivotCondition
  rw [Finset.sum_sub_distrib]
  simp only [Finset.sum_const,Finset.card_univ,Fintype.card_fin,nsmul_eq_mul]
  constructor <;> intro hb <;> nlinarith

def badPivotEquiv4 {m : ℕ} (r : Fin m → ℕ) (eta : ℝ) :
    RothAuxBad r eta ≃
      (i : {i : RothAuxPivots r // rothAuxPivotCondition r eta i}) ×
        ((h : Fin m) → Block9 (r h - (i.val h).val)) where
  toFun b := ⟨⟨fun h => (b.val h).val 0,(bad_iff_pivot6 r eta b.val).mp b.property⟩,
    fun h => (blockPivotEquiv10 (r h) (b.val h)).2⟩
  invFun p := ⟨fun h => (blockPivotEquiv10 (r h)).symm ⟨p.1.val h,p.2 h⟩,by
    apply (bad_iff_pivot6 r eta _).mpr
    exact p.1.property⟩
  left_inv b := by
    apply Subtype.ext
    funext h
    exact (blockPivotEquiv10 (r h)).symm_apply_apply (b.val h)
  right_inv p := by
    rcases p with ⟨⟨i,hi⟩,j⟩
    rfl

theorem bad_count_source10 {m : ℕ} (r : Fin m → ℕ) (eta : ℝ) :
    Fintype.card (RothAuxBad r eta) =
      ∑ i : {i : RothAuxPivots r // rothAuxPivotCondition r eta i},
        ∏ h, (r h - (i.val h).val + 8).choose 8 := by
  rw [Fintype.card_congr (badPivotEquiv4 r eta),Fintype.card_sigma]
  simp only [Fintype.card_pi,block9_card]

theorem constraint_count_source10 {m : ℕ} (r : Fin m → ℕ) (eta : ℝ) :
    Fintype.card (Equations10 r eta) = 30 *
      ∑ i : {i : RothAuxPivots r // rothAuxPivotCondition r eta i},
        ∏ h, (r h - (i.val h).val + 8).choose 8 := by
  simp only [Equations10,Fintype.card_prod,Fintype.card_fin,bad_count_source10]





end CompoundFeasibility.Auxiliary10

end
end M197Literal

open scoped BigOperators Matrix
namespace CompoundFeasibility
noncomputable section
abbrev lattice10Span (b : Basis (Fin 10) ℝ (Fin 10 → ℝ)) : Submodule ℤ (Fin 10 → ℝ) :=
  Submodule.span ℤ (Set.range b)
def coordinateGauge10 (e : (Fin 10 → ℝ) ≃ₗ[ℝ] (Fin 10 → ℝ)) (x : Fin 10 → ℝ) : ℝ := ‖e x‖
theorem lattice10Span_gauge_ball_finite (b : Basis (Fin 10) ℝ (Fin 10 → ℝ))
    (e : (Fin 10 → ℝ) ≃ₗ[ℝ] (Fin 10 → ℝ)) (r : ℝ) :
    ({x : (Fin 10 → ℝ) | coordinateGauge10 e x ≤ r} ∩ (lattice10Span b : Set (Fin 10 → ℝ))).Finite := by
  have hf := ZSpan.setFinite_inter (b.map e) (Metric.isBounded_closedBall (x := 0) (r := r))
  apply (hf.preimage e.injective.injOn).subset
  intro x hx
  refine ⟨?_, ?_⟩
  · simpa [coordinateGauge10, Metric.mem_closedBall, dist_zero_right] using hx.1
  · have hm := Submodule.mem_map_of_mem (f := (e.restrictScalars ℤ).toLinearMap) hx.2
    simpa only [lattice10Span, Submodule.map_span, Set.range_comp,
      LinearEquiv.coe_coe, LinearEquiv.restrictScalars_apply, Basis.coe_map] using hm

theorem exists_shortest_lattice10Span_vector_outside (b : Basis (Fin 10) ℝ (Fin 10 → ℝ))
    (e : (Fin 10 → ℝ) ≃ₗ[ℝ] (Fin 10 → ℝ)) (W : Submodule ℝ (Fin 10 → ℝ)) (hW : W ≠ ⊤) :
    ∃ x ∈ lattice10Span b, x ∉ W ∧ 0 < coordinateGauge10 e x ∧
      ∀ y ∈ lattice10Span b, y ∉ W → coordinateGauge10 e x ≤ coordinateGauge10 e y := by
  classical
  have hex : ∃ i, b i ∉ W := by
    by_contra! h
    apply hW
    apply top_unique
    rw [← b.span_eq]
    exact Submodule.span_le.mpr (by rintro _ ⟨i, rfl⟩; exact h i)
  obtain ⟨i, hi⟩ := hex
  let s : Set (Fin 10 → ℝ) :=
    {x | coordinateGauge10 e x ≤ coordinateGauge10 e (b i)} ∩
      (lattice10Span b : Set (Fin 10 → ℝ)) ∩ {x | x ∉ W}
  have hs : s.Finite := (lattice10Span_gauge_ball_finite b e _).inter_of_left _
  have hbi : b i ∈ lattice10Span b := Submodule.subset_span (Set.mem_range_self i)
  obtain ⟨x, hx, hmin⟩ := Set.exists_min_image s (coordinateGauge10 e) hs
    ⟨b i, ⟨⟨show coordinateGauge10 e (b i) ≤ coordinateGauge10 e (b i) from le_rfl, hbi⟩, hi⟩⟩
  refine ⟨x, hx.1.2, hx.2, ?_, ?_⟩
  · apply norm_pos_iff.mpr
    intro h0
    have : x = 0 := e.injective (h0.trans e.map_zero.symm)
    exact hx.2 (this ▸ W.zero_mem)
  · intro y hy hyW
    by_cases hle : coordinateGauge10 e y ≤ coordinateGauge10 e (b i)
    · exact hmin y ⟨⟨hle, hy⟩, hyW⟩
    · exact hx.1.1.trans (le_of_not_ge hle)

noncomputable def successiveRadius10 (b : Basis (Fin 10) ℝ (Fin 10 → ℝ))
    (e : (Fin 10 → ℝ) ≃ₗ[ℝ] (Fin 10 → ℝ)) (k : Fin 10) : ℝ :=
  sInf {r : ℝ | ∃ u : Fin (k.val + 1) → (Fin 10 → ℝ),
    LinearIndependent ℝ u ∧ (∀ i, u i ∈ lattice10Span b) ∧
      ∀ i, coordinateGauge10 e (u i) ≤ r}

theorem independent_family_escapes_subspace10 {n : ℕ} (u : Fin n → (Fin 10 → ℝ))
    (hu : LinearIndependent ℝ u) (W : Submodule ℝ (Fin 10 → ℝ))
    (hW : Module.finrank ℝ W < n) : ∃ i, u i ∉ W := by
  by_contra! h
  have hs : Submodule.span ℝ (Set.range u) ≤ W :=
    Submodule.span_le.mpr (by rintro _ ⟨i, rfl⟩; exact h i)
  have hd := Submodule.finrank_mono hs
  rw [finrank_span_eq_card hu, Fintype.card_fin] at hd
  omega



def minimaPrefix10 {n : ℕ} (v : Fin n → Fin 10 → ℝ) (i : Fin n) : Submodule ℝ (Fin 10 → ℝ) :=
  Submodule.span ℝ (Set.range (fun j : Fin i.val => v (Fin.castLE (by omega) j)))

theorem minimaPrefix10_snoc_castSucc {n : ℕ} (v : Fin n → Fin 10 → ℝ)
    (x : Fin 10 → ℝ) (i : Fin n) :
    minimaPrefix10 (Fin.snoc v x) i.castSucc = minimaPrefix10 v i := by
  unfold minimaPrefix10
  apply congrArg (fun u : Fin i.val → Fin 10 → ℝ => Submodule.span ℝ (Set.range u))
  funext j
  have he : (Fin.castLE (by omega) j : Fin (n+1)) =
      (Fin.castLE (Nat.le_of_lt i.isLt) j).castSucc := by apply Fin.ext; rfl
  rw [he]
  simp only [Fin.snoc_castSucc]

theorem minimaPrefix10_snoc_last {n : ℕ} (v : Fin n → Fin 10 → ℝ) (x : Fin 10 → ℝ) :
    minimaPrefix10 (Fin.snoc v x) (Fin.last n) = Submodule.span ℝ (Set.range v) := by
  unfold minimaPrefix10
  apply congrArg (fun u : Fin n → Fin 10 → ℝ => Submodule.span ℝ (Set.range u))
  funext j
  have he : (Fin.castLE (by omega) j : Fin (n+1)) = j.castSucc := by apply Fin.ext; rfl
  rw [he]
  simp only [Fin.snoc_castSucc]

theorem exists_greedy_prefix10 (b : Basis (Fin 10) ℝ (Fin 10 → ℝ))
    (e : (Fin 10 → ℝ) ≃ₗ[ℝ] (Fin 10 → ℝ)) :
    ∀ n ≤ 10, ∃ v : Fin n → Fin 10 → ℝ,
      LinearIndependent ℝ v ∧ (∀ i, v i ∈ lattice10Span b) ∧
      ∀ i, ∀ y ∈ lattice10Span b, y ∉ minimaPrefix10 v i →
        coordinateGauge10 e (v i) ≤ coordinateGauge10 e y := by
  intro n
  induction n with
  | zero =>
    intro _
    exact ⟨Fin.elim0,linearIndependent_empty_type,(fun i => Fin.elim0 i),fun i => Fin.elim0 i⟩
  | succ n ih =>
    intro hn
    obtain ⟨v,hv,hvL,hmin⟩ := ih (by omega)
    let W := Submodule.span ℝ (Set.range v)
    have hW : W ≠ ⊤ := by
      intro h
      have hd := finrank_span_eq_card hv
      simp only [Fintype.card_fin] at hd
      change Module.finrank ℝ W = n at hd
      rw [h,finrank_top,Module.finrank_pi,Fintype.card_fin] at hd
      omega
    obtain ⟨x,hxL,hxW,hxpos,hxmin⟩ := exists_shortest_lattice10Span_vector_outside b e W hW
    refine ⟨Fin.snoc v x,linearIndependent_fin_snoc.mpr ⟨hv,hxW⟩,?_,?_⟩
    · intro i
      refine Fin.lastCases ?_ (fun j => ?_) i
      · simpa only [Fin.snoc_last] using hxL
      · simpa only [Fin.snoc_castSucc] using hvL j
    · intro i
      refine Fin.lastCases ?_ (fun j => ?_) i
      · rw [minimaPrefix10_snoc_last]
        simpa only [Fin.snoc_last] using hxmin
      · rw [minimaPrefix10_snoc_castSucc]
        simpa only [Fin.snoc_castSucc] using hmin j

theorem minimaPrefix10_not_mem {n : ℕ} (v : Fin n → Fin 10 → ℝ)
    (hv : LinearIndependent ℝ v) (i j : Fin n) (hij : i ≤ j) : v j ∉ minimaPrefix10 v i := by
  have hle : minimaPrefix10 v i ≤ Submodule.span ℝ (v '' {t | t < i}) := by
    apply Submodule.span_le.mpr
    rintro _ ⟨a,rfl⟩
    exact Submodule.subset_span ⟨Fin.castLE (by omega) a,by change a.val < i.val; exact a.isLt,rfl⟩
  exact fun h => hv.not_mem_span_image (s := {t | t < i}) (x := j) (not_lt_of_ge hij) (hle h)

theorem minimaPrefix10_finrank {n : ℕ} (v : Fin n → Fin 10 → ℝ)
    (hv : LinearIndependent ℝ v) (i : Fin n) : Module.finrank ℝ (minimaPrefix10 v i) = i.val := by
  exact (finrank_span_eq_card (hv.comp (Fin.castLE (by omega) : Fin i.val → Fin n)
    (Fin.castLE_injective _))).trans (Fintype.card_fin i.val)

theorem exists_successive_minima10 (b : Basis (Fin 10) ℝ (Fin 10 → ℝ))
    (e : (Fin 10 → ℝ) ≃ₗ[ℝ] (Fin 10 → ℝ)) :
    ∃ v : Fin 10 → Fin 10 → ℝ, LinearIndependent ℝ v ∧
      (∀ i, v i ∈ lattice10Span b) ∧ (∀ i, 0 < successiveRadius10 b e i) ∧
      Monotone (successiveRadius10 b e) ∧
      (∀ i, coordinateGauge10 e (v i) = successiveRadius10 b e i) ∧
      ∀ i, ∀ y ∈ lattice10Span b, y ∉ minimaPrefix10 v i → coordinateGauge10 e (v i) ≤ coordinateGauge10 e y := by
  obtain ⟨v,hv,hvL,hmin⟩ := exists_greedy_prefix10 b e 10 le_rfl
  let f := coordinateGauge10 e
  have hmono : Monotone (fun i => f (v i)) := by
    intro i j hij
    exact hmin i (v j) (hvL j) (minimaPrefix10_not_mem v hv i j hij)
  have heq (k : Fin 10) : successiveRadius10 b e k = f (v k) := by
    apply IsLeast.csInf_eq
    constructor
    · let emb : Fin (k.val+1) → Fin 10 := Fin.castLE (by omega)
      refine ⟨fun i => v (emb i),hv.comp emb (Fin.castLE_injective _),fun i => hvL (emb i),?_⟩
      intro i
      exact hmono (show emb i ≤ k by change i.val ≤ k.val; omega)
    · rintro r ⟨a,ha,haL,har⟩
      obtain ⟨i,hi⟩ := independent_family_escapes_subspace10 a ha (minimaPrefix10 v k)
        (by rw [minimaPrefix10_finrank v hv k]; omega)
      exact (hmin k (a i) (haL i) hi).trans (har i)
  refine ⟨v,hv,hvL,?_,?_,?_,hmin⟩
  · intro i
    rw [heq]
    exact norm_pos_iff.mpr (fun h => hv.ne_zero i (e.injective (h.trans e.map_zero.symm)))
  · intro i j hij
    rw [heq,heq]
    exact hmono hij
  · intro i
    exact (heq i).symm

end
end CompoundFeasibility

end M197Literal

namespace M197Literal
open scoped BigOperators Matrix
open MeasureTheory
namespace CompoundFeasibility
noncomputable section

def minimaFlag10 (v : Fin 10 → Fin 10 → ℝ) (k : Fin 10) : Submodule ℝ (Fin 10 → ℝ) := minimaPrefix10 v k

theorem minimaFlag10_finrank (v : Fin 10 → Fin 10 → ℝ) (hv : LinearIndependent ℝ v) (k : Fin 10) :
    Module.finrank ℝ (minimaFlag10 v k) = k.val := minimaPrefix10_finrank v hv k

theorem minimaFlag10_mono (v : Fin 10 → (Fin 10 → ℝ)) (k l : Fin 10) (hkl : k ≤ l) :
    minimaFlag10 v k ≤ minimaFlag10 v l := by
  apply Submodule.span_mono
  rintro _ ⟨i, rfl⟩
  refine ⟨Fin.castLE hkl i, ?_⟩
  rfl

def latticeFlag10 (b : Basis (Fin 10) ℝ (Fin 10 → ℝ)) (v : Fin 10 → (Fin 10 → ℝ))
    (k : Fin 10) : Submodule ℤ (lattice10Span b) :=
  ((minimaFlag10 v k).restrictScalars ℤ).comap (lattice10Span b).subtype

def flagLattice10 (b : Basis (Fin 10) ℝ (Fin 10 → ℝ)) (v : Fin 10 → (Fin 10 → ℝ))
    (k : Fin 10) : Submodule ℤ (minimaFlag10 v k) :=
  (lattice10Span b).comap ((minimaFlag10 v k).subtype.restrictScalars ℤ)

theorem latticeFlag10_saturated (b : Basis (Fin 10) ℝ (Fin 10 → ℝ))
    (v : Fin 10 → (Fin 10 → ℝ)) (k : Fin 10) (n : ℤ) (hn : n ≠ 0)
    (x : lattice10Span b) (hx : n • x ∈ latticeFlag10 b v k) :
    x ∈ latticeFlag10 b v k := by
  change n • (x : (Fin 10 → ℝ)) ∈ minimaFlag10 v k at hx
  rw [← Int.cast_smul_eq_zsmul ℝ] at hx
  change (x : (Fin 10 → ℝ)) ∈ minimaFlag10 v k
  exact (Submodule.smul_mem_iff (minimaFlag10 v k) (by exact_mod_cast hn : (n : ℝ) ≠ 0)).mp hx

noncomputable def latticeFlag10_equiv_flagLattice10 (b : Basis (Fin 10) ℝ (Fin 10 → ℝ))
    (v : Fin 10 → (Fin 10 → ℝ)) (k : Fin 10) :
    latticeFlag10 b v k ≃ₗ[ℤ] flagLattice10 b v k where
  toFun x := ⟨⟨x.val.val, x.property⟩, x.val.property⟩
  invFun x := ⟨⟨x.val.val, x.property⟩, x.val.property⟩
  left_inv _ := rfl
  right_inv _ := rfl
  map_add' _ _ := rfl
  map_smul' _ _ := rfl

instance flagLattice10_discrete (b : Basis (Fin 10) ℝ (Fin 10 → ℝ))
    (v : Fin 10 → (Fin 10 → ℝ)) (k : Fin 10) : DiscreteTopology (flagLattice10 b v k) := by
  let f : flagLattice10 b v k → lattice10Span b := fun x => ⟨x.val.val, x.property⟩
  apply DiscreteTopology.of_continuous_injective (f := f)
  · exact (continuous_subtype_val.comp continuous_subtype_val).subtype_mk _
  · intro x y h
    apply Subtype.ext
    apply Subtype.ext
    exact congrArg (fun z : lattice10Span b => (z : (Fin 10 → ℝ))) h

instance flagLattice10_isZLattice (b : Basis (Fin 10) ℝ (Fin 10 → ℝ))
    (v : Fin 10 → (Fin 10 → ℝ)) (hv : LinearIndependent ℝ v)
    (hvL : ∀ i, v i ∈ lattice10Span b) (k : Fin 10) :
    IsZLattice ℝ (flagLattice10 b v k) where
  span_top := by
    let u : Fin k.val → (Fin 10 → ℝ) := fun i => v (Fin.castLE (by omega) i)
    let B : Basis (Fin k.val) ℝ (minimaFlag10 v k) :=
      Basis.span (hv.comp (Fin.castLE (by omega)) (Fin.castLE_injective _))
    apply top_unique
    rw [← B.span_eq]
    apply Submodule.span_mono
    rintro _ ⟨i, rfl⟩
    change (B i : (Fin 10 → ℝ)) ∈ lattice10Span b
    have hB : (B i : (Fin 10 → ℝ)) = u i :=
      Basis.span_apply (hv.comp (Fin.castLE (by omega)) (Fin.castLE_injective _)) i
    rw [hB]
    exact hvL (Fin.castLE (by omega) i)

theorem latticeFlag10_finrank (b : Basis (Fin 10) ℝ (Fin 10 → ℝ))
    (v : Fin 10 → (Fin 10 → ℝ)) (hv : LinearIndependent ℝ v)
    (hvL : ∀ i, v i ∈ lattice10Span b) (k : Fin 10) :
    Module.finrank ℤ (latticeFlag10 b v k) = k.val := by
  letI := flagLattice10_isZLattice b v hv hvL k
  rw [(latticeFlag10_equiv_flagLattice10 b v k).finrank_eq,
    ZLattice.rank ℝ (flagLattice10 b v k), minimaFlag10_finrank v hv k]


theorem latticeFlag10_basis_real_span {ι : Type*}
    (b : Basis (Fin 10) ℝ (Fin 10 → ℝ)) (v : Fin 10 → (Fin 10 → ℝ))
    (hvL : ∀ i, v i ∈ lattice10Span b) (k : Fin 10)
    (a : @Basis ι ℤ (latticeFlag10 b v k) _ _ (latticeFlag10 b v k).module) :
    Submodule.span ℝ (Set.range (fun i =>
      (((a i : latticeFlag10 b v k) : lattice10Span b) : (Fin 10 → ℝ)))) = minimaFlag10 v k := by
  let N := latticeFlag10 b v k
  letI nModule : Module ℤ N := N.module
  let W := Submodule.span ℝ (Set.range (fun i =>
    (((a i : latticeFlag10 b v k) : lattice10Span b) : (Fin 10 → ℝ))))
  have hw : ∀ x : N, ((x : lattice10Span b) : (Fin 10 → ℝ)) ∈ W := by
    have h : Submodule.span ℤ (Set.range a) ≤
        ((W.restrictScalars ℤ).comap (lattice10Span b).subtype).comap N.subtype := by
      apply Submodule.span_le.mpr
      rintro _ ⟨i, rfl⟩
      exact Submodule.subset_span (Set.mem_range_self i)
    rw [a.span_eq] at h
    intro x
    exact h (Submodule.mem_top : x ∈ (⊤ : Submodule ℤ N))
  apply le_antisymm
  · apply Submodule.span_le.mpr
    rintro _ ⟨i, rfl⟩
    exact (a i).property
  · apply Submodule.span_le.mpr
    rintro _ ⟨i, rfl⟩
    let x : N := ⟨⟨v (Fin.castLE (by omega) i), hvL _⟩,
      Submodule.subset_span (Set.mem_range_self i)⟩
    exact hw x


theorem extend_lattice_flag10
    (b : Basis (Fin 10) ℝ (Fin 10 → ℝ)) (v : Fin 10 → Fin 10 → ℝ)
    (hv : LinearIndependent ℝ v) (hvL : ∀ i, v i ∈ lattice10Span b)
    (k : Fin 9) (a : Basis (Fin k.val) ℤ (latticeFlag10 b v k.castSucc)) :
    ∃ c : Basis (Fin (k.val+1)) ℤ (latticeFlag10 b v k.succ),
      ∀ i : Fin k.val,
        ((c i.castSucc : latticeFlag10 b v k.succ) : lattice10Span b) =
          ((a i : latticeFlag10 b v k.castSucc) : lattice10Span b) := by
  let L := lattice10Span b
  letI lModule : Module ℤ L := (lattice10Span b).module
  letI lFinite : Module.Finite ℤ L := Module.Finite.of_basis (b.restrictScalars ℤ)
  let Nk : Submodule ℤ L := latticeFlag10 b v k.castSucc
  let Nn : Submodule ℤ L := latticeFlag10 b v k.succ
  letI nnModule : Module ℤ Nn := Nn.module
  letI nnFinite : Module.Finite ℤ Nn := Module.Finite.of_injective Nn.subtype Nn.subtype_injective
  letI nnNoZero : NoZeroSMulDivisors ℤ Nn := Submodule.noZeroSMulDivisors Nn
  have hkn : Nk ≤ Nn := fun _ hx => minimaFlag10_mono v _ _ (by change k.val ≤ k.val+1; omega) hx
  let S : Submodule ℤ Nn := Nk.comap Nn.subtype
  letI sModule : Module ℤ S := S.module
  let e : S ≃ₗ[ℤ] Nk := Submodule.comapSubtypeEquivOfLe hkn
  let aS : @Basis (Fin k.val) ℤ S _ _ sModule := a.map e.symm
  obtain ⟨c,hc⟩ := JSP000334.saturated_integer_basis_extend S aS
    (latticeFlag10_finrank b v hv hvL k.succ)
    (fun z hz x hx => latticeFlag10_saturated b v k.castSucc z hz (x : L) hx)
  refine ⟨c,?_⟩
  intro i
  simpa only [aS,Basis.map_apply,e,Submodule.comapSubtypeEquivOfLe_symm_apply] using
    congrArg (fun x : Nn => (x : L)) (hc i)

theorem exists_adapted_integer_basis10
    (b : Basis (Fin 10) ℝ (Fin 10 → ℝ)) (v : Fin 10 → Fin 10 → ℝ)
    (hv : LinearIndependent ℝ v) (hvL : ∀ i, v i ∈ lattice10Span b) :
    ∃ c : Basis (Fin 10) ℤ (lattice10Span b), ∀ k : Fin 10,
      Submodule.span ℝ (Set.range (fun i : Fin k.val =>
        ((c (Fin.castLE (by omega) i) : lattice10Span b) : Fin 10 → ℝ))) = minimaFlag10 v k := by
  classical
  let L := lattice10Span b
  letI lModule : Module ℤ L := (lattice10Span b).module
  letI lFinite : Module.Finite ℤ L := Module.Finite.of_basis (b.restrictScalars ℤ)
  letI lFree : Module.Free ℤ L := Module.Free.of_basis (b.restrictScalars ℤ)
  letI lNoZero : NoZeroSMulDivisors ℤ L := Submodule.noZeroSMulDivisors L
  let N1 : Submodule ℤ L := latticeFlag10 b v 1
  let N2 : Submodule ℤ L := latticeFlag10 b v 2
  let N3 : Submodule ℤ L := latticeFlag10 b v 3
  let N4 : Submodule ℤ L := latticeFlag10 b v 4
  let N5 : Submodule ℤ L := latticeFlag10 b v 5
  let N6 : Submodule ℤ L := latticeFlag10 b v 6
  let N7 : Submodule ℤ L := latticeFlag10 b v 7
  let N8 : Submodule ℤ L := latticeFlag10 b v 8
  let N9 : Submodule ℤ L := latticeFlag10 b v 9
  letI n1Module : Module ℤ N1 := N1.module
  letI n1Finite : Module.Finite ℤ N1 := Module.Finite.of_injective N1.subtype N1.subtype_injective
  letI n1NoZero : NoZeroSMulDivisors ℤ N1 := Submodule.noZeroSMulDivisors N1
  letI n1Free : Module.Free ℤ N1 := Module.free_of_finite_type_torsion_free'
  have hr1 : Module.finrank ℤ N1 = 1 := latticeFlag10_finrank b v hv hvL 1
  have hrL : Module.finrank ℤ L = 10 := by
    simpa using Module.finrank_eq_card_basis (b.restrictScalars ℤ)
  let a1 : @Basis (Fin 1) ℤ N1 _ _ n1Module := Module.finBasisOfFinrankEq ℤ N1 hr1
  obtain ⟨a2,he1⟩ := extend_lattice_flag10 b v hv hvL 1 a1
  obtain ⟨a3,he2⟩ := extend_lattice_flag10 b v hv hvL 2 a2
  obtain ⟨a4,he3⟩ := extend_lattice_flag10 b v hv hvL 3 a3
  obtain ⟨a5,he4⟩ := extend_lattice_flag10 b v hv hvL 4 a4
  obtain ⟨a6,he5⟩ := extend_lattice_flag10 b v hv hvL 5 a5
  obtain ⟨a7,he6⟩ := extend_lattice_flag10 b v hv hvL 6 a6
  obtain ⟨a8,he7⟩ := extend_lattice_flag10 b v hv hvL 7 a7
  obtain ⟨a9,he8⟩ := extend_lattice_flag10 b v hv hvL 8 a8
  obtain ⟨c,hc⟩ := JSP000334.saturated_integer_basis_extend N9 a9 hrL (latticeFlag10_saturated b v 9)
  refine ⟨c,?_⟩
  intro k
  fin_cases k
  · simp [minimaFlag10,minimaPrefix10]
  · change Submodule.span ℝ (Set.range (fun i : Fin 1 =>
      ((c (Fin.castLE (by omega) i) : L) : Fin 10 → ℝ))) = minimaFlag10 v 1
    have heq : (fun i : Fin 1 => ((c (Fin.castLE (by omega) i) : L) : Fin 10 → ℝ)) =
        (fun i : Fin 1 => (((a1 i : N1) : L) : Fin 10 → ℝ)) := by
      funext i
      apply congrArg (fun x : L => (x : Fin 10 → ℝ))
      calc
        (c (Fin.castLE (by omega) i) : L) = (a9 i.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc : L) := hc i.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc
        _ = (a8 i.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc : L) := he8 i.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc
        _ = (a7 i.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc : L) := he7 i.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc
        _ = (a6 i.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc : L) := he6 i.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc
        _ = (a5 i.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc : L) := he5 i.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc
        _ = (a4 i.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc : L) := he4 i.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc
        _ = (a3 i.castSucc.castSucc : L) := he3 i.castSucc.castSucc
        _ = (a2 i.castSucc : L) := he2 i.castSucc
        _ = (a1 i : L) := he1 i
    rw [heq]
    exact latticeFlag10_basis_real_span b v hvL 1 a1
  · change Submodule.span ℝ (Set.range (fun i : Fin 2 =>
      ((c (Fin.castLE (by omega) i) : L) : Fin 10 → ℝ))) = minimaFlag10 v 2
    have heq : (fun i : Fin 2 => ((c (Fin.castLE (by omega) i) : L) : Fin 10 → ℝ)) =
        (fun i : Fin 2 => (((a2 i : N2) : L) : Fin 10 → ℝ)) := by
      funext i
      apply congrArg (fun x : L => (x : Fin 10 → ℝ))
      calc
        (c (Fin.castLE (by omega) i) : L) = (a9 i.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc : L) := hc i.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc
        _ = (a8 i.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc : L) := he8 i.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc
        _ = (a7 i.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc : L) := he7 i.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc
        _ = (a6 i.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc : L) := he6 i.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc
        _ = (a5 i.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc : L) := he5 i.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc
        _ = (a4 i.castSucc.castSucc : L) := he4 i.castSucc.castSucc
        _ = (a3 i.castSucc : L) := he3 i.castSucc
        _ = (a2 i : L) := he2 i
    rw [heq]
    exact latticeFlag10_basis_real_span b v hvL 2 a2
  · change Submodule.span ℝ (Set.range (fun i : Fin 3 =>
      ((c (Fin.castLE (by omega) i) : L) : Fin 10 → ℝ))) = minimaFlag10 v 3
    have heq : (fun i : Fin 3 => ((c (Fin.castLE (by omega) i) : L) : Fin 10 → ℝ)) =
        (fun i : Fin 3 => (((a3 i : N3) : L) : Fin 10 → ℝ)) := by
      funext i
      apply congrArg (fun x : L => (x : Fin 10 → ℝ))
      calc
        (c (Fin.castLE (by omega) i) : L) = (a9 i.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc : L) := hc i.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc
        _ = (a8 i.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc : L) := he8 i.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc
        _ = (a7 i.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc : L) := he7 i.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc
        _ = (a6 i.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc : L) := he6 i.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc
        _ = (a5 i.castSucc.castSucc : L) := he5 i.castSucc.castSucc
        _ = (a4 i.castSucc : L) := he4 i.castSucc
        _ = (a3 i : L) := he3 i
    rw [heq]
    exact latticeFlag10_basis_real_span b v hvL 3 a3
  · change Submodule.span ℝ (Set.range (fun i : Fin 4 =>
      ((c (Fin.castLE (by omega) i) : L) : Fin 10 → ℝ))) = minimaFlag10 v 4
    have heq : (fun i : Fin 4 => ((c (Fin.castLE (by omega) i) : L) : Fin 10 → ℝ)) =
        (fun i : Fin 4 => (((a4 i : N4) : L) : Fin 10 → ℝ)) := by
      funext i
      apply congrArg (fun x : L => (x : Fin 10 → ℝ))
      calc
        (c (Fin.castLE (by omega) i) : L) = (a9 i.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc : L) := hc i.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc
        _ = (a8 i.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc : L) := he8 i.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc
        _ = (a7 i.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc : L) := he7 i.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc
        _ = (a6 i.castSucc.castSucc : L) := he6 i.castSucc.castSucc
        _ = (a5 i.castSucc : L) := he5 i.castSucc
        _ = (a4 i : L) := he4 i
    rw [heq]
    exact latticeFlag10_basis_real_span b v hvL 4 a4
  · change Submodule.span ℝ (Set.range (fun i : Fin 5 =>
      ((c (Fin.castLE (by omega) i) : L) : Fin 10 → ℝ))) = minimaFlag10 v 5
    have heq : (fun i : Fin 5 => ((c (Fin.castLE (by omega) i) : L) : Fin 10 → ℝ)) =
        (fun i : Fin 5 => (((a5 i : N5) : L) : Fin 10 → ℝ)) := by
      funext i
      apply congrArg (fun x : L => (x : Fin 10 → ℝ))
      calc
        (c (Fin.castLE (by omega) i) : L) = (a9 i.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc : L) := hc i.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc
        _ = (a8 i.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc : L) := he8 i.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc
        _ = (a7 i.castSucc.castSucc : L) := he7 i.castSucc.castSucc
        _ = (a6 i.castSucc : L) := he6 i.castSucc
        _ = (a5 i : L) := he5 i
    rw [heq]
    exact latticeFlag10_basis_real_span b v hvL 5 a5
  · change Submodule.span ℝ (Set.range (fun i : Fin 6 =>
      ((c (Fin.castLE (by omega) i) : L) : Fin 10 → ℝ))) = minimaFlag10 v 6
    have heq : (fun i : Fin 6 => ((c (Fin.castLE (by omega) i) : L) : Fin 10 → ℝ)) =
        (fun i : Fin 6 => (((a6 i : N6) : L) : Fin 10 → ℝ)) := by
      funext i
      apply congrArg (fun x : L => (x : Fin 10 → ℝ))
      calc
        (c (Fin.castLE (by omega) i) : L) = (a9 i.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc : L) := hc i.castSucc.castSucc.castSucc
        _ = (a8 i.castSucc.castSucc : L) := he8 i.castSucc.castSucc
        _ = (a7 i.castSucc : L) := he7 i.castSucc
        _ = (a6 i : L) := he6 i
    rw [heq]
    exact latticeFlag10_basis_real_span b v hvL 6 a6
  · change Submodule.span ℝ (Set.range (fun i : Fin 7 =>
      ((c (Fin.castLE (by omega) i) : L) : Fin 10 → ℝ))) = minimaFlag10 v 7
    have heq : (fun i : Fin 7 => ((c (Fin.castLE (by omega) i) : L) : Fin 10 → ℝ)) =
        (fun i : Fin 7 => (((a7 i : N7) : L) : Fin 10 → ℝ)) := by
      funext i
      apply congrArg (fun x : L => (x : Fin 10 → ℝ))
      calc
        (c (Fin.castLE (by omega) i) : L) = (a9 i.castSucc.castSucc : L) := hc i.castSucc.castSucc
        _ = (a8 i.castSucc : L) := he8 i.castSucc
        _ = (a7 i : L) := he7 i
    rw [heq]
    exact latticeFlag10_basis_real_span b v hvL 7 a7
  · change Submodule.span ℝ (Set.range (fun i : Fin 8 =>
      ((c (Fin.castLE (by omega) i) : L) : Fin 10 → ℝ))) = minimaFlag10 v 8
    have heq : (fun i : Fin 8 => ((c (Fin.castLE (by omega) i) : L) : Fin 10 → ℝ)) =
        (fun i : Fin 8 => (((a8 i : N8) : L) : Fin 10 → ℝ)) := by
      funext i
      apply congrArg (fun x : L => (x : Fin 10 → ℝ))
      calc
        (c (Fin.castLE (by omega) i) : L) = (a9 i.castSucc : L) := hc i.castSucc
        _ = (a8 i : L) := he8 i
    rw [heq]
    exact latticeFlag10_basis_real_span b v hvL 8 a8
  · change Submodule.span ℝ (Set.range (fun i : Fin 9 =>
      ((c (Fin.castLE (by omega) i) : L) : Fin 10 → ℝ))) = minimaFlag10 v 9
    have heq : (fun i : Fin 9 => ((c (Fin.castLE (by omega) i) : L) : Fin 10 → ℝ)) =
        (fun i : Fin 9 => (((a9 i : N9) : L) : Fin 10 → ℝ)) := by
      funext i
      apply congrArg (fun x : L => (x : Fin 10 → ℝ))
      exact hc i
    rw [heq]
    exact latticeFlag10_basis_real_span b v hvL 9 a9

end
end CompoundFeasibility
