def dyadicBasis {n : ℕ} (a : Fin n → ℕ) : Basis (Fin n) ℝ (Fin n → ℝ) :=
  (Pi.basisFun ℝ (Fin n)).isUnitSMul (w := fun i => (2 : ℝ)^a i)
    (fun i => isUnit_iff_ne_zero.mpr (pow_ne_zero _ (by norm_num : (2 : ℝ) ≠ 0)))

theorem dyadic_lattice_coordinates (a : Fin 10 → ℕ)
    (x : Fin 10 → ℝ) (hx : x ∈ Submodule.span ℤ (Set.range (dyadicBasis a))) :
    ∃ z : Fin 10 → ℤ, ∀ i, x i = (2 : ℝ) ^ a i * (z i : ℝ) := by
  let b := dyadicBasis a
  let z : Fin 10 → ℤ := fun i => (b.restrictScalars ℤ).repr ⟨x,hx⟩ i
  refine ⟨z,?_⟩
  intro i
  have hr (j) : b.repr x j = (z j : ℝ) :=
    (b.restrictScalars_repr_apply ℤ ⟨x,hx⟩ j).symm
  have h := congrFun (b.sum_repr x) i
  simp only [hr] at h
  simpa [b,dyadicBasis,Basis.isUnitSMul_apply,Finset.sum_apply,Pi.smul_apply,
    smul_eq_mul,Pi.basisFun_apply,Pi.single_apply,mul_comm] using h.symm

theorem dyadic_lattice_avoids (a : Fin 10 → ℕ) (ha : Antitone a)
    (f : (Fin 10 → ℝ) → ℝ)
    (hf : ∀ (r : ℝ) (x : Fin 10 → ℝ), f (r • x) = |r| * f x)
    (mu : Fin 10 → ℝ) (hmu : ∀ i, 1 < (2 : ℝ)^a i * mu i)
    (hlower : ∀ (z : Fin 10 → ℤ) (i : Fin 10), z i ≠ 0 →
      (∀ j, i < j → z j = 0) → mu i ≤ f (fun j => (z j : ℝ)))
    (x : Fin 10 → ℝ)
    (hx : x ∈ Submodule.span ℤ (Set.range (dyadicBasis a)))
    (hxf : f x ≤ 1) : x = 0 := by
  classical
  by_contra hne
  obtain ⟨z,hz⟩ := dyadic_lattice_coordinates a x hx
  let support := Finset.univ.filter (fun i => z i ≠ 0)
  have hs : support.Nonempty := by
    by_contra h
    apply hne
    ext i
    have hzi : z i = 0 := by
      by_contra hzi
      exact h ⟨i,Finset.mem_filter.mpr ⟨Finset.mem_univ i,hzi⟩⟩
    simp [hz i,hzi]
  let k := support.max' hs
  have hk : z k ≠ 0 := (Finset.mem_filter.mp (Finset.max'_mem support hs)).2
  have htail : ∀ j, k < j → z j = 0 := by
    intro j hj
    by_contra h
    have hle : j ≤ k := Finset.le_max' support j (Finset.mem_filter.mpr ⟨Finset.mem_univ j,h⟩)
    exact (not_le_of_gt hj) hle
  let w : Fin 10 → ℤ := fun i => if i ≤ k then (2 : ℤ)^(a i-a k)*z i else 0
  have hwk : w k ≠ 0 := by simpa [w] using hk
  have hw : (fun i => (w i : ℝ)) = ((2 : ℝ)^a k)⁻¹ • x := by
    ext i
    by_cases hi : i ≤ k
    · have hai : a k ≤ a i := ha hi
      simp only [w,if_pos hi,Int.cast_mul,Int.cast_pow,Int.cast_ofNat,Pi.smul_apply,smul_eq_mul]
      rw [hz i,← Nat.sub_add_cancel hai, pow_add]
      field_simp
      ring
    · have hit : k < i := lt_of_not_ge hi
      simp [w,hi,hz i,htail i hit]
  have hlow := hlower w k hwk (fun j hj => by simp [w,not_le_of_gt hj])
  rw [hw,hf,abs_of_pos (inv_pos.mpr (pow_pos (by norm_num : (0 : ℝ) < 2) _))] at hlow
  have hp : (0 : ℝ) < 2^a k := pow_pos (by norm_num) _
  have hle : mu k ≤ ((2 : ℝ)^a k)⁻¹ :=
    hlow.trans (by simpa using mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hxf (inv_nonneg.mpr hp.le))
  have hbound : (2 : ℝ)^a k * mu k ≤ 1 := by
    calc
      _ ≤ (2 : ℝ)^a k * ((2 : ℝ)^a k)⁻¹ := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hle hp.le
      _ = 1 := mul_inv_cancel₀ hp.ne'
  exact (not_le_of_gt (hmu k)) hbound


theorem dyadic_fundamental_volume {n : ℕ} (a : Fin n → ℕ) :
    volume (ZSpan.fundamentalDomain (dyadicBasis a)) =
      ENNReal.ofReal (∏ i, (2 : ℝ)^a i) := by
  have hm : Matrix.of (dyadicBasis a) = Matrix.diagonal (fun i => (2 : ℝ)^a i) := by
    ext i j
    by_cases h : i = j
    · subst j
      simp [dyadicBasis,Basis.isUnitSMul_apply,Pi.basisFun_apply]
    · simp [dyadicBasis,Basis.isUnitSMul_apply,Pi.basisFun_apply,Pi.single_apply,h]
  rw [ZSpan.volume_fundamentalDomain,hm,Matrix.det_diagonal,abs_of_pos]
  exact Finset.prod_pos (fun _ _ => pow_pos (by norm_num) _)

theorem dyadic_volume_bound (a : Fin 10 → ℕ) (ha : Antitone a)
    (f : (Fin 10 → ℝ) → ℝ)
    (hf : ∀ (r : ℝ) (x : Fin 10 → ℝ), f (r • x) = |r| * f x)
    (mu : Fin 10 → ℝ) (hmu : ∀ i, 1 < (2 : ℝ)^a i * mu i)
    (hlower : ∀ (z : Fin 10 → ℤ) (i : Fin 10), z i ≠ 0 →
      (∀ j, i < j → z j = 0) → mu i ≤ f (fun j => (z j : ℝ)))
    (hsymm : ∀ x, f (-x) = f x) (hconv : Convex ℝ {x | f x ≤ 1}) :
    volume.real {x | f x ≤ 1} ≤ (∏ i, (2 : ℝ)^a i) * 2^10 := by
  let b := dyadicBasis a
  letI : Countable (Submodule.span ℤ (Set.range b)) :=
    inferInstance
  letI : Countable (Submodule.span ℤ (Set.range b)).toAddSubgroup :=
    inferInstanceAs (Countable (Submodule.span ℤ (Set.range b)))
  have hvol : volume {x | f x ≤ 1} ≤
      volume (ZSpan.fundamentalDomain b) * 2^Module.finrank ℝ (Fin 10 → ℝ) := by
    by_contra h
    obtain ⟨x,hx,hfx⟩ := exists_ne_zero_mem_lattice_of_measure_mul_two_pow_lt_measure
      (ZSpan.isAddFundamentalDomain' b volume)
      (fun x hx => by simpa only [Set.mem_setOf_eq,hsymm] using hx) hconv (lt_of_not_ge h)
    apply hx
    apply Subtype.ext
    exact dyadic_lattice_avoids a ha f hf mu hmu hlower x x.property hfx
  rw [dyadic_fundamental_volume,Module.finrank_pi,Fintype.card_fin] at hvol
  have hfinite : ENNReal.ofReal (∏ i, (2 : ℝ)^a i) * (2 : ENNReal)^10 ≠ ⊤ := by
    exact ENNReal.mul_ne_top ENNReal.ofReal_ne_top (by simp)
  have h := ENNReal.toReal_mono hfinite hvol
  have hprod : (0 : ℝ) ≤ ∏ i : Fin 10, (2 : ℝ)^a i :=
    Finset.prod_nonneg (fun i _ => pow_nonneg (by norm_num : (0 : ℝ) ≤ 2) (a i))
  simpa only [measureReal_def,ENNReal.toReal_mul,ENNReal.toReal_ofReal hprod,
    ENNReal.toReal_pow,ENNReal.toReal_ofNat] using h


theorem exists_dyadic_exponents (mu : Fin 10 → ℝ)
    (hpos : ∀ i, 0 < mu i) (hone : ∀ i, mu i ≤ 1) (hmono : Monotone mu) :
    ∃ a : Fin 10 → ℕ, Antitone a ∧ ∀ i,
      1 < (2 : ℝ)^a i * mu i ∧ (2 : ℝ)^a i * mu i ≤ 2 := by
  classical
  have hex (i : Fin 10) : ∃ n : ℕ, 1 < (2 : ℝ)^n * mu i := by
    obtain ⟨n,hn⟩ := pow_unbounded_of_one_lt (mu i)⁻¹ (by norm_num : (1 : ℝ) < 2)
    refine ⟨n,?_⟩
    exact (div_lt_iff₀ (hpos i)).mp (by simpa only [one_div] using hn)
  let a := fun i => Nat.find (hex i)
  refine ⟨a,?_,?_⟩
  · intro i j hij
    apply Nat.find_min' (hex j)
    exact (Nat.find_spec (hex i)).trans_le
      (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (hmono hij) (pow_nonneg (by norm_num) _))
  · intro i
    refine ⟨Nat.find_spec (hex i),?_⟩
    have ha0 : a i ≠ 0 := by
      intro h
      have hg := Nat.find_spec (hex i)
      change 1 < (2 : ℝ)^a i * mu i at hg
      rw [h,pow_zero,one_mul] at hg
      exact (not_lt_of_ge (hone i)) hg
    have hpred : (2 : ℝ)^(a i-1) * mu i ≤ 1 := by
      apply le_of_not_gt
      exact Nat.find_min (hex i) (Nat.sub_lt (Nat.pos_of_ne_zero ha0) (by decide))
    have heq : a i = (a i-1)+1 := by omega
    rw [heq,pow_succ]
    nlinarith

theorem normalized_product_volume_upper
    (f : (Fin 10 → ℝ) → ℝ)
    (hf : ∀ (r : ℝ) (x : Fin 10 → ℝ), f (r • x) = |r| * f x)
    (mu : Fin 10 → ℝ) (hpos : ∀ i, 0 < mu i)
    (hone : ∀ i, mu i ≤ 1) (hmono : Monotone mu)
    (hlower : ∀ (z : Fin 10 → ℤ) (i : Fin 10), z i ≠ 0 →
      (∀ j, i < j → z j = 0) → mu i ≤ f (fun j => (z j : ℝ)))
    (hsymm : ∀ x, f (-x) = f x) (hconv : Convex ℝ {x | f x ≤ 1}) :
    (∏ i, mu i) * volume.real {x | f x ≤ 1} ≤ 4^10 := by
  obtain ⟨a,ha,hscale⟩ := exists_dyadic_exponents mu hpos hone hmono
  have hvol := dyadic_volume_bound a ha f hf mu (fun i => (hscale i).1) hlower hsymm hconv
  have hp : 0 ≤ ∏ i, mu i := Finset.prod_nonneg (fun i _ => (hpos i).le)
  have hprod : (∏ i, (2 : ℝ)^a i * mu i) ≤ 2^10 := by
    calc
      _ ≤ ∏ _ : Fin 10, (2 : ℝ) :=
        Finset.prod_le_prod (fun i _ => mul_nonneg (pow_nonneg (by norm_num) _) (hpos i).le)
          (fun i _ => (hscale i).2)
      _ = _ := by simp
  calc
    _ ≤ (∏ i, mu i) * ((∏ i, (2 : ℝ)^a i) * 2^10) := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hvol hp
    _ = (∏ i, (2 : ℝ)^a i * mu i) * 2^10 := by rw [Finset.prod_mul_distrib]; ring
    _ ≤ 2^10 * 2^10 := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hprod (pow_nonneg (by norm_num) _)
    _ = 4^10 := by rw [← mul_pow]; norm_num


theorem homogeneous_sublevel_dilate (f : (Fin 10 → ℝ) → ℝ)
    (hf : ∀ (r : ℝ) (x : Fin 10 → ℝ), f (r • x) = |r| * f x)
    (t : ℝ) (ht : 0 < t) : {x | f x / t ≤ 1} = t • {x | f x ≤ 1} := by
  ext x
  constructor
  · intro hx
    refine ⟨t⁻¹ • x,?_,smul_inv_smul₀ ht.ne' x⟩
    change f (t⁻¹ • x) ≤ 1
    rw [hf,abs_of_pos (inv_pos.mpr ht)]
    simpa only [div_eq_mul_inv,mul_comm] using hx
  · rintro ⟨y,hy,rfl⟩
    change f (t • y) / t ≤ 1
    rw [hf,abs_of_pos ht,mul_div_cancel_left₀ _ ht.ne']
    exact hy

theorem adapted_product_volume_upper
    (f : (Fin 10 → ℝ) → ℝ)
    (hf : ∀ (r : ℝ) (x : Fin 10 → ℝ), f (r • x) = |r| * f x)
    (mu : Fin 10 → ℝ) (hpos : ∀ i, 0 < mu i) (hmono : Monotone mu)
    (hlower : ∀ (z : Fin 10 → ℤ) (i : Fin 10), z i ≠ 0 →
      (∀ j, i < j → z j = 0) → mu i ≤ f (fun j => (z j : ℝ)))
    (hsymm : ∀ x, f (-x) = f x) (hconv : Convex ℝ {x | f x ≤ 1}) :
    (∏ i, mu i) * volume.real {x | f x ≤ 1} ≤ 4^10 := by
  let t := 1 + ∑ i, mu i
  have ht : 0 < t := by
    dsimp [t]
    exact add_pos_of_pos_of_nonneg zero_lt_one (Finset.sum_nonneg (fun i _ => (hpos i).le))
  have hle (i : Fin 10) : mu i ≤ t := by
    have h := Finset.single_le_sum (fun j _ => (hpos j).le) (Finset.mem_univ i)
    dsimp [t]
    linarith
  have heq := homogeneous_sublevel_dilate f hf t ht
  have hconv' : Convex ℝ {x | f x / t ≤ 1} := by rw [heq]; exact hconv.smul t
  have h := normalized_product_volume_upper (fun x => f x / t)
    (fun r x => by dsimp only; rw [hf]; ring) (fun i => mu i/t)
    (fun i => div_pos (hpos i) ht) (fun i => (div_le_one ht).mpr (hle i))
    (fun i j hij => div_le_div_of_nonneg_right (hmono hij) ht.le)
    (fun z i hz htail => div_le_div_of_nonneg_right (hlower z i hz htail) ht.le)
    (fun x => by dsimp only; rw [hsymm]) hconv'
  have hvol : volume.real {x | f x/t ≤ 1} = t^10 * volume.real {x | f x ≤ 1} := by
    rw [heq,measureReal_def,Measure.addHaar_smul,Module.finrank_pi,Fintype.card_fin,
      abs_of_pos (pow_pos ht _),ENNReal.toReal_mul,ENNReal.toReal_ofReal (pow_nonneg ht.le _)]
    rfl
  rw [hvol,Finset.prod_div_distrib] at h
  simp only [Finset.prod_const,Finset.card_univ,Fintype.card_fin] at h
  have hcancel : (∏ i, mu i) / t^10 * (t^10 * volume.real {x | f x ≤ 1}) =
      (∏ i, mu i) * volume.real {x | f x ≤ 1} := by
    field_simp
    ring
  rwa [hcancel] at h

end CompoundFeasibility.Minkowski10

end
end M197Literal

namespace M197Literal
open scoped BigOperators Matrix
open MeasureTheory
namespace CompoundFeasibility
noncomputable section

def integerPoint10 {b : Basis (Fin 10) ℝ (Fin 10 → ℝ)}
    (c : Basis (Fin 10) ℤ (lattice10Span b)) (z : Fin 10 → ℤ) : Fin 10 → ℝ :=
  (c.equivFun.symm z : lattice10Span b)

theorem integerPoint10_coord {b : Basis (Fin 10) ℝ (Fin 10 → ℝ)}
    (c : Basis (Fin 10) ℤ (lattice10Span b)) (z : Fin 10 → ℤ) (i : Fin 10) :
    (c.ofZLatticeBasis ℝ _).repr (integerPoint10 c z) i = (z i : ℝ) := by
  rw [integerPoint10,Basis.ofZLatticeBasis_repr_apply]
  change ((c.equivFun (c.equivFun.symm z)) i : ℝ) = (z i : ℝ)
  rw [LinearEquiv.apply_symm_apply]

theorem integerPoint10_outside {b : Basis (Fin 10) ℝ (Fin 10 → ℝ)}
    (c : Basis (Fin 10) ℤ (lattice10Span b)) (v : Fin 10 → Fin 10 → ℝ)
    (hadapt : ∀ k : Fin 10, Submodule.span ℝ (Set.range (fun i : Fin k.val =>
      ((c (Fin.castLE (by omega) i) : lattice10Span b) : Fin 10 → ℝ))) = minimaFlag10 v k)
    (z : Fin 10 → ℤ) (k : Fin 10) (hk : z k ≠ 0) : integerPoint10 c z ∉ minimaFlag10 v k := by
  let a := c.ofZLatticeBasis ℝ (lattice10Span b)
  have hle : minimaFlag10 v k ≤ LinearMap.ker (a.coord k) := by
    rw [← hadapt k]
    apply Submodule.span_le.mpr
    rintro _ ⟨j,rfl⟩
    change a.repr ((c (Fin.castLE (by omega) j) : lattice10Span b) : Fin 10 → ℝ) k = 0
    have hne : (Fin.castLE (by omega) j : Fin 10) ≠ k := by
      intro h
      have heq := congrArg Fin.val h
      change j.val = k.val at heq
      omega
    simp [a,Basis.ofZLatticeBasis_repr_apply,hne]
  intro h
  have hzero := hle h
  change a.repr (integerPoint10 c z) k = 0 at hzero
  rw [integerPoint10_coord] at hzero
  exact hk (by exact_mod_cast hzero)

theorem successive_minima10_product_volume_upper
    (b : Basis (Fin 10) ℝ (Fin 10 → ℝ)) (e : (Fin 10 → ℝ) ≃ₗ[ℝ] (Fin 10 → ℝ)) :
    (∏ i, successiveRadius10 b e i) * volume.real {x | coordinateGauge10 e x ≤ 1} /
      ZLattice.covolume (lattice10Span b) ≤ (4 : ℝ)^10 := by
  obtain ⟨v,hv,hvL,hpos,hmono,hatt,hshort⟩ := exists_successive_minima10 b e
  obtain ⟨c,hc⟩ := exists_adapted_integer_basis10 b v hv hvL
  let a := c.ofZLatticeBasis ℝ (lattice10Span b)
  let F := a.equivFun.symm
  let f : (Fin 10 → ℝ) → ℝ := fun x => coordinateGauge10 e (F x)
  have hf : ∀ (r : ℝ) (x : Fin 10 → ℝ), f (r • x) = |r| * f x := by
    intro r x
    simp [f,coordinateGauge10,map_smul,norm_smul,Real.norm_eq_abs]
  have hp (z : Fin 10 → ℤ) : F (fun i => (z i : ℝ)) = integerPoint10 c z := by
    apply a.equivFun.injective
    ext i
    change (a.equivFun (a.equivFun.symm (fun i => (z i : ℝ)))) i = _
    rw [LinearEquiv.apply_symm_apply]
    exact (integerPoint10_coord c z i).symm
  have hbody : {x | f x ≤ 1} = a.equivFun '' {x | coordinateGauge10 e x ≤ 1} := by
    rw [LinearEquiv.image_eq_preimage]
    rfl
  have hconv : Convex ℝ {x | f x ≤ 1} := by
    simpa only [Metric.closedBall,dist_zero_right] using
      (convex_closedBall (0 : Fin 10 → ℝ) 1).linear_preimage (e.toLinearMap.comp F.toLinearMap)
  have hsymm : ∀ x, f (-x) = f x := by intro x; simp [f,coordinateGauge10,map_neg]
  have hu := Minkowski10.adapted_product_volume_upper f hf (successiveRadius10 b e) hpos hmono
    (fun z k hk _ => by
      change _ ≤ coordinateGauge10 e (F (fun i => (z i : ℝ)))
      rw [hp,← hatt k]
      exact hshort k _ (c.equivFun.symm z).property (integerPoint10_outside c v hc z k hk)) hsymm hconv
  have hvol : volume.real (a.equivFun '' {x | coordinateGauge10 e x ≤ 1}) =
      volume.real {x | coordinateGauge10 e x ≤ 1} / ZLattice.covolume (lattice10Span b) := by
    rw [measureReal_def,ZLattice.volume_image_eq_volume_div_covolume (lattice10Span b) c,
      ENNReal.toReal_div,ENNReal.toReal_ofReal (ZLattice.covolume_pos (lattice10Span b) volume).le]
    rfl
  rw [hbody,hvol] at hu
  simpa only [div_eq_mul_inv,mul_assoc] using hu

end
end CompoundFeasibility

end M197Literal

namespace M197Literal
open scoped BigOperators Matrix Pointwise
open MeasureTheory Filter
namespace CompoundFeasibility
noncomputable section

def row10 {n : ℕ} (A : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) ℚ) (i : Fin n) : (Fin n → ℤ) →+ ℚ where
  toFun x := (A *ᵥ (fun j => (x j : ℚ))) i
  map_zero' := by
    change (A *ᵥ (0 : Fin n → ℚ)) i = 0
    rw [Matrix.mulVec_zero]
    rfl
  map_add' x y := by
    simp only [Pi.add_apply,Int.cast_add]
    change (A *ᵥ ((fun j => (x j : ℚ)) + (fun j => (y j : ℚ)))) i = _
    rw [Matrix.mulVec_add]
    rfl

def lattice10 {n : ℕ} (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (A : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) ℚ)
    (e : Fin n → ℕ) : AddSubgroup (Fin n → ℤ) :=
  ⨅ i, (normBall p (e i)).comap (row10 A i)

theorem lattice10_spec {n : ℕ} (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (A : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) ℚ)
    (e : Fin n → ℕ) (x : Fin n → ℤ) : x ∈ lattice10 p A e ↔
    ∀ i, padicNorm p ((A *ᵥ (fun j => (x j : ℚ))) i) ≤ (p : ℚ) ^ (-(e i : ℤ)) := by
  simp only [lattice10,AddSubgroup.mem_iInf,AddSubgroup.mem_comap]
  change (∀ i, padicNorm p ((A *ᵥ (fun j => (x j : ℚ))) i) ≤ ((p : ℚ) ^ e i)⁻¹) ↔ _
  simp only [zpow_neg,zpow_natCast]

def sourceLattice10 (A₂ A₃ : Matrix (Fin 10) (Fin 10) ℚ)
    (e₂ e₃ : Fin 10 → ℕ) : AddSubgroup (Fin 10 → ℤ) := lattice10 2 A₂ e₂ ⊓ lattice10 3 A₃ e₃

theorem sourceLattice10_multiple (A₂ A₃ : Matrix (Fin 10) (Fin 10) ℚ)
    (e₂ e₃ : Fin 10 → ℕ) :
    ∃ D : ℤ, D ≠ 0 ∧ ∀ j : Fin 10, Pi.single j D ∈ sourceLattice10 A₂ A₃ e₂ e₃ := by
  obtain ⟨D₂,h₂,hD₂⟩ := clear_matrix_denominators A₂
  obtain ⟨D₃,h₃,hD₃⟩ := clear_matrix_denominators A₃
  let D : ℤ := D₂ * D₃ * 2 ^ (∑ s, e₂ s) * 3 ^ (∑ s, e₃ s)
  refine ⟨D, by dsimp [D]; exact mul_ne_zero (mul_ne_zero (mul_ne_zero h₂ h₃) (by positivity)) (by positivity), ?_⟩
  intro j
  constructor
  · apply (lattice10_spec 2 A₂ e₂ _).mpr
    have he : D = D₂ * 2 ^ (∑ s, e₂ s) * (D₃ * 3 ^ (∑ s, e₃ s)) := by dsimp [D]; ring
    rw [he]
    exact local_multiple_bounds 2 A₂ e₂ D₂ hD₂ _ j
  · apply (lattice10_spec 3 A₃ e₃ _).mpr
    have he : D = D₃ * 3 ^ (∑ s, e₃ s) * (D₂ * 2 ^ (∑ s, e₂ s)) := by dsimp [D]; ring
    rw [he]
    exact local_multiple_bounds 3 A₃ e₃ D₃ hD₃ _ j

theorem sourceLattice10_has_basis (A₂ A₃ : Matrix (Fin 10) (Fin 10) ℚ)
    (e₂ e₃ : Fin 10 → ℕ) : Nonempty (Basis (Fin 10) ℤ (sourceLattice10 A₂ A₃ e₂ e₃)) := by
  obtain ⟨D,hD,hm⟩ := sourceLattice10_multiple A₂ A₃ e₂ e₃
  exact integer_subgroup_has_basis _ D hD hm

instance sourceLattice10_isZLattice (A₂ A₃ : Matrix (Fin 10) (Fin 10) ℚ)
    (e₂ e₃ : Fin 10 → ℕ) : IsZLattice ℝ (integerRealImage (sourceLattice10 A₂ A₃ e₂ e₃)) where
  span_top := by
    obtain ⟨D,hD,hm⟩ := sourceLattice10_multiple A₂ A₃ e₂ e₃
    exact integerRealImage_span_top _ D hD hm

theorem sourceLattice10_covolume_det (A₂ A₃ : Matrix (Fin 10) (Fin 10) ℚ)
    (e₂ e₃ : Fin 10 → ℕ) (c : Basis (Fin 10) ℤ (sourceLattice10 A₂ A₃ e₂ e₃)) :
    ZLattice.covolume (integerRealImage (sourceLattice10 A₂ A₃ e₂ e₃)) =
      |(Matrix.of (fun j i => ((c j : Fin 10 → ℤ) i : ℝ))).det| :=
  integerRealImage_covolume_det _ c

theorem sourceLattice10_covolume_index (A₂ A₃ : Matrix (Fin 10) (Fin 10) ℚ)
    (e₂ e₃ : Fin 10 → ℕ) :
    ZLattice.covolume (integerRealImage (sourceLattice10 A₂ A₃ e₂ e₃)) =
      ((sourceLattice10 A₂ A₃ e₂ e₃).index : ℝ) := by
  obtain ⟨c⟩ := sourceLattice10_has_basis A₂ A₃ e₂ e₃
  exact integerRealImage_covolume_index _ c

theorem inverse_integral_frame10 {n : ℕ} (A : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) ℚ) (hA : IsUnit A) :
    ∃ (D : ℤ) (B : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) ℤ), D ≠ 0 ∧ B.det ≠ 0 ∧
      B.map (Int.castRingHom ℚ) = (D : ℚ) • A⁻¹ := by
  classical
  obtain ⟨D,hD,hm⟩ := clear_matrix_denominators A⁻¹
  choose b hB using hm
  let B : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) ℤ := b
  have he : (B : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) ℤ).map (Int.castRingHom ℚ) = (D : ℚ) • A⁻¹ := by
    ext i j
    exact hB i j
  refine ⟨D,B,hD,?_,he⟩
  have hd : ((B : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) ℤ).map (Int.castRingHom ℚ)).det ≠ 0 := by
    rw [he, Matrix.det_smul, Matrix.det_nonsing_inv, Ring.inverse_eq_inv]
    exact mul_ne_zero (pow_ne_zero _ (by exact_mod_cast hD))
      (inv_ne_zero (isUnit_iff_ne_zero.mp ((Matrix.isUnit_iff_isUnit_det A).mp hA)))
  intro h
  apply hd
  rw [← integer_matrix_det_cast, h, Int.cast_zero]

theorem lattice10_index_upper {n : ℕ} (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime]
    (A : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) ℚ) (D : ℤ) (B : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) ℤ)
    (hB : B.det ≠ 0)
    (he : B.map (Int.castRingHom ℚ) = (D : ℚ) • A⁻¹) (hA : IsUnit A)
    (e : Fin n → ℕ) :
    (lattice10 p A e).index ≤ B.det.natAbs * p ^ (∑ i, e i) := by
  classical
  let U := B * Matrix.diagonal (fun i => (p : ℤ) ^ e i)
  have hd : U.det = B.det * (p : ℤ) ^ (∑ i, e i) := by
    rw [Matrix.det_mul, Matrix.det_diagonal, Finset.prod_pow_eq_pow_sum]
  have hp : p ≠ 0 := (Fact.out : p.Prime).ne_zero
  have hU : U.det ≠ 0 := by rw [hd]; exact mul_ne_zero hB (pow_ne_zero _ (by exact_mod_cast hp))
  have hm (j : Fin n) : U.col j ∈ lattice10 p A e := by
    apply (lattice10_spec p A e _).mpr
    have hcol : (fun k => (U.col j k : ℚ)) =
        (p : ℚ) ^ e j • (B.map (Int.castRingHom ℚ)).col j := by
      ext k
      simp [U,Matrix.mul_diagonal,Matrix.col_apply,smul_eq_mul,mul_comm]
    have hmul : A * B.map (Int.castRingHom ℚ) = (D : ℚ) • (1 : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) ℚ) := by
      rw [he,Matrix.mul_smul,Matrix.mul_nonsing_inv A ((Matrix.isUnit_iff_isUnit_det A).mp hA)]
    have hact : A *ᵥ (B.map (Int.castRingHom ℚ)).col j =
        ((D : ℚ) • (1 : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) ℚ)).col j := by
      change (A * B.map (Int.castRingHom ℚ)).col j = _
      rw [hmul]
    intro i
    rw [hcol,Matrix.mulVec_smul,hact]
    by_cases hij : i = j
    · subst i
      simp only [Pi.smul_apply,smul_eq_mul,Matrix.col_apply,Matrix.smul_apply,Matrix.one_apply_eq,mul_one]
      rw [show (p : ℚ) ^ e j * (D : ℚ) = (((p : ℤ) ^ e j * D : ℤ) : ℚ) by push_cast; ring]
      apply padicNorm.dvd_iff_norm_le.mp
      exact_mod_cast (dvd_mul_right ((p : ℤ) ^ e j) D)
    · simp [Matrix.col_apply,Matrix.one_apply,hij,Ne.symm hij]
  have h := integer_matrix_index_upper5 (lattice10 p A e) U hU hm
  rw [hd,Int.natAbs_mul,Int.natAbs_pow,Int.natAbs_natCast] at h
  exact h

theorem sourceLattice10_uniform_index_upper (A₂ A₃ : Matrix (Fin 10) (Fin 10) ℚ)
    (h₂ : IsUnit A₂) (h₃ : IsUnit A₃) :
    ∃ K : ℕ, 0 < K ∧ ∀ e₂ e₃ : Fin 10 → ℕ,
      (sourceLattice10 A₂ A₃ e₂ e₃).index ≤
        K * 2 ^ (∑ i, e₂ i) * 3 ^ (∑ i, e₃ i) := by
  obtain ⟨D₂,B₂,hD₂,hB₂,he₂⟩ := inverse_integral_frame10 A₂ h₂
  obtain ⟨D₃,B₃,hD₃,hB₃,he₃⟩ := inverse_integral_frame10 A₃ h₃
  refine ⟨B₂.det.natAbs * B₃.det.natAbs,
    Nat.mul_pos (Int.natAbs_pos.mpr hB₂) (Int.natAbs_pos.mpr hB₃),?_⟩
  intro e₂ e₃
  have h2 := lattice10_index_upper 2 A₂ D₂ B₂ hB₂ he₂ h₂ e₂
  have h3 := lattice10_index_upper 3 A₃ D₃ B₃ hB₃ he₃ h₃ e₃
  have h := (AddSubgroup.index_inf_le (H := lattice10 2 A₂ e₂) (K := lattice10 3 A₃ e₃)).trans
    (Nat.mul_le_mul h2 h3)
  simpa only [sourceLattice10, Nat.mul_assoc,Nat.mul_left_comm,Nat.mul_comm] using h

theorem q_prime_powers_product_upper10 (p : ℕ) (hp : 1 < p)
    (Q : ℝ) (hQ : 1 < Q) (c : Fin 10 → ℝ) (hc : ∀ i, c i ≤ 0) :
    (p : ℝ) ^ (∑ i, Nat.ceil (JSP000334.qThreshold p Q (c i))) ≤
      (p : ℝ) ^ 10 * ∏ i, Q ^ (-c i) := by
  have hp0 : (0 : ℝ) < p := by exact_mod_cast (lt_trans Nat.zero_lt_one hp)
  have hstep (i : Fin 10) :
      (p : ℝ) ^ Nat.ceil (JSP000334.qThreshold p Q (c i)) ≤ (p : ℝ) * Q ^ (-c i) := by
    rw [← JSP000334.qThreshold_rpow p hp Q (c i) hQ, ← Real.rpow_natCast]
    calc
      _ ≤ (p : ℝ) ^ (JSP000334.qThreshold p Q (c i) + 1) :=
        Real.rpow_le_rpow_of_exponent_le (by exact_mod_cast hp.le)
          (Nat.ceil_lt_add_one (JSP000334.qThreshold_nonneg p hp Q (c i) hQ (hc i))).le
      _ = _ := by rw [Real.rpow_add hp0,Real.rpow_one]; ring
  rw [← Finset.prod_pow_eq_pow_sum]
  have h := Finset.prod_le_prod (s := Finset.univ) (fun i _ => pow_nonneg hp0.le _) (fun i _ => hstep i)
  simpa only [Finset.prod_mul_distrib,Finset.prod_const,Finset.card_univ,Fintype.card_fin] using h

theorem q_prime_powers_normalized_upper10 (Q : ℝ) (hQ : 1 < Q)
    (c₀ c₂ c₃ : Fin 10 → ℝ) (h₂ : ∀ i, c₂ i ≤ 0) (h₃ : ∀ i, c₃ i ≤ 0)
    (hb : ∑ i, (c₀ i + c₂ i + c₃ i) = 0) :
    (2 : ℝ) ^ (∑ i, Nat.ceil (JSP000334.qThreshold 2 Q (c₂ i))) *
      3 ^ (∑ i, Nat.ceil (JSP000334.qThreshold 3 Q (c₃ i))) ≤
      60466176 * ∏ i, Q ^ c₀ i := by
  have hQ0 := lt_trans zero_lt_one hQ
  have hprod : (∏ i, Q ^ (-c₂ i)) * (∏ i, Q ^ (-c₃ i)) = ∏ i, Q ^ c₀ i := by
    rw [← Finset.prod_mul_distrib]
    simp_rw [← Real.rpow_add hQ0]
    rw [← Real.rpow_sum_of_pos hQ0 _ Finset.univ, ← Real.rpow_sum_of_pos hQ0 _ Finset.univ]
    congr 1
    simp only [Finset.sum_add_distrib,Finset.sum_neg_distrib] at hb ⊢
    linarith
  have h := mul_le_mul (q_prime_powers_product_upper10 2 (by decide) Q hQ c₂ h₂)
    (q_prime_powers_product_upper10 3 (by decide) Q hQ c₃ h₃)
    (by positivity) (by positivity)
  calc
    _ ≤ ((2 : ℝ) ^ 10 * ∏ i, Q ^ (-c₂ i)) * (3 ^ 10 * ∏ i, Q ^ (-c₃ i)) := h
    _ = ((2 : ℝ) ^ 10 * 3 ^ 10) * ((∏ i, Q ^ (-c₂ i)) * ∏ i, Q ^ (-c₃ i)) := by ring
    _ = _ := by rw [hprod]; norm_num

theorem sourceLattice10_uniform_normalized_covolume_upper
    (A₂ A₃ : Matrix (Fin 10) (Fin 10) ℚ) (h₂ : IsUnit A₂) (h₃ : IsUnit A₃) :
    ∃ K : ℕ, 0 < K ∧ ∀ (Q : ℝ), 1 < Q → ∀ c₀ c₂ c₃ : Fin 10 → ℝ,
      (∀ i, c₂ i ≤ 0) → (∀ i, c₃ i ≤ 0) →
      (∑ i, (c₀ i + c₂ i + c₃ i) = 0) →
      ZLattice.covolume (integerRealImage (sourceLattice10 A₂ A₃
        (fun i => Nat.ceil (JSP000334.qThreshold 2 Q (c₂ i)))
        (fun i => Nat.ceil (JSP000334.qThreshold 3 Q (c₃ i))))) /
        (∏ i, Q ^ c₀ i) ≤ (K : ℝ) * 60466176 := by
  obtain ⟨K,hK,hindex⟩ := sourceLattice10_uniform_index_upper A₂ A₃ h₂ h₃
  refine ⟨K,hK,?_⟩
  intro Q hQ c₀ c₂ c₃ hc₂ hc₃ hb
  have hW : 0 < ∏ i, Q ^ c₀ i := Finset.prod_pos
    (fun i _ => Real.rpow_pos_of_pos (lt_trans zero_lt_one hQ) _)
  apply (div_le_iff₀ hW).mpr
  rw [sourceLattice10_covolume_index]
  have hi := hindex (fun i => Nat.ceil (JSP000334.qThreshold 2 Q (c₂ i)))
    (fun i => Nat.ceil (JSP000334.qThreshold 3 Q (c₃ i)))
  have hiR : (((sourceLattice10 A₂ A₃
      (fun i => Nat.ceil (JSP000334.qThreshold 2 Q (c₂ i)))
      (fun i => Nat.ceil (JSP000334.qThreshold 3 Q (c₃ i))))).index : ℝ) ≤
      (K : ℝ) * 2 ^ (∑ i, Nat.ceil (JSP000334.qThreshold 2 Q (c₂ i))) *
        3 ^ (∑ i, Nat.ceil (JSP000334.qThreshold 3 Q (c₃ i))) := by exact_mod_cast hi
  have hn := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (q_prime_powers_normalized_upper10 Q hQ c₀ c₂ c₃ hc₂ hc₃ hb)
    (Nat.cast_nonneg K : (0 : ℝ) ≤ K)
  exact hiR.trans (by simpa only [mul_assoc] using hn)


end
end CompoundFeasibility

end M197Literal

namespace M197Literal
open scoped BigOperators Matrix
open MeasureTheory
namespace CompoundFeasibility
noncomputable section
noncomputable def weightedCoordinates10 {n : ℕ} (e : (Fin n → ℝ) ≃ₗ[ℝ] (Fin n → ℝ))
    (w : Fin n → ℝ) (hw : ∀ i, 0 < w i) : (Fin n → ℝ) ≃ₗ[ℝ] (Fin n → ℝ) where
  toFun x i := e x i / w i
  invFun y := e.symm (fun i => y i * w i)
  map_add' x y := by ext i; simp [add_div]
  map_smul' r x := by ext i; simp [smul_eq_mul,mul_div_assoc]
  left_inv x := by
    change e.symm (fun i => (e x i / w i) * w i) = x
    have h : (fun i => (e x i / w i) * w i) = e x :=
      funext (fun i => div_mul_cancel₀ _ (hw i).ne')
    rw [h,e.symm_apply_apply]
  right_inv y := by
    change (fun i => e (e.symm (fun j => y j * w j)) i / w i) = y
    rw [e.apply_symm_apply]
    exact funext (fun i => mul_div_cancel_right₀ _ (hw i).ne')

noncomputable def originalFormEquiv10 (L : Matrix (Fin 10) (Fin 10) ℝ)
    (hL : IsUnit L) : (Fin 10 → ℝ) ≃ₗ[ℝ] (Fin 10 → ℝ) :=
  LinearEquiv.ofBijective (Matrix.toLin' L) (by
    have hu := hL
    exact ⟨Matrix.mulVec_injective_iff_isUnit.mpr hu,Matrix.mulVec_surjective_iff_isUnit.mpr hu⟩)

theorem originalFormEquiv10_apply (L : Matrix (Fin 10) (Fin 10) ℝ)
    (hL : IsUnit L) (x : Fin 10 → ℝ) : originalFormEquiv10 L hL x = L *ᵥ x := rfl

noncomputable def originalCoordinates10 (L : Matrix (Fin 10) (Fin 10) ℝ) (hL : IsUnit L)
    (Q : ℝ) (hQ : 1 < Q) (c₀ : Fin 10 → ℝ) : (Fin 10 → ℝ) ≃ₗ[ℝ] (Fin 10 → ℝ) :=
  weightedCoordinates10 (originalFormEquiv10 L hL)
    (fun s => Q ^ c₀ s)
    (fun s => Real.rpow_pos_of_pos (lt_trans zero_lt_one hQ) _)

def originalBody10 (L : Matrix (Fin 10) (Fin 10) ℝ) (Q : ℝ) (c₀ : Fin 10 → ℝ) : Set (Fin 10 → ℝ) :=
  {x | ∀ s, |(L *ᵥ x) s| ≤ Q ^ c₀ s}

theorem originalCoordinates10_bounds (L : Matrix (Fin 10) (Fin 10) ℝ) (hL : IsUnit L)
    (Q : ℝ) (hQ : 1 < Q) (c₀ : Fin 10 → ℝ) (x : Fin 10 → ℝ) (r : ℝ) :
    ‖originalCoordinates10 L hL Q hQ c₀ x‖ ≤ r ↔
      ∀ s, |(L *ᵥ x) s| ≤ r * (Q ^ c₀ s) := by
  rw [pi_norm_le_iff_of_nonempty]
  apply forall_congr'
  intro s
  have hw : 0 < Q ^ c₀ s := Real.rpow_pos_of_pos (lt_trans zero_lt_one hQ) _
  change ‖(L *ᵥ x) s / (Q ^ c₀ s)‖ ≤ r ↔ _
  rw [Real.norm_eq_abs,abs_div,abs_of_pos hw,div_le_iff₀ hw]

theorem originalBody10_mem_iff (L : Matrix (Fin 10) (Fin 10) ℝ) (hL : IsUnit L)
    (Q : ℝ) (hQ : 1 < Q) (c₀ : Fin 10 → ℝ) (x : Fin 10 → ℝ) :
    x ∈ originalBody10 L Q c₀ ↔ ‖originalCoordinates10 L hL Q hQ c₀ x‖ ≤ 1 := by
  rw [originalCoordinates10_bounds]
  simp only [one_mul,originalBody10,Set.mem_setOf_eq]


noncomputable def sourceRealBasis10 (A₂ A₃ : Matrix (Fin 10) (Fin 10) ℚ)
    (e₂ e₃ : Fin 10 → ℕ) : Basis (Fin 10) ℝ (Fin 10 → ℝ) :=
  (integerRealImageBasis (sourceLattice10 A₂ A₃ e₂ e₃)
    (sourceLattice10_has_basis A₂ A₃ e₂ e₃).some).ofZLatticeBasis ℝ
      (integerRealImage (sourceLattice10 A₂ A₃ e₂ e₃))

theorem sourceRealBasis10_span (A₂ A₃ : Matrix (Fin 10) (Fin 10) ℚ)
    (e₂ e₃ : Fin 10 → ℕ) :
    lattice10Span (sourceRealBasis10 A₂ A₃ e₂ e₃) = integerRealImage (sourceLattice10 A₂ A₃ e₂ e₃) :=
  Basis.ofZLatticeBasis_span ℝ (integerRealImage (sourceLattice10 A₂ A₃ e₂ e₃))
    (integerRealImageBasis (sourceLattice10 A₂ A₃ e₂ e₃) (sourceLattice10_has_basis A₂ A₃ e₂ e₃).some)

noncomputable def originalSourceGroup10 (A₂ A₃ : Matrix (Fin 10) (Fin 10) ℚ)
    (Q : ℝ) (c₂ c₃ : Fin 10 → ℝ) : AddSubgroup (Fin 10 → ℤ) :=
  sourceLattice10 A₂ A₃ (fun i => Nat.ceil (JSP000334.qThreshold 2 Q (c₂ i)))
    (fun i => Nat.ceil (JSP000334.qThreshold 3 Q (c₃ i)))

noncomputable def sourceMinima10 (A₂ A₃ : Matrix (Fin 10) (Fin 10) ℚ)
    (L : Matrix (Fin 10) (Fin 10) ℝ) (hL : IsUnit L)
    (Q : ℝ) (hQ : 1 < Q) (c₀ c₂ c₃ : Fin 10 → ℝ) (j : Fin 10) : ℝ :=
  sInf {r : ℝ | ∃ u : Fin (j.val+1) → (Fin 10 → ℝ), LinearIndependent ℝ u ∧
    (∀ i, u i ∈ integerRealImage (originalSourceGroup10 A₂ A₃ Q c₂ c₃)) ∧
    ∀ i, coordinateGauge10 (originalCoordinates10 L hL Q hQ c₀) (u i) ≤ r}

theorem sourceMinima10_eq_radius (A₂ A₃ : Matrix (Fin 10) (Fin 10) ℚ)
    (L : Matrix (Fin 10) (Fin 10) ℝ) (hL : IsUnit L)
    (Q : ℝ) (hQ : 1 < Q) (c₀ c₂ c₃ : Fin 10 → ℝ) (j : Fin 10) :
    sourceMinima10 A₂ A₃ L hL Q hQ c₀ c₂ c₃ j =
      successiveRadius10 (sourceRealBasis10 A₂ A₃
        (fun i => Nat.ceil (JSP000334.qThreshold 2 Q (c₂ i)))
        (fun i => Nat.ceil (JSP000334.qThreshold 3 Q (c₃ i))))
        (originalCoordinates10 L hL Q hQ c₀) j := by
  simp only [sourceMinima10,successiveRadius10,sourceRealBasis10_span,originalSourceGroup10]

theorem exists_sourceMinima10_attaining (A₂ A₃ : Matrix (Fin 10) (Fin 10) ℚ)
    (L : Matrix (Fin 10) (Fin 10) ℝ) (hL : IsUnit L)
    (Q : ℝ) (hQ : 1 < Q) (c₀ c₂ c₃ : Fin 10 → ℝ) :
    ∃ u : Fin 10 → originalSourceGroup10 A₂ A₃ Q c₂ c₃,
      LinearIndependent ℝ (fun j => integerReal (u j : Fin 10 → ℤ)) ∧
      (∀ j, 0 < sourceMinima10 A₂ A₃ L hL Q hQ c₀ c₂ c₃ j) ∧
      Monotone (sourceMinima10 A₂ A₃ L hL Q hQ c₀ c₂ c₃) ∧
      (∀ j, coordinateGauge10 (originalCoordinates10 L hL Q hQ c₀)
        (integerReal (u j : Fin 10 → ℤ)) = sourceMinima10 A₂ A₃ L hL Q hQ c₀ c₂ c₃ j) ∧
      ∀ j s, |(L *ᵥ integerReal (u j : Fin 10 → ℤ)) s| ≤
        sourceMinima10 A₂ A₃ L hL Q hQ c₀ c₂ c₃ j * (Q ^ c₀ s) := by
  classical
  let e₂ := fun i => Nat.ceil (JSP000334.qThreshold 2 Q (c₂ i))
  let e₃ := fun i => Nat.ceil (JSP000334.qThreshold 3 Q (c₃ i))
  let b := sourceRealBasis10 A₂ A₃ e₂ e₃
  let e := originalCoordinates10 L hL Q hQ c₀
  obtain ⟨v,hv,hvL,hpos,hmono,hatt,_⟩ := exists_successive_minima10 b e
  have hrad (j : Fin 10) : sourceMinima10 A₂ A₃ L hL Q hQ c₀ c₂ c₃ j = successiveRadius10 b e j :=
    sourceMinima10_eq_radius A₂ A₃ L hL Q hQ c₀ c₂ c₃ j
  have hvImage (j : Fin 10) : v j ∈ integerRealImage (originalSourceGroup10 A₂ A₃ Q c₂ c₃) := by
    change v j ∈ integerRealImage (sourceLattice10 A₂ A₃ e₂ e₃)
    rw [← sourceRealBasis10_span A₂ A₃ e₂ e₃]
    exact hvL j
  have hlift (j : Fin 10) : ∃ x : originalSourceGroup10 A₂ A₃ Q c₂ c₃,
      integerReal (x : Fin 10 → ℤ) = v j := by
    obtain ⟨x,hx,he⟩ := hvImage j
    exact ⟨⟨x,hx⟩,he⟩
  choose u hu using hlift
  have hfun : (fun j => integerReal (u j : Fin 10 → ℤ)) = v := funext hu
  refine ⟨u,?_,?_,?_,?_,?_⟩
  · rw [hfun]
    exact hv
  · intro j
    rw [hrad]
    exact hpos j
  · intro i j hij
    rw [hrad,hrad]
    exact hmono hij
  · intro j
    rw [hu j,hrad]
    exact hatt j
  · intro j
    apply (originalCoordinates10_bounds L hL Q hQ c₀ _ _).mp
    change coordinateGauge10 e (integerReal (u j : Fin 10 → ℤ)) ≤ _
    rw [hu j,hrad,hatt j]


theorem sourceMinima10_admissible (A₂ A₃ : Matrix (Fin 10) (Fin 10) ℚ)
    (L : Matrix (Fin 10) (Fin 10) ℝ) (hL : IsUnit L)
    (Q : ℝ) (hQ : 1 < Q) (c₀ c₂ c₃ : Fin 10 → ℝ) (j : Fin 10) :
    ∃ v : Fin (j.val+1) → (Fin 10 → ℝ), LinearIndependent ℝ v ∧
      (∀ i, v i ∈ integerRealImage (originalSourceGroup10 A₂ A₃ Q c₂ c₃)) ∧
      ∀ i, coordinateGauge10 (originalCoordinates10 L hL Q hQ c₀) (v i) ≤
        sourceMinima10 A₂ A₃ L hL Q hQ c₀ c₂ c₃ j := by
  obtain ⟨u,hu,hpos,hmono,hatt,hpoint⟩ := exists_sourceMinima10_attaining A₂ A₃ L hL Q hQ c₀ c₂ c₃
  let f : Fin (j.val+1) → Fin 10 := Fin.castLE (by omega)
  refine ⟨fun i => integerReal (u (f i) : Fin 10 → ℤ),
    hu.comp f (Fin.castLE_injective _),?_,?_⟩
  · intro i
    exact ⟨(u (f i) : Fin 10 → ℤ),(u (f i)).property,rfl⟩
  · intro i
    rw [hatt]
    exact hmono (show f i ≤ j from (by change i.val ≤ j.val; omega))

theorem sourceMinima10_radius_set_properties (A₂ A₃ : Matrix (Fin 10) (Fin 10) ℚ)
    (L : Matrix (Fin 10) (Fin 10) ℝ) (hL : IsUnit L)
    (Q : ℝ) (hQ : 1 < Q) (c₀ c₂ c₃ : Fin 10 → ℝ) (j : Fin 10) :
    let S := {r : ℝ | ∃ v : Fin (j.val+1) → (Fin 10 → ℝ), LinearIndependent ℝ v ∧
      (∀ i, v i ∈ integerRealImage (originalSourceGroup10 A₂ A₃ Q c₂ c₃)) ∧
      ∀ i, coordinateGauge10 (originalCoordinates10 L hL Q hQ c₀) (v i) ≤ r}
    S.Nonempty ∧ BddBelow S ∧ IsLeast S (sourceMinima10 A₂ A₃ L hL Q hQ c₀ c₂ c₃ j) := by
  dsimp only
  have hm := sourceMinima10_admissible A₂ A₃ L hL Q hQ c₀ c₂ c₃ j
  have hne : ({r : ℝ | ∃ v : Fin (j.val+1) → (Fin 10 → ℝ), LinearIndependent ℝ v ∧
      (∀ i, v i ∈ integerRealImage (originalSourceGroup10 A₂ A₃ Q c₂ c₃)) ∧
      ∀ i, coordinateGauge10 (originalCoordinates10 L hL Q hQ c₀) (v i) ≤ r}).Nonempty := ⟨_,hm⟩
  have hb : BddBelow {r : ℝ | ∃ v : Fin (j.val+1) → (Fin 10 → ℝ), LinearIndependent ℝ v ∧
      (∀ i, v i ∈ integerRealImage (originalSourceGroup10 A₂ A₃ Q c₂ c₃)) ∧
      ∀ i, coordinateGauge10 (originalCoordinates10 L hL Q hQ c₀) (v i) ≤ r} := by
    refine ⟨0,?_⟩
    rintro r ⟨v,_,_,hv⟩
    exact (norm_nonneg _).trans (hv 0)
  refine ⟨hne,hb,hm,?_⟩
  intro r hr
  exact csInf_le hb hr

theorem sourceMinima10_pos_mono (A₂ A₃ : Matrix (Fin 10) (Fin 10) ℚ)
    (L : Matrix (Fin 10) (Fin 10) ℝ) (hL : IsUnit L)
    (Q : ℝ) (hQ : 1 < Q) (c₀ c₂ c₃ : Fin 10 → ℝ) :
    (∀ j, 0 < sourceMinima10 A₂ A₃ L hL Q hQ c₀ c₂ c₃ j) ∧
      Monotone (sourceMinima10 A₂ A₃ L hL Q hQ c₀ c₂ c₃) := by
  obtain ⟨u,hu,hpos,hmono,hatt,hpoint⟩ := exists_sourceMinima10_attaining A₂ A₃ L hL Q hQ c₀ c₂ c₃
  exact ⟨hpos,hmono⟩

theorem weightedCoordinates10_det {n : ℕ} (A : (Fin n → ℝ) ≃ₗ[ℝ] (Fin n → ℝ)) (w : Fin n → ℝ)
    (hw : ∀ i, 0 < w i) :
    LinearMap.det (weightedCoordinates10 A w hw).toLinearMap = LinearMap.det A.toLinearMap / ∏ i, w i := by
  let B := Pi.basisFun ℝ (Fin n)
  have hm : LinearMap.toMatrix B B (weightedCoordinates10 A w hw).toLinearMap =
      Matrix.diagonal (fun i => (w i)⁻¹) * LinearMap.toMatrix B B A.toLinearMap := by
    ext i j
    simp [LinearMap.toMatrix_apply, B, Pi.basisFun_repr, Matrix.diagonal_mul,
      weightedCoordinates10, div_eq_mul_inv, mul_comm]
  rw [← LinearMap.det_toMatrix B, hm, Matrix.det_mul, Matrix.det_diagonal,
    Finset.prod_inv_distrib, LinearMap.det_toMatrix]
  ring

theorem originalCoordinates10_det (L : Matrix (Fin 10) (Fin 10) ℝ) (hL : IsUnit L)
    (Q : ℝ) (hQ : 1 < Q) (c₀ : Fin 10 → ℝ) :
    LinearMap.det (originalCoordinates10 L hL Q hQ c₀).toLinearMap = L.det / ∏ i, Q ^ c₀ i := by
  rw [originalCoordinates10,weightedCoordinates10_det]
  congr 1
  exact LinearMap.det_toLin' L

theorem originalBody10_volume (L : Matrix (Fin 10) (Fin 10) ℝ) (hL : IsUnit L)
    (Q : ℝ) (hQ : 1 < Q) (c₀ : Fin 10 → ℝ) :
    volume.real (originalBody10 L Q c₀) = 1024 * (∏ i, Q ^ c₀ i) / |L.det| := by
  have hw : 0 < ∏ i, Q ^ c₀ i := Finset.prod_pos
    (fun i _ => Real.rpow_pos_of_pos (lt_trans zero_lt_one hQ) _)
  have he : originalBody10 L Q c₀ = (originalCoordinates10 L hL Q hQ c₀) ⁻¹'
      Set.pi Set.univ (fun _ : Fin 10 => Set.Icc (-1 : ℝ) 1) := by
    ext x
    rw [originalBody10_mem_iff L hL Q hQ c₀ x,pi_norm_le_iff_of_nonempty]
    simp only [Set.mem_preimage,Set.mem_pi,Set.mem_univ,forall_const,Set.mem_Icc,Real.norm_eq_abs,abs_le]
  have hvol : volume.real (originalBody10 L Q c₀) =
      1024 / |LinearMap.det (originalCoordinates10 L hL Q hQ c₀).toLinearMap| := by
    rw [measureReal_def,he,Measure.addHaar_preimage_linearEquiv,volume_pi,Measure.pi_pi]
    simp only [Real.volume_Icc,LinearEquiv.det_coe_symm,abs_inv,
      ENNReal.toReal_mul,ENNReal.toReal_ofReal,abs_nonneg,div_eq_mul_inv]
    norm_num
    ring
  rw [hvol,originalCoordinates10_det,abs_div,abs_of_pos hw]
  field_simp



end
end CompoundFeasibility

theorem q_width_product_le_prime_powers10 (Q : ℝ) (hQ : 1 < Q)
    (c₀ c₂ c₃ : Fin 10 → ℝ) (hb : ∑ i, (c₀ i + c₂ i + c₃ i) = 0) :
    (∏ i, Q ^ c₀ i) ≤
      (2 : ℝ) ^ (∑ i, Nat.ceil (JSP000334.qThreshold 2 Q (c₂ i))) *
      3 ^ (∑ i, Nat.ceil (JSP000334.qThreshold 3 Q (c₃ i))) := by
  have hQpos : 0 < Q := lt_trans zero_lt_one hQ
  have hi (p : ℕ) (hp : 1 < p) (c : ℝ) :
      Q ^ (-c) ≤ (p : ℝ) ^ Nat.ceil (JSP000334.qThreshold p Q c) := by
    rw [← JSP000334.qThreshold_rpow p hp Q c hQ, ← Real.rpow_natCast]
    exact Real.rpow_le_rpow_of_exponent_le (by exact_mod_cast hp.le) (Nat.le_ceil _)
  have heq : (∏ i, Q ^ (-c₂ i) * Q ^ (-c₃ i)) = ∏ i, Q ^ c₀ i := by
    simp_rw [← Real.rpow_add hQpos]
    rw [← Real.rpow_sum_of_pos hQpos _ Finset.univ, ← Real.rpow_sum_of_pos hQpos _ Finset.univ]
    congr 1
    simp only [Finset.sum_add_distrib,Finset.sum_neg_distrib]
    simp only [Finset.sum_add_distrib] at hb
    linarith
  rw [← heq, ← Finset.prod_pow_eq_pow_sum Finset.univ _ (2 : ℝ),
    ← Finset.prod_pow_eq_pow_sum Finset.univ _ (3 : ℝ), ← Finset.prod_mul_distrib]
  exact Finset.prod_le_prod (fun i _ => mul_nonneg (Real.rpow_pos_of_pos hQpos _).le (Real.rpow_pos_of_pos hQpos _).le)
    (fun i _ => mul_le_mul (hi 2 (by decide) _) (hi 3 (by decide) _)
      (Real.rpow_pos_of_pos hQpos _).le (by positivity))


def sourceLocalDetFactor10 (A₂ A₃ : Matrix (Fin 10) (Fin 10) ℚ) : ℝ :=
  (padicNorm 2 A₂.det : ℝ) * (padicNorm 3 A₃.det : ℝ)

theorem sourceLocalDetFactor10_pos (A₂ A₃ : Matrix (Fin 10) (Fin 10) ℚ)
    (h₂ : IsUnit A₂) (h₃ : IsUnit A₃) : 0 < sourceLocalDetFactor10 A₂ A₃ := by
  have hd₂ := isUnit_iff_ne_zero.mp ((Matrix.isUnit_iff_isUnit_det _).mp h₂)
  have hd₃ := isUnit_iff_ne_zero.mp ((Matrix.isUnit_iff_isUnit_det _).mp h₃)
  exact mul_pos
    (Rat.cast_pos.mpr ((IsAbsoluteValue.toAbsoluteValue (padicNorm 2)).pos_iff.mpr hd₂))
    (Rat.cast_pos.mpr ((IsAbsoluteValue.toAbsoluteValue (padicNorm 3)).pos_iff.mpr hd₃))

theorem sourceLattice10_covolume_width_lower (A₂ A₃ : Matrix (Fin 10) (Fin 10) ℚ)
    (Q : ℝ) (hQ : 1 < Q) (c₀ c₂ c₃ : Fin 10 → ℝ)
    (hb : ∑ i, (c₀ i+c₂ i+c₃ i) = 0) :
    sourceLocalDetFactor10 A₂ A₃ * (∏ i, Q^c₀ i) ≤
      ZLattice.covolume (integerRealImage (originalSourceGroup10 A₂ A₃ Q c₂ c₃)) := by
  let e₂ := fun i => Nat.ceil (JSP000334.qThreshold 2 Q (c₂ i))
  let e₃ := fun i => Nat.ceil (JSP000334.qThreshold 3 Q (c₃ i))
  obtain ⟨c⟩ := sourceLattice10_has_basis A₂ A₃ e₂ e₃
  have hlow := integerRealImage_padic_covolume_lower (sourceLattice10 A₂ A₃ e₂ e₃) c A₂ A₃ e₂ e₃
    (fun x hx => (lattice10_spec 2 A₂ e₂ x).mp hx.1)
    (fun x hx => (lattice10_spec 3 A₃ e₃ x).mp hx.2)
  have hf : 0 ≤ sourceLocalDetFactor10 A₂ A₃ := mul_nonneg
    (Rat.cast_nonneg.mpr (padicNorm.nonneg _)) (Rat.cast_nonneg.mpr (padicNorm.nonneg _))
  exact (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (q_width_product_le_prime_powers10 Q hQ c₀ c₂ c₃ hb) hf).trans
    (by simpa only [sourceLocalDetFactor10,mul_assoc] using hlow)

theorem originalBody10_compact_convex (L : Matrix (Fin 10) (Fin 10) ℝ) (hL : IsUnit L)
    (Q : ℝ) (hQ : 1 < Q) (c₀ : Fin 10 → ℝ) :
    IsCompact (originalBody10 L Q c₀) ∧ Convex ℝ (originalBody10 L Q c₀) := by
  let e := originalCoordinates10 L hL Q hQ c₀
  have he : originalBody10 L Q c₀ = e ⁻¹' Metric.closedBall (0 : Fin 10 → ℝ) 1 := by
    ext x
    rw [originalBody10_mem_iff L hL Q hQ c₀ x]
    simp only [Set.mem_preimage,Metric.mem_closedBall,dist_zero_right,e]
  rw [he]
  exact ⟨e.toContinuousLinearEquiv.toHomeomorph.isCompact_preimage.mpr (isCompact_closedBall _ _),
    (convex_closedBall (0 : Fin 10 → ℝ) 1).linear_preimage e.toLinearMap⟩

theorem originalBody10_symmetric (L : Matrix (Fin 10) (Fin 10) ℝ) (Q : ℝ) (c₀ : Fin 10 → ℝ) :
    ∀ x ∈ originalBody10 L Q c₀, -x ∈ originalBody10 L Q c₀ := by
  intro x hx i
  simpa only [Matrix.mulVec_neg,Pi.neg_apply,abs_neg] using hx i

theorem originalBody10_zero_interior (L : Matrix (Fin 10) (Fin 10) ℝ) (hL : IsUnit L)
    (Q : ℝ) (hQ : 1 < Q) (c₀ : Fin 10 → ℝ) : 0 ∈ interior (originalBody10 L Q c₀) := by
  let e := originalCoordinates10 L hL Q hQ c₀
  have ho : IsOpen (e ⁻¹' Metric.ball (0 : Fin 10 → ℝ) 1) :=
    Metric.isOpen_ball.preimage e.toContinuousLinearEquiv.continuous
  apply mem_interior_iff_mem_nhds.mpr
  apply Filter.mem_of_superset (ho.mem_nhds (by simp))
  intro x hx
  rw [originalBody10_mem_iff L hL Q hQ c₀ x]
  exact (by simpa only [Set.mem_preimage,Metric.mem_ball,dist_zero_right] using hx : ‖e x‖ < 1).le

theorem originalBody10_volume_pos (L : Matrix (Fin 10) (Fin 10) ℝ) (hL : IsUnit L)
    (Q : ℝ) (hQ : 1 < Q) (c₀ : Fin 10 → ℝ) : 0 < volume.real (originalBody10 L Q c₀) := by
  rw [originalBody10_volume L hL Q hQ]
  have hd : L.det ≠ 0 := isUnit_iff_ne_zero.mp ((Matrix.isUnit_iff_isUnit_det _).mp hL)
  exact div_pos (mul_pos (by norm_num) (Finset.prod_pos (fun i _ => Real.rpow_pos_of_pos (by linarith) _))) (abs_pos.mpr hd)

end
end CompoundFeasibility

instance originalSourceGroup10_isZLattice (A₂ A₃ : Matrix (Fin 10) (Fin 10) ℚ)
    (Q : ℝ) (c₂ c₃ : Fin 10 → ℝ) :
    IsZLattice ℝ (integerRealImage (originalSourceGroup10 A₂ A₃ Q c₂ c₃)) := by
  change IsZLattice ℝ (integerRealImage (sourceLattice10 A₂ A₃
    (fun i => Nat.ceil (JSP000334.qThreshold 2 Q (c₂ i)))
    (fun i => Nat.ceil (JSP000334.qThreshold 3 Q (c₃ i)))))
  infer_instance


theorem scalar_product_comparison10 (W D A d l u : ℝ)
    (hW : 0 < W) (hD : 0 < D) (hd : 0 < d)
    (hl : l*W ≤ D) (hu : D ≤ u*W)
    (hdet : d*D ≤ 3628800*A*W) (hm : A*(1024*W/d)/D ≤ 1048576) :
    d*l/3628800 ≤ A ∧ A ≤ 1024*d*u := by
  have hlo : (d*l)*W ≤ (3628800*A)*W := by
    calc
      (d*l)*W = d*(l*W) := by ring
      _ ≤ d*D := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hl hd.le
      _ ≤ (3628800*A)*W := hdet
  have hlc := (mul_le_mul_right hW).mp hlo
  have hm' := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right ((div_le_iff₀ hD).mp hm) hd.le
  have he : A*(1024*W/d)*d = (A*1024)*W := by field_simp; ring
  rw [he] at hm'
  have hhi : (A*1024)*W ≤ (1048576*d*u)*W := hm'.trans (by
    convert mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hu (show 0 ≤ 1048576*d by positivity) using 1 <;> ring)
  have hhc := (mul_le_mul_right hW).mp hhi
  constructor <;> nlinarith

theorem scalar_volume_ratio10 (W D d l u : ℝ) (hW : 0 < W) (hD : 0 < D)
    (hd : 0 < d) (hl : 0 < l) (hu : 0 < u) (hlo : l*W ≤ D) (hhi : D ≤ u*W) :
    1024/(d*u) ≤ (1024*W/d)/D ∧ (1024*W/d)/D ≤ 1024/(d*l) := by
  have hv : 0 ≤ 1024*W/d := by positivity
  constructor
  · calc
      _ = (1024*W/d)/(u*W) := by field_simp; ring
      _ ≤ _ := div_le_div_of_nonneg_left hv hD hhi
  · calc
      _ ≤ (1024*W/d)/(l*W) := div_le_div_of_nonneg_left hv (mul_pos hl hW) hlo
      _ = _ := by field_simp; ring

theorem sourceMinima10_product_volume_upper (A₂ A₃ : Matrix (Fin 10) (Fin 10) ℚ)
    (L : Matrix (Fin 10) (Fin 10) ℝ) (hL : IsUnit L)
    (Q : ℝ) (hQ : 1 < Q) (c₀ c₂ c₃ : Fin 10 → ℝ) :
    (∏ i, sourceMinima10 A₂ A₃ L hL Q hQ c₀ c₂ c₃ i) * volume.real (originalBody10 L Q c₀) /
      ZLattice.covolume (integerRealImage (originalSourceGroup10 A₂ A₃ Q c₂ c₃)) ≤ (4 : ℝ)^10 := by
  let b := sourceRealBasis10 A₂ A₃ (fun i => Nat.ceil (JSP000334.qThreshold 2 Q (c₂ i)))
    (fun i => Nat.ceil (JSP000334.qThreshold 3 Q (c₃ i)))
  have hu := successive_minima10_product_volume_upper b (originalCoordinates10 L hL Q hQ c₀)
  have hbody : {x | coordinateGauge10 (originalCoordinates10 L hL Q hQ c₀) x ≤ 1} = originalBody10 L Q c₀ := by
    ext x
    exact (originalBody10_mem_iff L hL Q hQ c₀ x).symm
  have heq : sourceMinima10 A₂ A₃ L hL Q hQ c₀ c₂ c₃ = successiveRadius10 b (originalCoordinates10 L hL Q hQ c₀) :=
    funext (sourceMinima10_eq_radius A₂ A₃ L hL Q hQ c₀ c₂ c₃)
  rw [← heq,hbody,sourceRealBasis10_span] at hu
  exact hu

theorem source_original_uniform_minima_two_sided10
    (A₂ A₃ : Matrix (Fin 10) (Fin 10) ℚ) (L : Matrix (Fin 10) (Fin 10) ℝ)
    (h₂ : IsUnit A₂) (h₃ : IsUnit A₃) (hL : IsUnit L) :
    ∃ c10 C10 : ℝ, 0 < c10 ∧ 0 < C10 ∧
      ∀ (Q : ℝ) (hQ : 1 < Q) (c₀ c₂ c₃ : Fin 10 → ℝ),
        (∀ i, c₂ i ≤ 0) → (∀ i, c₃ i ≤ 0) → (∑ i, (c₀ i+c₂ i+c₃ i) = 0) →
        c10 ≤ (∏ i, sourceMinima10 A₂ A₃ L hL Q hQ c₀ c₂ c₃ i) ∧
          (∏ i, sourceMinima10 A₂ A₃ L hL Q hQ c₀ c₂ c₃ i) ≤ C10 := by
  obtain ⟨K,hK,hupper⟩ := sourceLattice10_uniform_normalized_covolume_upper A₂ A₃ h₂ h₃
  have hd : 0 < |L.det| := abs_pos.mpr (isUnit_iff_ne_zero.mp ((Matrix.isUnit_iff_isUnit_det _).mp hL))
  have hl := sourceLocalDetFactor10_pos A₂ A₃ h₂ h₃
  refine ⟨|L.det| * sourceLocalDetFactor10 A₂ A₃/3628800,1024*|L.det| * ((K : ℝ)*60466176),by positivity,by positivity,?_⟩
  intro Q hQ c₀ c₂ c₃ hc₂ hc₃ hb
  let G := originalSourceGroup10 A₂ A₃ Q c₂ c₃
  let W := ∏ i, Q^c₀ i
  have hW : 0 < W := Finset.prod_pos (fun i _ => Real.rpow_pos_of_pos (by linarith) _)
  have hD : 0 < ZLattice.covolume (integerRealImage G) := ZLattice.covolume_pos _ volume
  obtain ⟨u,hu,hpos,hmono,hatt,hpoint⟩ := exists_sourceMinima10_attaining A₂ A₃ L hL Q hQ c₀ c₂ c₃
  obtain ⟨c⟩ := sourceLattice10_has_basis A₂ A₃
    (fun i => Nat.ceil (JSP000334.qThreshold 2 Q (c₂ i)))
    (fun i => Nat.ceil (JSP000334.qThreshold 3 Q (c₃ i)))
  have hdet := integerRealImage_frame_product_lower G c L u hu (fun i => Q^c₀ i)
    (sourceMinima10 A₂ A₃ L hL Q hQ c₀ c₂ c₃) hpoint
  have hcovlo := sourceLattice10_covolume_width_lower A₂ A₃ Q hQ c₀ c₂ c₃ hb
  have hcovhi := (div_le_iff₀ hW).mp (hupper Q hQ c₀ c₂ c₃ hc₂ hc₃ hb)
  have hm := sourceMinima10_product_volume_upper A₂ A₃ L hL Q hQ c₀ c₂ c₃
  rw [originalBody10_volume L hL Q hQ] at hm
  exact scalar_product_comparison10 W _ _ |L.det| (sourceLocalDetFactor10 A₂ A₃) ((K : ℝ)*60466176)
    hW hD hd hcovlo hcovhi (by simpa using hdet) (by norm_num only [show (4 : ℝ)^10 = 1048576 by norm_num] at hm; exact hm)

theorem sourceLattice10_full_rank (A₂ A₃ : Matrix (Fin 10) (Fin 10) ℚ)
    (e₂ e₃ : Fin 10 → ℕ) : Module.finrank ℤ (sourceLattice10 A₂ A₃ e₂ e₃) = 10 := by
  obtain ⟨c⟩ := sourceLattice10_has_basis A₂ A₃ e₂ e₃
  simpa using Module.finrank_eq_card_basis c

theorem sourceLattice10_index_pos (A₂ A₃ : Matrix (Fin 10) (Fin 10) ℚ)
    (e₂ e₃ : Fin 10 → ℕ) : 0 < (sourceLattice10 A₂ A₃ e₂ e₃).index := by
  have h := ZLattice.covolume_pos (integerRealImage (sourceLattice10 A₂ A₃ e₂ e₃)) volume
  rw [sourceLattice10_covolume_index] at h
  exact_mod_cast h

theorem source_original_uniform_volume_ratio10
    (A₂ A₃ : Matrix (Fin 10) (Fin 10) ℚ) (L : Matrix (Fin 10) (Fin 10) ℝ)
    (h₂ : IsUnit A₂) (h₃ : IsUnit A₃) (hL : IsUnit L) :
    ∃ a b : ℝ, 0 < a ∧ 0 < b ∧
      ∀ (Q : ℝ) (hQ : 1 < Q) (c₀ c₂ c₃ : Fin 10 → ℝ),
        (∀ i, c₂ i ≤ 0) → (∀ i, c₃ i ≤ 0) → (∑ i, (c₀ i+c₂ i+c₃ i) = 0) →
        a ≤ volume.real (originalBody10 L Q c₀) /
          ZLattice.covolume (integerRealImage (originalSourceGroup10 A₂ A₃ Q c₂ c₃)) ∧
        volume.real (originalBody10 L Q c₀) /
          ZLattice.covolume (integerRealImage (originalSourceGroup10 A₂ A₃ Q c₂ c₃)) ≤ b := by
  obtain ⟨K,hK,hupper⟩ := sourceLattice10_uniform_normalized_covolume_upper A₂ A₃ h₂ h₃
  have hd : 0 < |L.det| := abs_pos.mpr (isUnit_iff_ne_zero.mp ((Matrix.isUnit_iff_isUnit_det _).mp hL))
  have hl := sourceLocalDetFactor10_pos A₂ A₃ h₂ h₃
  refine ⟨1024/(|L.det| * ((K : ℝ)*60466176)),1024/(|L.det| * sourceLocalDetFactor10 A₂ A₃),
    by positivity,by positivity,?_⟩
  intro Q hQ c₀ c₂ c₃ hc₂ hc₃ hb
  let W := ∏ i, Q^c₀ i
  have hW : 0 < W := Finset.prod_pos (fun i _ => Real.rpow_pos_of_pos (by linarith) _)
  have hD := ZLattice.covolume_pos (integerRealImage (originalSourceGroup10 A₂ A₃ Q c₂ c₃)) volume
  rw [originalBody10_volume L hL Q hQ]
  exact scalar_volume_ratio10 W _ |L.det| (sourceLocalDetFactor10 A₂ A₃) ((K : ℝ)*60466176)
    hW hD hd hl (by positivity)
    (sourceLattice10_covolume_width_lower A₂ A₃ Q hQ c₀ c₂ c₃ hb)
    ((div_le_iff₀ hW).mp (hupper Q hQ c₀ c₂ c₃ hc₂ hc₃ hb))

end
end CompoundFeasibility

end M197Literal

namespace M197Literal
open scoped BigOperators Matrix
namespace CompoundFeasibility
noncomputable section

def parallelepipedBody10 (e : (Fin 10 → ℝ) ≃ₗ[ℝ] (Fin 10 → ℝ)) : Set (Fin 10 → ℝ) :=
  e ⁻¹' Set.pi Set.univ (fun _ : Fin 10 => Set.Icc (-1 : ℝ) 1)

theorem mem_parallelepipedBody10_iff (e : (Fin 10 → ℝ) ≃ₗ[ℝ] (Fin 10 → ℝ)) (x : (Fin 10 → ℝ)) :
    x ∈ parallelepipedBody10 e ↔ coordinateGauge10 e x ≤ 1 := by
  simp [parallelepipedBody10, coordinateGauge10, pi_norm_le_iff_of_nonempty,
    Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_le, Pi.le_def, forall_and]

open scoped Pointwise in
theorem mem_dilate_parallelepipedBody10_iff (e : (Fin 10 → ℝ) ≃ₗ[ℝ] (Fin 10 → ℝ))
    (x : (Fin 10 → ℝ)) (r : ℝ) (hr : 0 < r) :
    x ∈ r • parallelepipedBody10 e ↔ coordinateGauge10 e x ≤ r := by
  rw [Set.mem_smul_set_iff_inv_smul_mem₀ hr.ne', mem_parallelepipedBody10_iff]
  simp only [coordinateGauge10, map_smul, norm_smul, Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_inv,
    abs_of_pos hr]
  simpa using (inv_mul_le_iff₀ hr : r⁻¹ * ‖e x‖ ≤ 1 ↔ ‖e x‖ ≤ r * 1)

theorem parallelepipedBody10_volume (e : (Fin 10 → ℝ) ≃ₗ[ℝ] (Fin 10 → ℝ)) :
    MeasureTheory.volume.real (parallelepipedBody10 e) =
      1024 / |LinearMap.det e.toLinearMap| := by
  rw [MeasureTheory.measureReal_def, parallelepipedBody10,
    MeasureTheory.Measure.addHaar_preimage_linearEquiv,
    MeasureTheory.volume_pi, MeasureTheory.Measure.pi_pi]
  simp only [Real.volume_Icc, LinearEquiv.det_coe_symm, abs_inv,
    ENNReal.toReal_mul, ENNReal.toReal_ofReal, abs_nonneg, div_eq_mul_inv]
  norm_num
  ring

theorem successiveRadius10_le_family (b : Basis (Fin 10) ℝ (Fin 10 → ℝ))
    (e : (Fin 10 → ℝ) ≃ₗ[ℝ] (Fin 10 → ℝ)) (k : Fin 10)
    (u : Fin (k.val + 1) → (Fin 10 → ℝ)) (hu : LinearIndependent ℝ u)
    (huL : ∀ i, u i ∈ lattice10Span b) (r : ℝ)
    (hr : ∀ i, coordinateGauge10 e (u i) ≤ r) :
    successiveRadius10 b e k ≤ r := by
  unfold successiveRadius10
  apply csInf_le (s := {s : ℝ | ∃ w : Fin (k.val + 1) → (Fin 10 → ℝ),
    LinearIndependent ℝ w ∧ (∀ i, w i ∈ lattice10Span b) ∧
      ∀ i, coordinateGauge10 e (w i) ≤ s}) _ ⟨u, hu, huL, hr⟩
  refine ⟨0, ?_⟩
  rintro s ⟨w, _, _, hw⟩
  exact (norm_nonneg (e (w 0))).trans (hw 0)

theorem lattice10Span_short_mem_minimaFlag (b : Basis (Fin 10) ℝ (Fin 10 → ℝ))
    (e : (Fin 10 → ℝ) ≃ₗ[ℝ] (Fin 10 → ℝ)) (v : Fin 10 → (Fin 10 → ℝ))
    (hv : LinearIndependent ℝ v) (hvL : ∀ i, v i ∈ lattice10Span b)
    (hvr : ∀ i, coordinateGauge10 e (v i) = successiveRadius10 b e i)
    (k : Fin 10) (hgap : ∀ i : Fin 10, i < k →
      successiveRadius10 b e i < successiveRadius10 b e k)
    (x : (Fin 10 → ℝ)) (hx : x ∈ lattice10Span b)
    (hxr : coordinateGauge10 e x < successiveRadius10 b e k) :
    x ∈ minimaFlag10 v k := by
  classical
  by_contra hxW
  let u : Fin k.val → (Fin 10 → ℝ) := fun i => v (Fin.castLE (by omega) i)
  have hu : LinearIndependent ℝ u := hv.comp _ (Fin.castLE_injective _)
  let w : Fin (k.val + 1) → (Fin 10 → ℝ) := Fin.snoc u x
  have hux : LinearIndependent ℝ w :=
    linearIndependent_fin_snoc.mpr ⟨hu, hxW⟩
  have hL : ∀ i : Fin (k.val + 1), w i ∈ lattice10Span b := by
    intro i
    refine Fin.lastCases ?_ (fun j => ?_) i
    · simpa [w] using hx
    · simpa [w, u] using hvL (Fin.castLE (by omega) j)
  have hlt : ∀ i : Fin (k.val + 1), coordinateGauge10 e (w i) < successiveRadius10 b e k := by
    intro i
    refine Fin.lastCases ?_ (fun j => ?_) i
    · simpa [w] using hxr
    · simpa [w, u, hvr] using hgap (Fin.castLE (by omega) j) (by exact j.isLt)
  obtain ⟨i, _, hi⟩ := Finset.exists_max_image Finset.univ
    (fun i => coordinateGauge10 e (w i)) (Finset.univ_nonempty)
  have hle := successiveRadius10_le_family b e k w hux hL _
    (fun j => hi j (Finset.mem_univ j))
  exact (not_lt_of_ge hle) (hlt i)

theorem davenport_original_off_flag10 (b : Basis (Fin 10) ℝ (Fin 10 → ℝ))
    (e : (Fin 10 → ℝ) ≃ₗ[ℝ] (Fin 10 → ℝ)) (v : Fin 10 → (Fin 10 → ℝ))
    (hv : LinearIndependent ℝ v) (hvL : ∀ i, v i ∈ lattice10Span b)
    (hvr : ∀ i, coordinateGauge10 e (v i) = successiveRadius10 b e i)
    (j : Fin 10) (x : Fin 10 → ℝ) (hx : x ∈ lattice10Span b)
    (houtside : x ∉ minimaFlag10 v j) : successiveRadius10 b e j ≤ coordinateGauge10 e x := by
  classical
  obtain ⟨_, _, _, _, hm, _⟩ := exists_successive_minima10 b e
  let s := Finset.univ.filter (fun i => successiveRadius10 b e i = successiveRadius10 b e j)
  have hjs : j ∈ s := by simp [s]
  obtain ⟨r, hrs, hmin⟩ := Finset.exists_min_image s Fin.val ⟨j, hjs⟩
  have hr : successiveRadius10 b e r = successiveRadius10 b e j := (Finset.mem_filter.mp hrs).2
  have hrj : r ≤ j := hmin j hjs
  have hgap : ∀ i : Fin 10, i < r → successiveRadius10 b e i < successiveRadius10 b e r := by
    intro i hir
    have hle := hm (le_of_lt hir)
    apply lt_of_le_of_ne hle
    intro heq
    have his : i ∈ s := by simp [s, heq, hr]
    exact (not_le_of_gt hir) (hmin i his)
  by_contra h
  have hshort : coordinateGauge10 e x < successiveRadius10 b e r := by
    rw [hr]
    exact lt_of_not_ge h
  exact houtside (minimaFlag10_mono v _ _ hrj
    (lattice10Span_short_mem_minimaFlag b e v hv hvL hvr r hgap x hx hshort))

theorem davenport_pivot_step10 (k : ℕ) (W V : Submodule ℝ (Fin 10 → ℝ))
    (hW : Module.finrank ℝ W = k) (hV : Module.finrank ℝ V = k+1) (hWV : W ≤ V)
    (p : Fin k → Fin 10) (C : ℝ)
    (hbound : ∀ x ∈ W, ∀ M : ℝ, 0 ≤ M → (∀ i, |x (p i)| ≤ M) → ‖x‖ ≤ C*M) :
    ∃ a : Fin 10, a ∉ Set.range p ∧
      ∀ x ∈ V, ∀ M : ℝ, 0 ≤ M → (∀ i, |x (p i)| ≤ M) → |x a| ≤ M →
        ‖x‖ ≤ (2*C+1)*M := by
  classical
  let f : V →ₗ[ℝ] (Fin k → ℝ) :=
    { toFun := fun x i => x.val (p i)
      map_add' := fun _ _ => rfl
      map_smul' := fun _ _ => rfl }
  have hdim : Module.finrank ℝ (Fin k → ℝ) < Module.finrank ℝ V := by
    rw [hV]
    simp
  obtain ⟨z, hz, hzne⟩ := Submodule.exists_mem_ne_zero_of_ne_bot
    (LinearMap.ker_ne_bot_of_finrank_lt (f := f) hdim)
  let y : Fin 10 → ℝ := z.val
  have hyV : y ∈ V := z.property
  have hyne : y ≠ 0 := by
    intro h
    apply hzne
    exact Subtype.ext h
  have hyp (i : Fin k) : y (p i) = 0 := congrFun hz i
  have hyW : y ∉ W := by
    intro h
    have hzbound : ∀ i, |y (p i)| ≤ (0 : ℝ) := by
      intro i
      exact le_of_eq ((congrArg abs (hyp i)).trans (abs_zero))
    have hb : ‖y‖ ≤ C * 0 := hbound y h 0 (le_refl 0) hzbound
    have hn : ‖y‖ = 0 := le_antisymm (by simpa using hb) (norm_nonneg _)
    exact hyne (norm_eq_zero.mp hn)
  obtain ⟨a, _, ha⟩ := Finset.exists_max_image Finset.univ (fun i => |y i|) Finset.univ_nonempty
  have hnorm : ‖y‖ = |y a| := by
    apply le_antisymm
    · apply (pi_norm_le_iff_of_nonempty y).mpr
      intro i
      simpa only [Real.norm_eq_abs] using ha i (Finset.mem_univ i)
    · simpa only [Real.norm_eq_abs] using norm_le_pi_norm y a
  have haout : a ∉ Set.range p := by
    rintro ⟨i, hi⟩
    have hay : y a = 0 := hi ▸ hyp i
    exact hyne (norm_eq_zero.mp (by rw [hnorm, hay, abs_zero]))
  have hsupV : W ⊔ Submodule.span ℝ {y} ≤ V :=
    sup_le hWV (Submodule.span_le.mpr (by simpa only [Set.singleton_subset_iff] using hyV))
  have hstrict : W < W ⊔ Submodule.span ℝ {y} := by
    apply lt_of_le_of_ne le_sup_left
    intro heq
    apply hyW
    rw [heq]
    exact (show Submodule.span ℝ {y} ≤ W ⊔ Submodule.span ℝ {y} from le_sup_right)
      (Submodule.subset_span (Set.mem_singleton y))
  have hsup : W ⊔ Submodule.span ℝ {y} = V := by
    apply Submodule.eq_of_le_of_finrank_eq hsupV
    have hl := Submodule.finrank_lt_finrank_of_lt hstrict
    have hu := Submodule.finrank_mono hsupV
    omega
  refine ⟨a, haout, ?_⟩
  intro x hx M hM hp hxa
  have hxu : x ∈ W ⊔ Submodule.span ℝ {y} := hsup.symm ▸ hx
  obtain ⟨w, hw, t, ht, he⟩ := Submodule.mem_sup.mp hxu
  obtain ⟨c, rfl⟩ := Submodule.mem_span_singleton.mp ht
  have hwp (i : Fin k) : w (p i) = x (p i) := by
    have hh := congrFun he (p i)
    simpa only [Pi.add_apply, Pi.smul_apply, smul_eq_mul, hyp i, mul_zero, add_zero] using hh
  have hwb : ‖w‖ ≤ C*M := hbound w hw M hM (by intro i; rw [hwp]; exact hp i)
  have hrel : c*y a = x a - w a := by
    have hh := congrFun he a
    change w a + c*y a = x a at hh
    linarith
  have hca : |c| * ‖y‖ ≤ M+‖w‖ := by
    calc
      |c| * ‖y‖ = |c*y a| := by rw [hnorm, abs_mul]
      _ = |x a-w a| := by rw [hrel]
      _ ≤ |x a|+|w a| := abs_sub _ _
      _ ≤ M+‖w‖ := add_le_add hxa (by simpa using norm_le_pi_norm w a)
  have hn : ‖x‖ ≤ ‖w‖+|c| * ‖y‖ := by
    rw [← he]
    simpa only [norm_smul, Real.norm_eq_abs] using norm_add_le w (c • y)
  nlinarith

def prefixSpan10 (v : Fin 10 → (Fin 10 → ℝ)) (k : ℕ) (hk : k ≤ 10) :
    Submodule ℝ (Fin 10 → ℝ) :=
  Submodule.span ℝ (Set.range (fun i : Fin k => v (Fin.castLE hk i)))

theorem prefixSpan10_finrank (v : Fin 10 → (Fin 10 → ℝ)) (hv : LinearIndependent ℝ v)
    (k : ℕ) (hk : k ≤ 10) : Module.finrank ℝ (prefixSpan10 v k hk) = k := by
  simpa only [prefixSpan10, Fintype.card_fin] using
    finrank_span_eq_card (hv.comp (Fin.castLE hk) (Fin.castLE_injective _))

theorem prefixSpan10_mono (v : Fin 10 → (Fin 10 → ℝ)) {i j : ℕ}
    (hi : i ≤ 10) (hj : j ≤ 10) (hij : i ≤ j) : prefixSpan10 v i hi ≤ prefixSpan10 v j hj := by
  apply Submodule.span_mono
  rintro _ ⟨k, rfl⟩
  exact ⟨Fin.castLE hij k, rfl⟩

theorem davenport_pivot_prefix10 (v : Fin 10 → (Fin 10 → ℝ)) (hv : LinearIndependent ℝ v)
    (k : ℕ) (hk : k ≤ 10) :
    ∃ p : Fin k → Fin 10, Function.Injective p ∧
      ∀ (j : ℕ) (hj : j ≤ k), ∀ x ∈ prefixSpan10 v j (hj.trans hk), ∀ M : ℝ, 0 ≤ M →
        (∀ i : Fin j, |x (p (Fin.castLE hj i))| ≤ M) → ‖x‖ ≤ ((2 : ℝ)^j-1)*M := by
  induction k with
  | zero =>
    refine ⟨Fin.elim0, ?_, ?_⟩
    · intro i
      exact Fin.elim0 i
    · intro j hj x hx M hM hcoords
      have hj0 : j = 0 := by omega
      subst j
      have hx0 : x = 0 := by simpa [prefixSpan10] using hx
      simp [hx0]
  | succ k ih =>
    have hk0 : k ≤ 10 := by omega
    obtain ⟨p, hp, hb⟩ := ih hk0
    have hbound : ∀ x ∈ prefixSpan10 v k hk0, ∀ M : ℝ, 0 ≤ M →
        (∀ i, |x (p i)| ≤ M) → ‖x‖ ≤ ((2 : ℝ)^k-1)*M := by
      intro x hx M hM hc
      exact hb k le_rfl x hx M hM hc
    obtain ⟨a, ha, hab⟩ := davenport_pivot_step10 k
      (prefixSpan10 v k hk0) (prefixSpan10 v (k+1) hk)
      (prefixSpan10_finrank v hv k hk0) (prefixSpan10_finrank v hv (k+1) hk)
      (prefixSpan10_mono v hk0 hk (Nat.le_succ k)) p ((2 : ℝ)^k-1) hbound
    let q : Fin (k+1) → Fin 10 := Fin.snoc p a
    have hq : Function.Injective q := by
      intro i j
      refine Fin.lastCases ?_ (fun i' => ?_) i
      · refine Fin.lastCases ?_ (fun j' => ?_) j
        · intro _
          rfl
        · intro h
          exact False.elim (ha ⟨j', (by simpa [q] using h.symm)⟩)
      · refine Fin.lastCases ?_ (fun j' => ?_) j
        · intro h
          exact False.elim (ha ⟨i', (by simpa [q] using h)⟩)
        · intro h
          exact congrArg Fin.castSucc (hp (by simpa [q] using h))
    refine ⟨q, hq, ?_⟩
    intro j hj x hx M hM hc
    by_cases hjk : j ≤ k
    · apply hb j hjk x hx M hM
      intro i
      have he : q (Fin.castLE hj i) = p (Fin.castLE hjk i) := by
        have heq : (Fin.castLE hj i : Fin (k+1)) = (Fin.castLE hjk i).castSucc := rfl
        rw [heq]
        simp only [q, Fin.snoc_castSucc]
      rw [← he]
      exact hc i
    · have hj' : j = k+1 := by omega
      subst j
      have hold (i : Fin k) : |x (p i)| ≤ M := by
        simpa only [Fin.castLE_rfl, id_eq, q, Fin.snoc_castSucc] using hc i.castSucc
      have hnew : |x a| ≤ M := by
        simpa only [Fin.castLE_rfl, id_eq, q, Fin.snoc_last] using hc (Fin.last k)
      have hn := hab x hx M hM hold hnew
      have hC : (2 * ((2 : ℝ)^k-1)+1)*M = ((2 : ℝ)^(k+1)-1)*M := by
        rw [pow_succ]
        ring
      rwa [hC] at hn

theorem prefixSpan10_map (e : (Fin 10 → ℝ) ≃ₗ[ℝ] (Fin 10 → ℝ))
    (v : Fin 10 → (Fin 10 → ℝ)) (k : ℕ) (hk : k ≤ 10) (x : Fin 10 → ℝ)
    (hx : x ∈ prefixSpan10 v k hk) : e x ∈ prefixSpan10 (e ∘ v) k hk := by
  have hs : (prefixSpan10 v k hk).map e.toLinearMap = prefixSpan10 (e ∘ v) k hk := by
    simp only [prefixSpan10, Submodule.map_span, ← Set.range_comp, Function.comp_def]
    rfl
  rw [← hs]
  exact Submodule.mem_map.mpr ⟨x, hx, rfl⟩

theorem davenport_flag_pivot10 (v : Fin 10 → (Fin 10 → ℝ)) (hv : LinearIndependent ℝ v) :
    ∃ p : Equiv.Perm (Fin 10), ∀ j : Fin 10,
      ∀ x ∈ prefixSpan10 v (j.val+1) (by omega), ∀ M : ℝ, 0 ≤ M →
        (∀ i : Fin (j.val+1), |x (p (Fin.castLE (by omega) i))| ≤ M) → ‖x‖ ≤ 1024*M := by
  obtain ⟨p, hp, hb⟩ := davenport_pivot_prefix10 v hv 10 le_rfl
  let q : Equiv.Perm (Fin 10) := Equiv.ofBijective p (Finite.injective_iff_bijective.mp hp)
  refine ⟨q, ?_⟩
  intro j x hx M hM hc
  have hn := hb (j.val+1) (by omega) x hx M hM hc
  have hC : (2 : ℝ)^(j.val+1)-1 ≤ 1024 := by fin_cases j <;> norm_num
  exact hn.trans (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hC hM)

noncomputable def davenportCoordinates10 (e : (Fin 10 → ℝ) ≃ₗ[ℝ] (Fin 10 → ℝ))
    (deform : Fin 10 → ℝ) (sigma : Equiv.Perm (Fin 10)) (hd : ∀ i, 0 < deform i) :
    (Fin 10 → ℝ) ≃ₗ[ℝ] (Fin 10 → ℝ) :=
  weightedCoordinates10 e (fun i => (deform (sigma i))⁻¹) (fun i => inv_pos.mpr (hd _))

theorem davenportCoordinates10_apply (e : (Fin 10 → ℝ) ≃ₗ[ℝ] (Fin 10 → ℝ))
    (deform : Fin 10 → ℝ) (sigma : Equiv.Perm (Fin 10)) (hd : ∀ i, 0 < deform i)
    (x : Fin 10 → ℝ) (i : Fin 10) :
    davenportCoordinates10 e deform sigma hd x i = deform (sigma i) * e x i := by
  change e x i / (deform (sigma i))⁻¹ = _
  rw [div_inv_eq_mul, mul_comm]

theorem davenport_off_flag10_of_pivot (b : Basis (Fin 10) ℝ (Fin 10 → ℝ))
    (e : (Fin 10 → ℝ) ≃ₗ[ℝ] (Fin 10 → ℝ)) (v : Fin 10 → (Fin 10 → ℝ))
    (hv : LinearIndependent ℝ v) (hvL : ∀ i, v i ∈ lattice10Span b)
    (hvr : ∀ i, coordinateGauge10 e (v i) = successiveRadius10 b e i)
    (deform : Fin 10 → ℝ) (hd : ∀ i, 0 < deform i) (hanti : Antitone deform)
    (hscaled : Monotone (fun i => deform i * successiveRadius10 b e i))
    (p : Equiv.Perm (Fin 10))
    (hp : ∀ j : Fin 10, ∀ x ∈ prefixSpan10 (e ∘ v) (j.val+1) (by omega), ∀ M : ℝ, 0 ≤ M →
      (∀ i : Fin (j.val+1), |x (p (Fin.castLE (by omega) i))| ≤ M) → ‖x‖ ≤ 1024*M) :
    ∀ j : Fin 10, ∀ x ∈ lattice10Span b, x ∉ minimaFlag10 v j →
      deform j * successiveRadius10 b e j / 1024 ≤
        coordinateGauge10 (davenportCoordinates10 e deform p.symm hd) x := by
  classical
  intro j x hx hout
  let g := coordinateGauge10 (davenportCoordinates10 e deform p.symm hd) x
  have hg : 0 ≤ g := norm_nonneg _
  have hc (i : Fin 10) : deform i * |e x (p i)| ≤ g := by
    have hn := norm_le_pi_norm (davenportCoordinates10 e deform p.symm hd x) (p i)
    simpa only [Real.norm_eq_abs, davenportCoordinates10_apply, p.symm_apply_apply,
      abs_mul, abs_of_pos (hd i)] using hn
  let S := Finset.univ.filter (fun k : Fin 10 => x ∉ minimaFlag10 v k)
  have hjs : j ∈ S := by simp [S, hout]
  obtain ⟨k, hkS, hmax⟩ := Finset.exists_max_image S Fin.val ⟨j, hjs⟩
  have hkout : x ∉ minimaFlag10 v k := (Finset.mem_filter.mp hkS).2
  have hjk : j ≤ k := hmax j hjs
  have hxpre : x ∈ prefixSpan10 v (k.val+1) (by omega) := by
    by_cases hk : k.val+1 < 10
    · by_contra hnot
      let l : Fin 10 := ⟨k.val+1, hk⟩
      have hlS : l ∈ S := by
        refine Finset.mem_filter.mpr ⟨Finset.mem_univ _, ?_⟩
        exact hnot
      have hle := hmax l hlS
      change k.val+1 ≤ k.val at hle
      omega
    · have htop : prefixSpan10 v (k.val+1) (by omega) = ⊤ := by
        apply Submodule.eq_top_of_finrank_eq
        rw [prefixSpan10_finrank v hv]
        simp only [Module.finrank_pi, Module.finrank_self, Fintype.card_fin, mul_one]
        omega
      rw [htop]
      exact Submodule.mem_top
  have hk10 : k.val+1 ≤ 10 := by omega
  let f : Fin (k.val+1) → Fin 10 := Fin.castLE hk10
  have hcoord (i : Fin (k.val+1)) : |e x (p (f i))| ≤ g / deform k := by
    apply (le_div_iff₀ (hd k)).mpr
    let l : Fin 10 := f i
    have hl : l ≤ k := by change i.val ≤ k.val; omega
    have hweight := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right (hanti hl) (abs_nonneg (e x (p l)))
    simpa only [mul_comm] using hweight.trans (hc l)
  have hnorm := hp k (e x) (prefixSpan10_map e v _ _ x hxpre) (g / deform k)
    (div_nonneg hg (hd k).le) hcoord
  have hlower := davenport_original_off_flag10 b e v hv hvL hvr k x hx hkout
  have hmul := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (hlower.trans hnorm) (hd k).le
  have hcancel : deform k * (1024 * (g / deform k)) = 1024*g := by
    field_simp [(hd k).ne']
  rw [hcancel] at hmul
  have hmono := hscaled hjk
  change deform j * successiveRadius10 b e j / 1024 ≤ g
  apply (div_le_iff₀ (by norm_num : (0 : ℝ) < 1024)).mpr
  exact hmono.trans (hmul.trans_eq (mul_comm 1024 g))

theorem davenportCoordinates10_det (e : (Fin 10 → ℝ) ≃ₗ[ℝ] (Fin 10 → ℝ))
    (deform : Fin 10 → ℝ) (sigma : Equiv.Perm (Fin 10)) (hd : ∀ i, 0 < deform i)
    (hprod : (∏ i, deform i) = 1) :
    LinearMap.det (davenportCoordinates10 e deform sigma hd).toLinearMap = LinearMap.det e.toLinearMap := by
  rw [davenportCoordinates10, weightedCoordinates10_det, Finset.prod_inv_distrib,
    Equiv.prod_comp, hprod, inv_one, div_one]

theorem davenportBody10_volume (e : (Fin 10 → ℝ) ≃ₗ[ℝ] (Fin 10 → ℝ))
    (deform : Fin 10 → ℝ) (sigma : Equiv.Perm (Fin 10)) (hd : ∀ i, 0 < deform i)
    (hprod : (∏ i, deform i) = 1) :
    MeasureTheory.volume.real (parallelepipedBody10 (davenportCoordinates10 e deform sigma hd)) =
      MeasureTheory.volume.real (parallelepipedBody10 e) := by
  rw [parallelepipedBody10_volume, parallelepipedBody10_volume, davenportCoordinates10_det e deform sigma hd hprod]

theorem davenport_minima_lower10 (b : Basis (Fin 10) ℝ (Fin 10 → ℝ))
    (e' : (Fin 10 → ℝ) ≃ₗ[ℝ] (Fin 10 → ℝ)) (v : Fin 10 → (Fin 10 → ℝ))
    (hv : LinearIndependent ℝ v) (s : Fin 10 → ℝ)
    (hsep : ∀ j, ∀ x ∈ lattice10Span b, x ∉ minimaFlag10 v j → s j ≤ coordinateGauge10 e' x)
    (j : Fin 10) : s j ≤ successiveRadius10 b e' j := by
  obtain ⟨u, hu, huL, _, hm, hur, _⟩ := exists_successive_minima10 b e'
  let f : Fin (j.val+1) → Fin 10 := Fin.castLE (by omega)
  have hf : Function.Injective f := Fin.castLE_injective _
  obtain ⟨i, hi⟩ := independent_family_escapes_subspace10 (u ∘ f) (hu.comp f hf)
    (minimaFlag10 v j) (by rw [minimaFlag10_finrank v hv j]; omega)
  have hh := hsep j (u (f i)) (huL (f i)) hi
  rw [hur] at hh
  exact hh.trans (hm (show f i ≤ j from (by change i.val ≤ j.val; omega)))

theorem davenport_adapted_rows10 (v : Fin 10 → (Fin 10 → ℝ)) (hv : LinearIndependent ℝ v) :
    ∃ p : Equiv.Perm (Fin 10), ∃ y : Fin 10 → (Fin 10 → ℝ),
      (∀ j, v j - y j ∈ minimaFlag10 v j) ∧
      (∀ j i : Fin 10, i < j → y j (p i) = 0) ∧
      (∀ j : Fin 10, ‖y j‖ ≤ (2 : ℝ)^j.val * ‖v j‖) ∧
      ∀ j : Fin 10, ∀ x ∈ prefixSpan10 v (j.val+1) (by omega), ∀ M : ℝ, 0 ≤ M →
        (∀ i : Fin (j.val+1), |x (p (Fin.castLE (by omega) i))| ≤ M) → ‖x‖ ≤ 1024*M := by
  classical
  obtain ⟨p, hp, hb⟩ := davenport_pivot_prefix10 v hv 10 le_rfl
  let q : Equiv.Perm (Fin 10) := Equiv.ofBijective p (Finite.injective_iff_bijective.mp hp)
  have hex (j : Fin 10) : ∃ w : minimaFlag10 v j,
      (∀ i : Fin j.val, w.val (q (Fin.castLE (by omega) i)) = v j (q (Fin.castLE (by omega) i))) ∧
      ‖w.val‖ ≤ ((2 : ℝ)^j.val-1)*‖v j‖ := by
    have hj10 : j.val ≤ 10 := Nat.le_of_lt j.isLt
    let f : minimaFlag10 v j →ₗ[ℝ] (Fin j.val → ℝ) :=
      { toFun := fun x i => x.val (q (Fin.castLE hj10 i))
        map_add' := fun _ _ => rfl
        map_smul' := fun _ _ => rfl }
    have hf : Function.Injective f := by
      intro x1 x2 he
      apply Subtype.ext
      apply sub_eq_zero.mp
      apply norm_eq_zero.mp
      apply le_antisymm ?_ (norm_nonneg _)
      have hxW : x1.val-x2.val ∈ prefixSpan10 v j.val hj10 :=
        (minimaFlag10 v j).sub_mem x1.property x2.property
      have hzero (i : Fin j.val) : |(x1.val-x2.val) (p (Fin.castLE hj10 i))| ≤ (0 : ℝ) := by
        have hi := congrFun he i
        change x1.val (p (Fin.castLE hj10 i)) = x2.val (p (Fin.castLE hj10 i)) at hi
        change |x1.val (p (Fin.castLE hj10 i))-x2.val (p (Fin.castLE hj10 i))| ≤ (0 : ℝ)
        exact le_of_eq ((congrArg abs (sub_eq_zero.mpr hi)).trans abs_zero)
      have hn := hb j.val hj10 (x1.val-x2.val) hxW 0 le_rfl hzero
      simpa using hn
    have hdim : Module.finrank ℝ (minimaFlag10 v j) = Module.finrank ℝ (Fin j.val → ℝ) := by
      rw [minimaFlag10_finrank v hv j]
      simp
    have hsurj := (LinearMap.injective_iff_surjective_of_finrank_eq_finrank hdim (f := f)).mp hf
    obtain ⟨w, hw⟩ := hsurj (fun i => v j (q (Fin.castLE hj10 i)))
    have hcoords (i : Fin j.val) : w.val (q (Fin.castLE hj10 i)) = v j (q (Fin.castLE hj10 i)) :=
      congrFun hw i
    refine ⟨w, hcoords, ?_⟩
    apply hb j.val hj10 w.val w.property ‖v j‖ (norm_nonneg _)
    intro i
    rw [show p (Fin.castLE hj10 i) = q (Fin.castLE hj10 i) from rfl, hcoords]
    simpa only [Real.norm_eq_abs] using norm_le_pi_norm (v j) (q (Fin.castLE hj10 i))
  choose w hw hnorm using hex
  refine ⟨q, fun j => v j-(w j).val, ?_, ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
  · intro j
    have he : v j-(v j-(w j).val) = (w j).val := by abel
    rw [he]
    exact (w j).property
  · intro j i hij
    let t : Fin j.val := ⟨i.val, hij⟩
    have hi : (Fin.castLE (by omega) t : Fin 10) = i := Fin.ext rfl
    have hh := hw j t
    rw [hi] at hh
    change v j (q i)-(w j).val (q i) = 0
    exact sub_eq_zero.mpr hh.symm
  · intro j
    have hn := norm_sub_le (v j) (w j).val
    have hb := hnorm j
    nlinarith
  · intro j x hx M hM hc
    have hn := hb (j.val+1) (by omega) x hx M hM hc
    have hC : (2 : ℝ)^(j.val+1)-1 ≤ 1024 := by fin_cases j <;> norm_num
    exact hn.trans (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hC hM)

theorem independent_prefix_adjustment10 (v w : Fin 10 → (Fin 10 → ℝ))
    (hv : LinearIndependent ℝ v) (hw : ∀ j, w j-v j ∈ minimaFlag10 v j) :
    LinearIndependent ℝ w := by
  classical
  let b := basisOfLinearIndependentOfCardEqFinrank hv (by simp)
  have hb (i : Fin 10) : b i = v i :=
    congrFun (coe_basisOfLinearIndependentOfCardEqFinrank hv _) i
  let f := b.equivFun
  have hbase (j i : Fin 10) : f (v j) i = if j = i then 1 else 0 := by
    rw [← hb j]
    exact b.equivFun_self j i
  have hzero (j i : Fin 10) (hji : j ≤ i) (x : Fin 10 → ℝ) (hx : x ∈ minimaFlag10 v j) :
      f x i = 0 := by
    let g : (Fin 10 → ℝ) →ₗ[ℝ] ℝ := (LinearMap.proj i).comp f.toLinearMap
    have hs : minimaFlag10 v j ≤ LinearMap.ker g := by
      apply Submodule.span_le.mpr
      rintro _ ⟨k, rfl⟩
      change f (v (Fin.castLE (by omega) k)) i = 0
      rw [hbase]
      have hne : (Fin.castLE (by omega) k : Fin 10) ≠ i := by
        intro heq
        have he := congrArg Fin.val heq
        change k.val = i.val at he
        omega
      simp only [hne, if_false]
    exact hs hx
  have hcoeff (j i : Fin 10) (hji : j ≤ i) : f (w j) i = f (v j) i := by
    have hz := hzero j i hji (w j-v j) (hw j)
    have he : f (w j-v j) i = f (w j) i-f (v j) i := by
      rw [map_sub]
      rfl
    rw [he] at hz
    exact sub_eq_zero.mp hz
  let T : Matrix (Fin 10) (Fin 10) ℝ := fun j i => f (w j) i
  have hdiag (i : Fin 10) : T i i = 1 := by
    change f (w i) i = 1
    rw [hcoeff i i le_rfl, hbase]
    simp
  have htri : T.transpose.BlockTriangular id := by
    intro i j hji
    change f (w j) i = 0
    rw [hcoeff j i (le_of_lt hji), hbase]
    exact if_neg (show j ≠ i from ne_of_lt hji)
  have hdet : T.det = 1 := by
    rw [← Matrix.det_transpose, Matrix.det_of_upperTriangular htri]
    simp only [Matrix.transpose_apply, hdiag, Finset.prod_const_one]
  have hunit : IsUnit T := (Matrix.isUnit_iff_isUnit_det T).mpr (by rw [hdet]; exact isUnit_one)
  have hrows : LinearIndependent ℝ (Matrix.row T) := Matrix.linearIndependent_rows_iff_isUnit.mpr hunit
  exact hrows.of_comp f.toLinearMap

theorem davenport_integral_reduce10 (Lambda : Submodule ℤ (Fin 10 → ℝ))
    (v : Fin 10 → (Fin 10 → ℝ)) (hvL : ∀ i, v i ∈ Lambda)
    (F : (Fin 10 → ℝ) ≃ₗ[ℝ] (Fin 10 → ℝ))
    (htri : ∀ i j : Fin 10, j < i → F (v j) i = 0)
    (hdiag : ∀ i : Fin 10, F (v i) i = 1)
    (k : ℕ) (hk : k ≤ 10) (x : Fin 10 → ℝ) (hx : x ∈ Lambda) :
    ∃ z ∈ Lambda, z-x ∈ prefixSpan10 v k hk ∧
      (∀ i : Fin 10, i.val < k → 0 ≤ F z i ∧ F z i < 1) ∧
      (∀ i : Fin 10, k ≤ i.val → F z i = F x i) := by
  classical
  induction k generalizing x with
  | zero =>
    refine ⟨x, hx, ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
    · simp only [sub_self]
      exact Submodule.zero_mem _
    · intro i hi
      omega
    · intro i hi
      rfl
  | succ k ih =>
    have hk0 : k ≤ 10 := by omega
    let a : Fin 10 := ⟨k, by omega⟩
    let n : ℤ := Int.floor (F x a)
    let x' : Fin 10 → ℝ := x-(n : ℝ) • v a
    have hx'L : x' ∈ Lambda := by
      apply Lambda.sub_mem hx
      simpa only [Int.cast_smul_eq_zsmul] using Lambda.smul_mem n (hvL a)
    obtain ⟨z, hzL, hzspan, hzlo, hzhi⟩ := ih hk0 x' hx'L
    have hdelta : x'-x ∈ prefixSpan10 v (k+1) hk := by
      have hva : v a ∈ prefixSpan10 v (k+1) hk :=
        Submodule.subset_span ⟨⟨k, Nat.lt_succ_self k⟩, rfl⟩
      have he : x'-x = -((n : ℝ) • v a) := by dsimp [x']; abel
      rw [he]
      exact (prefixSpan10 v (k+1) hk).neg_mem ((prefixSpan10 v (k+1) hk).smul_mem (n : ℝ) hva)
    have hfrac : F x' a = Int.fract (F x a) := by
      simp only [x', map_sub, map_smul, Pi.sub_apply, Pi.smul_apply, smul_eq_mul,
        hdiag a, mul_one, n, Int.self_sub_floor]
    refine ⟨z, hzL, ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
    · have he : z-x = (z-x')+(x'-x) := by abel
      rw [he]
      exact (prefixSpan10 v (k+1) hk).add_mem
        (prefixSpan10_mono v hk0 hk (Nat.le_succ k) hzspan) hdelta
    · intro i hi
      by_cases hik : i.val < k
      · exact hzlo i hik
      · have hia : i = a := Fin.ext (by dsimp [a]; omega)
        subst i
        rw [hzhi a (by exact le_rfl), hfrac]
        exact ⟨Int.fract_nonneg _, Int.fract_lt_one _⟩
    · intro i hi
      rw [hzhi i (by omega)]
      have hai : a < i := by change k < i.val; omega
      simp only [x', map_sub, map_smul, Pi.sub_apply, Pi.smul_apply, smul_eq_mul,
        htri i a hai, mul_zero, sub_zero]

theorem basis_prefix_coordinate_zero10 (b : Basis (Fin 10) ℝ (Fin 10 → ℝ))
    (j i : Fin 10) (hji : j ≤ i) (x : Fin 10 → ℝ) (hx : x ∈ minimaFlag10 b j) :
    b.equivFun x i = 0 := by
  let g : (Fin 10 → ℝ) →ₗ[ℝ] ℝ := (LinearMap.proj i).comp b.equivFun.toLinearMap
  have hs : minimaFlag10 b j ≤ LinearMap.ker g := by
    apply Submodule.span_le.mpr
    rintro _ ⟨k, rfl⟩
    change b.equivFun (b (Fin.castLE (by omega) k)) i = 0
    rw [b.equivFun_self]
    have hne : (Fin.castLE (by omega) k : Fin 10) ≠ i := by
      intro heq
      have he := congrArg Fin.val heq
      change k.val = i.val at he
      omega
    exact if_neg hne
  exact hs hx

theorem davenport_adapted_basis10 (v y : Fin 10 → (Fin 10 → ℝ)) (hv : LinearIndependent ℝ v)
    (hadapt : ∀ j, v j-y j ∈ minimaFlag10 v j) :
    ∃ b : Basis (Fin 10) ℝ (Fin 10 → ℝ), (∀ i, b i = y i) ∧
      ∀ j i : Fin 10, j ≤ i → b.equivFun (v j) i = if j = i then 1 else 0 := by
  classical
  have hy : LinearIndependent ℝ y := independent_prefix_adjustment10 v y hv (by
    intro j
    simpa only [neg_sub] using (minimaFlag10 v j).neg_mem (hadapt j))
  let b := basisOfLinearIndependentOfCardEqFinrank hy (by simp)
  have hb (i : Fin 10) : b i = y i :=
    congrFun (coe_basisOfLinearIndependentOfCardEqFinrank hy _) i
  have hbfun : (b : Fin 10 → (Fin 10 → ℝ)) = y := funext hb
  have hflags (j : Fin 10) : minimaFlag10 b j = minimaFlag10 v j := by
    apply Submodule.eq_of_le_of_finrank_eq
    · apply Submodule.span_le.mpr
      rintro _ ⟨k, rfl⟩
      let i : Fin 10 := Fin.castLE (by omega) k
      change b i ∈ minimaFlag10 v j
      rw [hb i]
      have hij : i ≤ j := by change k.val ≤ j.val; omega
      have hvj : v i ∈ minimaFlag10 v j := Submodule.subset_span ⟨k, rfl⟩
      have hwj : v i-y i ∈ minimaFlag10 v j := minimaFlag10_mono v _ _ hij (hadapt i)
      have he : v i-(v i-y i) = y i := by abel
      rw [← he]
      exact (minimaFlag10 v j).sub_mem hvj hwj
    · rw [minimaFlag10_finrank _ b.linearIndependent, minimaFlag10_finrank v hv]
  refine ⟨b, hb, ?_⟩
  intro j i hji
  have hx : v j-y j ∈ minimaFlag10 b j := (hflags j).symm ▸ hadapt j
  have hz := basis_prefix_coordinate_zero10 b j i hji (v j-y j) hx
  have he : b.equivFun (v j-y j) i = b.equivFun (v j) i-b.equivFun (y j) i := by
    rw [map_sub]
    rfl
  rw [he] at hz
  have hycoord : b.equivFun (y j) i = if j = i then 1 else 0 := by
    rw [← hb j]
    exact b.equivFun_self j i
  rw [← hycoord]
  exact sub_eq_zero.mp hz

theorem davenportCoordinates10_comp (e : (Fin 10 → ℝ) ≃ₗ[ℝ] (Fin 10 → ℝ))
    (deform : Fin 10 → ℝ) (sigma : Equiv.Perm (Fin 10)) (hd : ∀ i, 0 < deform i)
    (x : Fin 10 → ℝ) :
    davenportCoordinates10 e deform sigma hd x =
      davenportCoordinates10 (LinearEquiv.refl ℝ (Fin 10 → ℝ)) deform sigma hd (e x) := by
  ext i
  rw [davenportCoordinates10_apply, davenportCoordinates10_apply]
  rfl

theorem davenport_row_gauge_bound10 (y : Fin 10 → ℝ) (p : Equiv.Perm (Fin 10))
    (deform : Fin 10 → ℝ) (hd : ∀ i, 0 < deform i) (hanti : Antitone deform)
    (j : Fin 10) (hzero : ∀ i : Fin 10, i < j → y (p i) = 0) :
    coordinateGauge10 (davenportCoordinates10 (LinearEquiv.refl ℝ (Fin 10 → ℝ)) deform p.symm hd) y ≤
      deform j * ‖y‖ := by
  change ‖davenportCoordinates10 (LinearEquiv.refl ℝ (Fin 10 → ℝ)) deform p.symm hd y‖ ≤ _
  apply (pi_norm_le_iff_of_nonempty _).mpr
  intro a
  rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, davenportCoordinates10_apply]
  change |deform (p.symm a) * y a| ≤ _
  rw [abs_mul, abs_of_pos (hd _)]
  by_cases h : p.symm a < j
  · have hz : y a = 0 := by simpa only [p.apply_symm_apply] using hzero (p.symm a) h
    rw [hz, abs_zero, mul_zero]
    exact mul_nonneg (hd j).le (norm_nonneg _)
  · have hj : j ≤ p.symm a := le_of_not_gt h
    have hnorm : |y a| ≤ ‖y‖ := by simpa only [Real.norm_eq_abs] using norm_le_pi_norm y a
    exact (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right (hanti hj) (abs_nonneg _)).trans
      (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hnorm (hd j).le)

theorem davenport_reduced_frame10 (b : Basis (Fin 10) ℝ (Fin 10 → ℝ))
    (e : (Fin 10 → ℝ) ≃ₗ[ℝ] (Fin 10 → ℝ)) (v : Fin 10 → (Fin 10 → ℝ))
    (hv : LinearIndependent ℝ v) (hvL : ∀ i, v i ∈ lattice10Span b)
    (hvr : ∀ i, coordinateGauge10 e (v i) = successiveRadius10 b e i)
    (deform : Fin 10 → ℝ) (hd : ∀ i, 0 < deform i) (hanti : Antitone deform)
    (hscaled : Monotone (fun i => deform i * successiveRadius10 b e i)) :
    ∃ p : Equiv.Perm (Fin 10), ∃ z : Fin 10 → (Fin 10 → ℝ), LinearIndependent ℝ z ∧
      (∀ i, z i ∈ lattice10Span b) ∧
      (∀ j, coordinateGauge10 (davenportCoordinates10 e deform p.symm hd) (z j) ≤
        1024 * (deform j * successiveRadius10 b e j)) ∧
      ∀ j : Fin 10, ∀ x ∈ prefixSpan10 (e ∘ v) (j.val+1) (by omega), ∀ M : ℝ, 0 ≤ M →
        (∀ i : Fin (j.val+1), |x (p (Fin.castLE (by omega) i))| ≤ M) → ‖x‖ ≤ 1024*M := by
  classical
  have he : LinearIndependent ℝ (e ∘ v) :=
    hv.map' e.toLinearMap (LinearMap.ker_eq_bot.mpr e.injective)
  obtain ⟨p, y, hyspan, hyzero, hynorm, hp⟩ := davenport_adapted_rows10 (e ∘ v) he
  obtain ⟨B, hB, hcoeff⟩ := davenport_adapted_basis10 (e ∘ v) y he hyspan
  let F : (Fin 10 → ℝ) ≃ₗ[ℝ] (Fin 10 → ℝ) := e.trans B.equivFun
  have htri (i j : Fin 10) (hji : j < i) : F (v j) i = 0 := by
    have h := hcoeff j i hji.le
    change B.equivFun (e (v j)) i = 0
    simpa only [Function.comp_apply, ne_of_lt hji, if_false] using h
  have hdiag (i : Fin 10) : F (v i) i = 1 := by
    simpa only [Function.comp_apply, if_pos rfl] using hcoeff i i le_rfl
  have hex (j : Fin 10) : ∃ z ∈ lattice10Span b, z-v j ∈ minimaFlag10 v j ∧
      (∀ i : Fin 10, i < j → 0 ≤ F z i ∧ F z i < 1) ∧
      (∀ i : Fin 10, j ≤ i → F z i = if j = i then 1 else 0) := by
    obtain ⟨z, hzL, hzspan, hzlo, hzhi⟩ :=
      davenport_integral_reduce10 (lattice10Span b) v hvL F htri hdiag j.val (by omega) (v j) (hvL j)
    refine ⟨z, hzL, hzspan, hzlo, ?_⟩
    intro i hji
    rw [hzhi i hji]
    exact hcoeff j i hji
  choose z hzL hzspan hzlo hzhi using hex
  have hz : LinearIndependent ℝ z := independent_prefix_adjustment10 v z hv hzspan
  let D := davenportCoordinates10 (LinearEquiv.refl ℝ (Fin 10 → ℝ)) deform p.symm hd
  have hybound (i : Fin 10) : coordinateGauge10 D (y i) ≤
      (2 : ℝ)^i.val * (deform i * successiveRadius10 b e i) := by
    have h0 := davenport_row_gauge_bound10 (y i) p deform hd hanti i (hyzero i)
    have h1 := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (hynorm i) (hd i).le
    have h2 : deform i * ((2 : ℝ)^i.val * ‖(e ∘ v) i‖) =
        (2 : ℝ)^i.val * (deform i * successiveRadius10 b e i) := by
