      change deform i * ((2 : ℝ)^i.val * coordinateGauge10 e (v i)) = _
      rw [hvr i]
      ring
    exact h0.trans (h1.trans_eq h2)
  refine ⟨p, z, hz, hzL, ?_, hp⟩
  intro j
  have haj : 0 ≤ deform j * successiveRadius10 b e j := by
    apply mul_nonneg (hd j).le
    rw [← hvr j]
    exact norm_nonneg _
  have hrepr : e (z j) = ∑ i : Fin 10, F (z j) i • y i := by
    have hh := B.sum_equivFun (e (z j))
    simpa only [hB] using hh.symm
  have hnorm : coordinateGauge10 (davenportCoordinates10 e deform p.symm hd) (z j) ≤
      ∑ i : Fin 10, (2 : ℝ)^i.val * (deform j * successiveRadius10 b e j) := by
    change ‖davenportCoordinates10 e deform p.symm hd (z j)‖ ≤ _
    rw [davenportCoordinates10_comp, hrepr, map_sum]
    simp only [map_smul]
    apply (norm_sum_le _ _).trans
    apply Finset.sum_le_sum
    intro i hi
    rw [norm_smul, Real.norm_eq_abs]
    by_cases hij : i ≤ j
    · have hcoef : |F (z j) i| ≤ 1 := by
        by_cases hlt : i < j
        · rw [abs_of_nonneg (hzlo j i hlt).1]
          exact (hzlo j i hlt).2.le
        · have heq : i = j := le_antisymm hij (le_of_not_gt hlt)
          subst i
          rw [hzhi j j le_rfl]
          norm_num
      have hm0 := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hcoef (norm_nonneg (D (y i)))
      have hm1 := hybound i
      change ‖D (y i)‖ ≤ _ at hm1
      have hm2 := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (hscaled hij) (by positivity : 0 ≤ (2 : ℝ)^i.val)
      rw [one_mul] at hm0
      exact hm0.trans (hm1.trans hm2)
    · have hji : j < i := lt_of_not_ge hij
      rw [hzhi j i hji.le, if_neg (ne_of_lt hji), abs_zero, zero_mul]
      exact mul_nonneg (by positivity) haj
  have hsum : (∑ i : Fin 10, (2 : ℝ)^i.val * (deform j * successiveRadius10 b e j)) =
      1023 * (deform j * successiveRadius10 b e j) := by
    have hpow : (∑ i : Fin 10, (2 : ℝ)^i.val) = 1023 := by
      norm_num [Fin.sum_univ_succ]
    rw [← Finset.sum_mul, hpow]
  rw [hsum] at hnorm
  exact hnorm.trans (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right (by norm_num : (1023 : ℝ) ≤ 1024) haj)

theorem davenport_lemma10 (b : Basis (Fin 10) ℝ (Fin 10 → ℝ))
    (e : (Fin 10 → ℝ) ≃ₗ[ℝ] (Fin 10 → ℝ)) (v : Fin 10 → (Fin 10 → ℝ))
    (hv : LinearIndependent ℝ v) (hvL : ∀ i, v i ∈ lattice10Span b)
    (hvr : ∀ i, coordinateGauge10 e (v i) = successiveRadius10 b e i)
    (deform : Fin 10 → ℝ) (hd : ∀ i, 0 < deform i) (hprod : (∏ i, deform i) = 1)
    (hanti : Antitone deform) (hscaled : Monotone (fun i => deform i * successiveRadius10 b e i)) :
    ∃ sigma : Equiv.Perm (Fin 10),
      (∀ j, deform j * successiveRadius10 b e j / 1024 ≤
          successiveRadius10 b (davenportCoordinates10 e deform sigma hd) j ∧
        successiveRadius10 b (davenportCoordinates10 e deform sigma hd) j ≤
          1024 * (deform j * successiveRadius10 b e j)) ∧
      MeasureTheory.volume.real (parallelepipedBody10 (davenportCoordinates10 e deform sigma hd)) =
        MeasureTheory.volume.real (parallelepipedBody10 e) ∧
      ∀ j : Fin 10, ∀ x ∈ lattice10Span b, x ∉ minimaFlag10 v j →
        deform j * successiveRadius10 b e j / 1024 ≤
          coordinateGauge10 (davenportCoordinates10 e deform sigma hd) x := by
  obtain ⟨p, z, hz, hzL, hzbound, hp⟩ := davenport_reduced_frame10 b e v hv hvL hvr deform hd hanti hscaled
  have hsep := davenport_off_flag10_of_pivot b e v hv hvL hvr deform hd hanti hscaled p hp
  refine ⟨p.symm, ?_, davenportBody10_volume e deform p.symm hd hprod, hsep⟩
  intro j
  refine ⟨davenport_minima_lower10 b _ v hv _ hsep j, ?_⟩
  let f : Fin (j.val+1) → Fin 10 := Fin.castLE (by omega)
  apply successiveRadius10_le_family b _ j (z ∘ f) (hz.comp f (Fin.castLE_injective _))
    (fun i => hzL (f i))
  intro i
  have hi : f i ≤ j := by change i.val ≤ j.val; omega
  exact (hzbound (f i)).trans (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (hscaled hi) (by norm_num : (0 : ℝ) ≤ 1024))

end
end CompoundFeasibility

end M197Literal

namespace M197Literal
open scoped BigOperators Classical Matrix
noncomputable section
namespace CompoundFeasibility.SchmidtNormal10
def normalHeight10 (M : Fin 10 → ℤ) : ℕ := Finset.univ.sup (fun i => (M i).natAbs)

theorem normalHeight10_le_iff (M : Fin 10 → ℤ) (b : ℕ) :
    normalHeight10 M ≤ b ↔ ∀ i, (M i).natAbs ≤ b := by
  simp only [normalHeight10,Finset.sup_le_iff,Finset.mem_univ,true_implies]



theorem primitive_common_divisor10 (M : Fin 10 → ℤ)
    (hM : Ideal.span (Set.range M) = ⊤) (d : ℕ)
    (hd : ∀ i, d ∣ (M i).natAbs) : d = 1 := by
  have hspan : Ideal.span (Set.range M) ≤ Ideal.span ({(d : ℤ)} : Set ℤ) := by
    apply Ideal.span_le.mpr
    rintro _ ⟨i,rfl⟩
    exact Ideal.mem_span_singleton.mpr (Int.natCast_dvd.mpr (hd i))
  have hone : (1 : ℤ) ∈ Ideal.span ({(d : ℤ)} : Set ℤ) := by
    apply hspan
    rw [hM]
    trivial
  simpa using Int.isUnit_iff_natAbs_eq.mp
    (isUnit_of_dvd_one (Ideal.mem_span_singleton.mp hone))

theorem height_attaining10 (M : Fin 10 → ℤ) (hM : M ≠ 0) :
    ∃ i : Fin 10, M i ≠ 0 ∧ (∀ k, (M k).natAbs ≤ (M i).natAbs) ∧
      normalHeight10 M = (M i).natAbs := by
  obtain ⟨i,_,he⟩ := Finset.exists_mem_eq_sup Finset.univ Finset.univ_nonempty
    (fun k : Fin 10 => (M k).natAbs)
  have hmax (k) : (M k).natAbs ≤ (M i).natAbs := by
    rw [← he]
    exact Finset.le_sup (f := fun k : Fin 10 => (M k).natAbs) (Finset.mem_univ k)
  have hi : M i ≠ 0 := by
    intro hz
    apply hM
    funext k
    have hk := hmax k
    rw [hz,Int.natAbs_zero] at hk
    exact Int.natAbs_eq_zero.mp (Nat.eq_zero_of_le_zero hk)
  exact ⟨i,hi,hmax,he⟩

theorem nine_gcd_product_dvd10 (M : Fin 10 → ℤ)
    (hM : Ideal.span (Set.range M) = ⊤) (i : Fin 10) (hi : M i ≠ 0) :
    (∏ j ∈ Finset.univ.erase i, Nat.gcd (M i).natAbs (M j).natAbs) ∣
      (M i).natAbs^8 := by
  let A := (M i).natAbs
  let d := fun j : Fin 10 => Nat.gcd A (M j).natAbs
  let S := Finset.univ.erase i
  have hA : A ≠ 0 := Int.natAbs_ne_zero.mpr hi
  have hd (j : Fin 10) : d j ≠ 0 := (Nat.gcd_pos_of_pos_left _ (Nat.pos_of_ne_zero hA)).ne'
  have hS : S.card = 9 := by simp [S]
  have hSn : S.Nonempty := Finset.card_pos.mp (by omega)
  change (∏ j ∈ S, d j) ∣ A^8
  apply (Nat.factorization_prime_le_iff_dvd (Finset.prod_ne_zero_iff.mpr (fun j _ => hd j))
    (pow_ne_zero 8 hA)).mp
  intro p hp
  have hbound (j : Fin 10) : (d j).factorization p ≤ A.factorization p :=
    (Nat.factorization_le_iff_dvd (hd j) hA).mpr (Nat.gcd_dvd_left _ _) p
  have hsome : ∃ j ∈ S, ¬p ∣ d j := by
    by_contra! hall
    have hone : p = 1 := primitive_common_divisor10 M hM p (by
      intro k
      by_cases hk : k = i
      · subst k
        obtain ⟨j,hj⟩ := hSn
        exact (hall j hj).trans (Nat.gcd_dvd_left _ _)
      · exact (hall k (by simpa [S] using hk)).trans (Nat.gcd_dvd_right _ _))
    exact hp.ne_one hone
  obtain ⟨j,hj,hjp⟩ := hsome
  have hz : (d j).factorization p = 0 := Nat.factorization_eq_zero_of_not_dvd hjp
  rw [Nat.factorization_prod (fun j _ => hd j),Nat.factorization_pow]
  simp only [Finsupp.finset_sum_apply,Finsupp.smul_apply,smul_eq_mul]
  calc
    (∑ k ∈ S, (d k).factorization p) = ∑ k ∈ S.erase j, (d k).factorization p := by
      rw [← Finset.sum_erase_add S (fun k => (d k).factorization p) hj,hz,add_zero]
    _ ≤ ∑ k ∈ S.erase j, A.factorization p := Finset.sum_le_sum (fun k _ => hbound k)
    _ = 8*A.factorization p := by
      simp [Finset.card_erase_of_mem hj,hS]

theorem minimum_secondary_gcd10 (M : Fin 10 → ℤ) (i : Fin 10) :
    ∃ j : Fin 10, j ≠ i ∧
      (∀ k : Fin 10, k ≠ i → Int.gcd (M i) (M j) ≤ Int.gcd (M i) (M k)) ∧
      Int.gcd (M i) (M j)^9 ≤
        ∏ k ∈ Finset.univ.erase i, Nat.gcd (M i).natAbs (M k).natAbs := by
  let S := Finset.univ.erase i
  let d := fun k : Fin 10 => Int.gcd (M i) (M k)
  have hS : S.card = 9 := by simp [S]
  obtain ⟨j,hj,hmin⟩ := Finset.exists_min_image S d (Finset.card_pos.mp (by omega))
  have hjne : j ≠ i := (Finset.mem_erase.mp hj).1
  refine ⟨j,hjne,fun k hk => hmin k (by simpa [S] using hk),?_⟩
  have hp := Finset.prod_le_prod' hmin
  simpa [d,hS] using hp

theorem primitive_nonzero10 (M : Fin 10 → ℤ) (hM : Ideal.span (Set.range M) = ⊤) : M ≠ 0 := by
  intro hz
  have h := primitive_common_divisor10 M hM 0 (by intro k; rw [hz]; simp)
  omega

theorem select_minimum_pair10 (M : Fin 10 → ℤ) (hM : Ideal.span (Set.range M) = ⊤) :
    ∃ i j : Fin 10, i ≠ j ∧ M i ≠ 0 ∧
      (∀ k, (M k).natAbs ≤ (M i).natAbs) ∧ normalHeight10 M = (M i).natAbs ∧
      (∀ k : Fin 10, k ≠ i → Int.gcd (M i) (M j) ≤ Int.gcd (M i) (M k)) ∧
      Int.gcd (M i) (M j)^9 ≤ (M i).natAbs^8 := by
  obtain ⟨i,hi,hmax,hheight⟩ := height_attaining10 M (primitive_nonzero10 M hM)
  obtain ⟨j,hji,hmin,hpow⟩ := minimum_secondary_gcd10 M i
  have hprod := nine_gcd_product_dvd10 M hM i hi
  exact ⟨i,j,Ne.symm hji,hi,hmax,hheight,hmin,hpow.trans
    (Nat.le_of_dvd (pow_pos (Int.natAbs_pos.mpr hi) 8) hprod)⟩

theorem select_pair10 (M : Fin 10 → ℤ) (hM : Ideal.span (Set.range M) = ⊤) :
    ∃ i j : Fin 10, i ≠ j ∧ M i ≠ 0 ∧
      (∀ k, (M k).natAbs ≤ (M i).natAbs) ∧
      Int.gcd (M i) (M j)^9 ≤ (M i).natAbs^8 := by
  obtain ⟨i,j,hij,hi,hmax,_,_,hpow⟩ := select_minimum_pair10 M hM
  exact ⟨i,j,hij,hi,hmax,hpow⟩

def pairPermutation10 (i j : Fin 10) : Equiv.Perm (Fin 10) :=
  (Equiv.swap 1 (Equiv.swap 0 i j)).trans (Equiv.swap 0 i)

theorem pairPermutation6_coordinates (i j : Fin 10) (hij : i ≠ j) :
    pairPermutation10 i j 0 = i ∧ pairPermutation10 i j 1 = j := by
  have hk : Equiv.swap 0 i j ≠ 0 := by
    intro hz
    have he : Equiv.swap 0 i j = Equiv.swap 0 i i := by simpa using hz
    exact hij ((Equiv.swap 0 i).injective he).symm
  constructor
  · simp only [pairPermutation10,Equiv.trans_apply]
    rw [Equiv.swap_apply_of_ne_of_ne (by decide : (0 : Fin 10) ≠ 1) (Ne.symm hk),
      Equiv.swap_apply_left]
  · simp [pairPermutation10,Equiv.trans_apply]

def coefficientMultiset10 (M : Fin 10 → ℤ) : Multiset ℤ := ∑ k, ({M k} : Multiset ℤ)

theorem permuted_coefficients10 (M : Fin 10 → ℤ) (e : Equiv.Perm (Fin 10)) :
    coefficientMultiset10 (fun k => M (e k)) = coefficientMultiset10 M := by
  exact e.sum_comp Finset.univ (fun k => ({M k} : Multiset ℤ)) (by intro k _; simp)

theorem permuted_range10 (M : Fin 10 → ℤ) (e : Equiv.Perm (Fin 10)) :
    Set.range (fun k => M (e k)) = Set.range M := by
  ext x
  constructor
  · rintro ⟨k,rfl⟩
    exact ⟨e k,rfl⟩
  · rintro ⟨k,rfl⟩
    exact ⟨e.symm k,by simp⟩

theorem permuted_primitive10 (M : Fin 10 → ℤ) (e : Equiv.Perm (Fin 10))
    (hM : Ideal.span (Set.range M) = ⊤) :
    Ideal.span (Set.range (fun k => M (e k))) = ⊤ := by
  rw [permuted_range10 M e,hM]

theorem permuted_height10 (M : Fin 10 → ℤ) (e : Equiv.Perm (Fin 10)) :
    normalHeight10 (fun k => M (e k)) = normalHeight10 M := by
  apply le_antisymm
  · apply (normalHeight10_le_iff _ _).mpr
    intro k
    exact Finset.le_sup (f := fun j => (M j).natAbs) (Finset.mem_univ (e k))
  · apply (normalHeight10_le_iff _ _).mpr
    intro k
    simpa using Finset.le_sup (f := fun j => (M (e j)).natAbs) (Finset.mem_univ (e.symm k))

theorem permuted_bezout10 (M a : Fin 10 → ℤ) (e : Equiv.Perm (Fin 10))
    (ha : (∑ k, a k*M k) = 1) : (∑ k, a (e k)*M (e k)) = 1 := by
  rw [e.sum_comp Finset.univ (fun k => a k*M k) (by intro k _; simp),ha]

structure NormalCoordinateChoice10 (M : Fin 10 → ℤ) where
  i : Fin 10
  j : Fin 10
  distinct : i ≠ j
  primitive : Ideal.span (Set.range M) = ⊤
  first_nonzero : M i ≠ 0
  maximal : ∀ k, (M k).natAbs ≤ (M i).natAbs
  height_attains : normalHeight10 M = (M i).natAbs
  minimum : ∀ k : Fin 10, k ≠ i → Int.gcd (M i) (M j) ≤ Int.gcd (M i) (M k)
  gcd_bound : Int.gcd (M i) (M j)^9 ≤ (M i).natAbs^8
  e : Equiv.Perm (Fin 10)
  e_zero : e 0 = i
  e_one : e 1 = j

theorem normal_coordinate_choice10 (M : Fin 10 → ℤ)
    (hM : Ideal.span (Set.range M) = ⊤) : Nonempty (NormalCoordinateChoice10 M) := by
  obtain ⟨i,j,hij,hi,hmax,hheight,hmin,hpow⟩ := select_minimum_pair10 M hM
  obtain ⟨he0,he1⟩ := pairPermutation6_coordinates i j hij
  exact ⟨{
    i := i, j := j, distinct := hij, primitive := hM, first_nonzero := hi,
    maximal := hmax, height_attains := hheight, minimum := hmin, gcd_bound := hpow,
    e := pairPermutation10 i j, e_zero := he0, e_one := he1 }⟩

theorem NormalCoordinateChoice10.permuted_data {M : Fin 10 → ℤ} (C : NormalCoordinateChoice10 M) :
    coefficientMultiset10 (fun k => M (C.e k)) = coefficientMultiset10 M ∧
    Ideal.span (Set.range (fun k => M (C.e k))) = ⊤ ∧
    normalHeight10 (fun k => M (C.e k)) = normalHeight10 M ∧
    M (C.e 0) ≠ 0 ∧
    Int.gcd (M (C.e 0)) (M (C.e 1))^9 ≤ normalHeight10 (fun k => M (C.e k))^8 ∧
    (∀ k, C.e.symm (C.e k) = k) ∧ (∀ k, C.e (C.e.symm k) = k) ∧
    (∀ k, (fun j => M (C.e j)) (C.e.symm k) = M k) := by
  refine ⟨permuted_coefficients10 M C.e,permuted_primitive10 M C.e C.primitive,
    permuted_height10 M C.e,?_,?_,C.e.symm_apply_apply,C.e.apply_symm_apply,?_⟩
  · rw [C.e_zero]
    exact C.first_nonzero
  · rw [C.e_zero,C.e_one,permuted_height10 M C.e,C.height_attains]
    exact C.gcd_bound
  · intro k
    simp


variable {K : Type*} [Field K] [CharZero K]

abbrev Vars10 (m : ℕ) := Fin m × Fin 10

def linearNormal10 {m : ℕ} (M : Fin m → Fin 10 → ℤ) (h : Fin m) :
    MvPolynomial (Vars10 m) K :=
  ∑ j, MvPolynomial.C (M h j : K)*MvPolynomial.X (h,j)

def sourceNormalIdeal10 {m : ℕ} (M : Fin m → Fin 10 → ℤ)
    (r : Fin m → ℕ) (c : ℝ) : Ideal (MvPolynomial (Vars10 m) ℝ) :=
  Ideal.span {F | ∃ i : Fin m → ℕ, c ≤ ∑ h, (i h : ℝ)/(r h : ℝ) ∧
    F = ∏ h, linearNormal10 M h^(i h)}

def sourceStrictNormalIdeal10 {m : ℕ} (M : Fin m → Fin 10 → ℤ)
    (r : Fin m → ℕ) (c : ℝ) : Ideal (MvPolynomial (Vars10 m) ℝ) :=
  Ideal.span {F | ∃ i : Fin m → ℕ, c < ∑ h, (i h : ℝ)/(r h : ℝ) ∧
    F = ∏ h, linearNormal10 M h^(i h)}

def forward10 {m : ℕ} (M : Fin m → Fin 10 → ℤ) (k : Fin m → Fin 10) :
    MvPolynomial (Vars10 m) K →+* MvPolynomial (Vars10 m) K :=
  MvPolynomial.eval₂Hom MvPolynomial.C
    (fun u => if u.2 = k u.1 then linearNormal10 M u.1 else MvPolynomial.X u)

def inverse10 {m : ℕ} (M : Fin m → Fin 10 → ℤ) (k : Fin m → Fin 10) :
    MvPolynomial (Vars10 m) K →+* MvPolynomial (Vars10 m) K :=
  MvPolynomial.eval₂Hom MvPolynomial.C (fun u => if u.2 = k u.1 then
    MvPolynomial.C ((M u.1 (k u.1) : K)⁻¹) *
      (MvPolynomial.X u - ∑ j ∈ Finset.univ.erase (k u.1),
        MvPolynomial.C (M u.1 j : K) * MvPolynomial.X (u.1,j))
    else MvPolynomial.X u)

theorem forward6_inverse10 {m : ℕ} (M : Fin m → Fin 10 → ℤ)
    (k : Fin m → Fin 10) (hk : ∀ h, M h (k h) ≠ 0)
    (P : MvPolynomial (Vars10 m) K) :
    forward10 M k (inverse10 M k P) = P := by
  classical
  have he : (forward10 (K := K) M k).comp (inverse10 M k) = RingHom.id _ := by
    apply MvPolynomial.ringHom_ext
    · intro c
      simp [forward10,inverse10]
    · intro u
      rcases u with ⟨h,i⟩
      by_cases hi : i = k h
      · subst i
        have hc : (MvPolynomial.C ((M h (k h) : K)⁻¹) : MvPolynomial (Vars10 m) K) *
            MvPolynomial.C (M h (k h) : K) = 1 := by
          rw [← MvPolynomial.C_mul,inv_mul_cancel₀ (by exact_mod_cast hk h),map_one]
        have hs : linearNormal10 M h =
            (∑ j ∈ Finset.univ.erase (k h),
              MvPolynomial.C (M h j : K) * MvPolynomial.X (h,j)) +
              MvPolynomial.C (M h (k h) : K) * MvPolynomial.X (h,k h) := by
          exact (Finset.sum_erase_add _ _ (Finset.mem_univ (k h))).symm
        simp only [RingHom.comp_apply,inverse10,MvPolynomial.eval₂Hom_X',
          Prod.fst,Prod.snd,ite_true,map_mul,map_sub,map_sum,forward10,
          MvPolynomial.eval₂Hom_C,MvPolynomial.eval₂Hom_X',RingHom.id_apply]
        have hfree : (∑ j ∈ Finset.univ.erase (k h),
            MvPolynomial.C (M h j : K) *
              (if j = k h then linearNormal10 M h else MvPolynomial.X (h,j))) =
            ∑ j ∈ Finset.univ.erase (k h),
              MvPolynomial.C (M h j : K) * MvPolynomial.X (h,j) := by
          apply Finset.sum_congr rfl
          intro j hj
          rw [if_neg (Finset.mem_erase.mp hj).1]
        rw [hfree,hs,add_sub_cancel_left,← mul_assoc,hc,one_mul]
      · simp [forward10,inverse10,hi]
  exact congrArg (fun f => f P) he

theorem inverse6_forward10 {m : ℕ} (M : Fin m → Fin 10 → ℤ)
    (k : Fin m → Fin 10) (hk : ∀ h, M h (k h) ≠ 0)
    (P : MvPolynomial (Vars10 m) K) :
    inverse10 M k (forward10 M k P) = P := by
  classical
  have he : (inverse10 (K := K) M k).comp (forward10 M k) = RingHom.id _ := by
    apply MvPolynomial.ringHom_ext
    · intro c
      simp [forward10,inverse10]
    · intro u
      rcases u with ⟨h,i⟩
      by_cases hi : i = k h
      · subst i
        have hc : (MvPolynomial.C (M h (k h) : K) : MvPolynomial (Vars10 m) K) *
            MvPolynomial.C ((M h (k h) : K)⁻¹) = 1 := by
          rw [← MvPolynomial.C_mul,mul_inv_cancel₀ (by exact_mod_cast hk h),map_one]
        simp only [RingHom.comp_apply,forward10,MvPolynomial.eval₂Hom_X',
          Prod.fst,Prod.snd,ite_true,RingHom.id_apply]
        change inverse10 M k (linearNormal10 M h) = MvPolynomial.X (h,k h)
        rw [linearNormal10,← Finset.sum_erase_add _ _ (Finset.mem_univ (k h))]
        simp only [map_add,map_sum,map_mul,inverse10,MvPolynomial.eval₂Hom_C,
          MvPolynomial.eval₂Hom_X',Prod.fst,Prod.snd,ite_true]
        have hfree : (∑ j ∈ Finset.univ.erase (k h),
            MvPolynomial.C (M h j : K) *
              (if j = k h then MvPolynomial.C ((M h (k h) : K)⁻¹) *
                (MvPolynomial.X (h,j) - ∑ l ∈ Finset.univ.erase (k h),
                  MvPolynomial.C (M h l : K) * MvPolynomial.X (h,l))
               else MvPolynomial.X (h,j))) =
            ∑ j ∈ Finset.univ.erase (k h),
              MvPolynomial.C (M h j : K) * MvPolynomial.X (h,j) := by
          apply Finset.sum_congr rfl
          intro j hj
          rw [if_neg (Finset.mem_erase.mp hj).1]
        rw [hfree,← mul_assoc,hc,one_mul]
        ring
      · simp [forward10,inverse10,hi]
  exact congrArg (fun f => f P) he



def normalEquiv10 {m : ℕ} (M : Fin m → Fin 10 → ℤ) (k : Fin m → Fin 10)
    (hk : ∀ h, M h (k h) ≠ 0) :
    MvPolynomial (Vars10 m) K ≃+* MvPolynomial (Vars10 m) K where
  toFun := forward10 M k
  invFun := inverse10 M k
  left_inv := inverse6_forward10 M k hk
  right_inv := forward6_inverse10 M k hk
  map_mul' := map_mul (forward10 M k)
  map_add' := map_add (forward10 M k)

theorem inverse10_normal {m : ℕ} (M : Fin m → Fin 10 → ℤ)
    (k : Fin m → Fin 10) (hk : ∀ h, M h (k h) ≠ 0) (h : Fin m) :
    inverse10 (K := K) M k (linearNormal10 M h) = MvPolynomial.X (h,k h) := by
  simpa only [forward10,MvPolynomial.eval₂Hom_X',Prod.fst,Prod.snd,ite_true]
    using inverse6_forward10 (K := K) M k hk (MvPolynomial.X (h,k h))

theorem linearNormal10_map {F : Type*} [Field F] [CharZero F] (f : K →+* F)
    {m : ℕ} (M : Fin m → Fin 10 → ℤ) (h : Fin m) :
    (linearNormal10 (K := K) M h).map f = linearNormal10 M h := by
  simp [linearNormal10]

theorem inverse10_map {F : Type*} [Field F] [CharZero F] (f : K →+* F)
    {m : ℕ} (M : Fin m → Fin 10 → ℤ) (k : Fin m → Fin 10)
    (P : MvPolynomial (Vars10 m) K) :
    (inverse10 M k P).map f = inverse10 M k (P.map f) := by
  induction P using MvPolynomial.induction_on with
  | C c => simp [inverse10]
  | add P Q hP hQ => simp only [map_add,hP,hQ]
  | mul_X P u hP =>
    simp only [map_mul,hP,MvPolynomial.map_X]
    by_cases hu : u.2 = k u.1 <;> simp [inverse10,hu]

theorem forward10_map {F : Type*} [Field F] [CharZero F] (f : K →+* F)
    {m : ℕ} (M : Fin m → Fin 10 → ℤ) (k : Fin m → Fin 10)
    (P : MvPolynomial (Vars10 m) K) :
    (forward10 M k P).map f = forward10 M k (P.map f) := by
  induction P using MvPolynomial.induction_on with
  | C c => simp [forward10]
  | add P Q hP hQ => simp only [map_add,hP,hQ]
  | mul_X P u hP =>
    simp only [map_mul,hP,MvPolynomial.map_X]
    by_cases hu : u.2 = k u.1 <;> simp [forward10,hu,linearNormal10_map]

def normalPolynomialQ10 {m : ℕ} (M : Fin m → Fin 10 → ℤ)
    (P : MvPolynomial (Vars10 m) ℤ) : MvPolynomial (Vars10 m) ℚ :=
  inverse10 M (fun _ => 0) (P.map (Int.castRingHom ℚ))

theorem normalPolynomialQ10_forward {m : ℕ} (M : Fin m → Fin 10 → ℤ)
    (hM : ∀ h, M h 0 ≠ 0) (P : MvPolynomial (Vars10 m) ℤ) :
    forward10 M (fun _ => 0) (normalPolynomialQ10 M P) = P.map (Int.castRingHom ℚ) :=
  forward6_inverse10 M (fun _ => 0) hM _

theorem normalPolynomialQ10_nonzero {m : ℕ} (M : Fin m → Fin 10 → ℤ)
    (hM : ∀ h, M h 0 ≠ 0) (P : MvPolynomial (Vars10 m) ℤ) (hP : P ≠ 0) :
    normalPolynomialQ10 M P ≠ 0 := by
  intro hz
  have he := normalPolynomialQ10_forward M hM P
  rw [hz,map_zero] at he
  exact hP ((MvPolynomial.map_injective (Int.castRingHom ℚ) Int.cast_injective)
    (he.symm.trans (map_zero _).symm))

theorem normalPolynomialQ10_real {m : ℕ} (M : Fin m → Fin 10 → ℤ)
    (P : MvPolynomial (Vars10 m) ℤ) :
    (normalPolynomialQ10 M P).map (Rat.castHom ℝ) =
      inverse10 (K := ℝ) M (fun _ => 0) (P.map (Int.castRingHom ℝ)) := by
  rw [normalPolynomialQ10,inverse10_map,MvPolynomial.map_map]
  rfl


theorem inverse10_normal_products {m : ℕ} (M : Fin m → Fin 10 → ℤ)
    (hM : ∀ h, M h 0 ≠ 0) (i : Fin m → ℕ) :
    inverse10 (K := K) M (fun _ => 0) (∏ h, linearNormal10 M h^(i h)) =
      ∏ h, MvPolynomial.X (h,0)^(i h) := by
  simp only [map_prod,map_pow,inverse10_normal M (fun _ => 0) hM]

def coordinateIdeal10 {m : ℕ} (r : Fin m → ℕ) (c : ℝ) :
    Ideal (MvPolynomial (Vars10 m) ℝ) :=
  Ideal.span {F | ∃ i : Fin m → ℕ, c ≤ ∑ h, (i h : ℝ)/(r h : ℝ) ∧
    F = ∏ h, MvPolynomial.X (h,0)^(i h)}

def coordinateStrictIdeal10 {m : ℕ} (r : Fin m → ℕ) (c : ℝ) :
    Ideal (MvPolynomial (Vars10 m) ℝ) :=
  Ideal.span {F | ∃ i : Fin m → ℕ, c < ∑ h, (i h : ℝ)/(r h : ℝ) ∧
    F = ∏ h, MvPolynomial.X (h,0)^(i h)}

theorem inverse10_ideal_map {m : ℕ} (M : Fin m → Fin 10 → ℤ)
    (hM : ∀ h, M h 0 ≠ 0) (r : Fin m → ℕ) (c : ℝ) :
    Ideal.map (inverse10 M (fun _ => 0)) (sourceNormalIdeal10 M r c) = coordinateIdeal10 r c := by
  unfold sourceNormalIdeal10 coordinateIdeal10
  rw [Ideal.map_span]
  congr 1
  ext F
  constructor
  · rintro ⟨G,⟨i,hi,rfl⟩,rfl⟩
    exact ⟨i,hi,inverse10_normal_products M hM i⟩
  · rintro ⟨i,hi,rfl⟩
    exact ⟨_,⟨i,hi,rfl⟩,inverse10_normal_products M hM i⟩

theorem inverse10_strictIdeal_map {m : ℕ} (M : Fin m → Fin 10 → ℤ)
    (hM : ∀ h, M h 0 ≠ 0) (r : Fin m → ℕ) (c : ℝ) :
    Ideal.map (inverse10 M (fun _ => 0)) (sourceStrictNormalIdeal10 M r c) =
      coordinateStrictIdeal10 r c := by
  unfold sourceStrictNormalIdeal10 coordinateStrictIdeal10
  rw [Ideal.map_span]
  congr 1
  ext F
  constructor
  · rintro ⟨G,⟨i,hi,rfl⟩,rfl⟩
    exact ⟨i,hi,inverse10_normal_products M hM i⟩
  · rintro ⟨i,hi,rfl⟩
    exact ⟨_,⟨i,hi,rfl⟩,inverse10_normal_products M hM i⟩

theorem inverse10_ideal_iff {m : ℕ} (M : Fin m → Fin 10 → ℤ)
    (hM : ∀ h, M h 0 ≠ 0) (r : Fin m → ℕ) (c : ℝ)
    (P : MvPolynomial (Vars10 m) ℝ) :
    P ∈ sourceNormalIdeal10 M r c ↔ inverse10 M (fun _ => 0) P ∈ coordinateIdeal10 r c := by
  rw [← inverse10_ideal_map M hM r c]
  constructor
  · exact Ideal.mem_map_of_mem _
  · intro hP
    obtain ⟨Q,hQ,he⟩ := (Ideal.mem_map_iff_of_surjective
      (inverse10 M (fun _ => 0)) (normalEquiv10 M (fun _ => 0) hM).symm.surjective).mp hP
    exact (normalEquiv10 M (fun _ => 0) hM).symm.injective he ▸ hQ

theorem inverse10_strictIdeal_iff {m : ℕ} (M : Fin m → Fin 10 → ℤ)
    (hM : ∀ h, M h 0 ≠ 0) (r : Fin m → ℕ) (c : ℝ)
    (P : MvPolynomial (Vars10 m) ℝ) :
    P ∈ sourceStrictNormalIdeal10 M r c ↔
      inverse10 M (fun _ => 0) P ∈ coordinateStrictIdeal10 r c := by
  rw [← inverse10_strictIdeal_map M hM r c]
  constructor
  · exact Ideal.mem_map_of_mem _
  · intro hP
    obtain ⟨Q,hQ,he⟩ := (Ideal.mem_map_iff_of_surjective
      (inverse10 M (fun _ => 0)) (normalEquiv10 M (fun _ => 0) hM).symm.surjective).mp hP
    exact (normalEquiv10 M (fun _ => 0) hM).symm.injective he ▸ hQ

theorem selected_normal_substitution10 {m : ℕ} (M : Fin m → Fin 10 → ℤ)
    (hM : ∀ h, Ideal.span (Set.range (M h)) = ⊤)
    (P : MvPolynomial (Vars10 m) ℤ) (hP : P ≠ 0) :
    ∃ (e : Fin m → Equiv.Perm (Fin 10)) (N : Fin m → Fin 10 → ℤ)
      (PN : MvPolynomial (Vars10 m) ℚ),
      (∀ h k, N h k = M h (e h k)) ∧
      (∀ h, N h 0 ≠ 0 ∧ (N h 0).natAbs = normalHeight10 (M h) ∧
        Int.gcd (N h 0) (N h 1)^9 ≤ (N h 0).natAbs^8) ∧
      PN = normalPolynomialQ10 N P ∧ PN ≠ 0 ∧
      forward10 N (fun _ => 0) PN = P.map (Int.castRingHom ℚ) ∧
      (∀ (r : Fin m → ℕ) (c : ℝ),
        P.map (Int.castRingHom ℝ) ∈ sourceNormalIdeal10 N r c ↔
          PN.map (Rat.castHom ℝ) ∈ coordinateIdeal10 r c) ∧
      (∀ (r : Fin m → ℕ) (c : ℝ),
        P.map (Int.castRingHom ℝ) ∈ sourceStrictNormalIdeal10 N r c ↔
          PN.map (Rat.castHom ℝ) ∈ coordinateStrictIdeal10 r c) := by
  let C := fun h => Classical.choice (normal_coordinate_choice10 (M h) (hM h))
  let e := fun h => (C h).e
  let N := fun h k => M h (e h k)
  have hn : ∀ h, N h 0 ≠ 0 := by
    intro h
    change M h ((C h).e 0) ≠ 0
    rw [(C h).e_zero]
    exact (C h).first_nonzero
  let PN := normalPolynomialQ10 N P
  refine ⟨e,N,PN,fun _ _ => rfl,?_,rfl,normalPolynomialQ10_nonzero N hn P hP,
    normalPolynomialQ10_forward N hn P,?_,?_⟩
  · intro h
    refine ⟨hn h,?_,?_⟩
    · change (M h ((C h).e 0)).natAbs = _
      rw [(C h).e_zero]
      exact (C h).height_attains.symm
    · change Int.gcd (M h ((C h).e 0)) (M h ((C h).e 1))^9 ≤ (M h ((C h).e 0)).natAbs^8
      rw [(C h).e_zero,(C h).e_one]
      exact (C h).gcd_bound
  · intro r c
    rw [show PN.map (Rat.castHom ℝ) = inverse10 N (fun _ => 0)
      (P.map (Int.castRingHom ℝ)) from normalPolynomialQ10_real N P]
    exact inverse10_ideal_iff N hn r c _
  · intro r c
    rw [show PN.map (Rat.castHom ℝ) = inverse10 N (fun _ => 0)
      (P.map (Int.castRingHom ℝ)) from normalPolynomialQ10_real N P]
    exact inverse10_strictIdeal_iff N hn r c _

def firstNineSpan10 {K : Type*} [Field K] (v : Fin 10 → Fin 10 → K) :
    Submodule K (Fin 10 → K) := Submodule.span K (Set.range (fun j : Fin 9 => v j.castSucc))

theorem lastDual10_kernel (v : Fin 10 → Fin 10 → ℚ) (hv : LinearIndependent ℚ v) :
    LinearMap.ker (rationalFrameDual42 v hv 9) = firstNineSpan10 v := by
  apply le_antisymm
  · intro x hx
    have hz : (rationalFrameBasis42 v hv).repr x 9 = 0 := by
      rw [← Basis.dualBasis_apply]
      exact (show rationalFrameDual42 v hv 9 x = 0 from hx)
    have he := (rationalFrameBasis42 v hv).sum_repr x
    rw [Fin.sum_univ_castSucc,show Fin.last 9 = (9 : Fin 10) from rfl,hz,zero_smul,add_zero] at he
    rw [← he]
    apply Submodule.sum_mem
    intro i _
    apply Submodule.smul_mem
    rw [rationalFrameBasis42_apply]
    exact Submodule.subset_span ⟨i,rfl⟩
  · apply Submodule.span_le.mpr
    rintro _ ⟨i,rfl⟩
    change rationalFrameDual42 v hv 9 (v i.castSucc) = 0
    rw [rationalFrameDual42_pairing,if_neg]
    exact fun h => by have := congrArg Fin.val h; simp at this; omega

def normalForm10 (M : Fin 10 → ℤ) : Module.Dual ℚ (Fin 10 → ℚ) :=
  ∑ i, (M i : ℚ) • LinearMap.proj i

theorem normalForm10_apply (M : Fin 10 → ℤ) (x : Fin 10 → ℚ) :
    normalForm10 M x = ∑ i, (M i : ℚ)*x i := by
  simp [normalForm10]

theorem functional_coordinates10 (f : Module.Dual ℚ (Fin 10 → ℚ)) (x : Fin 10 → ℚ) :
    f x = ∑ i, x i * f (Pi.single i 1) := by
  have hx : x = ∑ i : Fin 10, x i • Pi.single i (1 : ℚ) := by
    ext j
    simp [Pi.single_apply, mul_ite]
  conv_lhs => rw [hx]
  simp

theorem primitive_integer10 (z : Fin 10 → ℤ) (hz : z ≠ 0) :
    ∃ (g : ℤ) (M a : Fin 10 → ℤ), g ≠ 0 ∧ M ≠ 0 ∧
      (∀ i, z i = g*M i) ∧ (∑ i, a i*M i) = 1 ∧ Ideal.span (Set.range M) = ⊤ := by
  let I : Ideal ℤ := Ideal.span (Set.range z)
  let g := Submodule.IsPrincipal.generator I
  have hdiv (i : Fin 10) : g ∣ z i :=
    (Submodule.IsPrincipal.mem_iff_generator_dvd I).mp
      (Ideal.subset_span (Set.mem_range_self i))
  choose M hM using hdiv
  have hg : g ≠ 0 := by
    intro he
    apply hz
    ext i
    rw [hM i,he,zero_mul]
    rfl
  have hgm : g ∈ Submodule.span ℤ (Set.range z) := by
    rw [Ideal.submodule_span_eq]
    exact Submodule.IsPrincipal.generator_mem I
  obtain ⟨a,ha⟩ := (Submodule.mem_span_range_iff_exists_fun ℤ).mp hgm
  simp only [smul_eq_mul] at ha
  have hsum : ∑ i, a i*M i = 1 := by
    apply mul_left_cancel₀ hg
    calc
      g*(∑ i, a i*M i) = ∑ i, a i*z i := by
        rw [Finset.mul_sum]
        apply Finset.sum_congr rfl
        intro i _
        rw [hM i]
        ring
      _ = g*1 := by simpa using ha
  have hspan : Ideal.span (Set.range M) = ⊤ := by
    apply (Ideal.eq_top_iff_one _).mpr
    rw [← hsum]
    apply Ideal.sum_mem
    intro i _
    exact Ideal.mul_mem_left _ _ (Ideal.subset_span (Set.mem_range_self i))
  have hMn : M ≠ 0 := by
    intro he
    apply hz
    ext i
    rw [hM i,he]
    simp
  exact ⟨g,M,a,hg,hMn,hM,hsum,hspan⟩

theorem primitive_normal10 (v : Fin 10 → Fin 10 → ℤ)
    (hv : LinearIndependent ℚ (fun j i => (v j i : ℚ))) :
    ∃ M a : Fin 10 → ℤ, M ≠ 0 ∧ (∑ i, a i*M i) = 1 ∧
      Ideal.span (Set.range M) = ⊤ ∧
      (∀ j : Fin 9, (∑ i, M i*v j.castSucc i) = 0) ∧
      LinearMap.ker (normalForm10 M) =
        Submodule.span ℚ (Set.range (fun j : Fin 9 => fun i => (v j.castSucc i : ℚ))) := by
  let u : Fin 10 → Fin 10 → ℚ := fun j i => (v j i : ℚ)
  let f := rationalFrameDual42 u hv 9
  obtain ⟨D,hD⟩ := IsLocalization.exist_integer_multiples_of_finite
    (nonZeroDivisors ℤ) (fun i : Fin 10 => f (Pi.single i 1))
  have hDn : (D : ℤ) ≠ 0 := mem_nonZeroDivisors_iff_ne_zero.mp D.property
  have hDQ : ((D : ℤ) : ℚ) ≠ 0 := by exact_mod_cast hDn
  have hcoords : ∀ i : Fin 10, ∃ z : ℤ, (z : ℚ) = ((D : ℤ) : ℚ)*f (Pi.single i 1) := by
    intro i
    obtain ⟨z,hz⟩ := hD i
    exact ⟨z,by simpa only [Algebra.smul_def,map_intCast] using hz⟩
  choose z hz using hcoords
  have hzf : normalForm10 z = ((D : ℤ) : ℚ) • f := by
    apply LinearMap.ext
    intro x
    rw [normalForm10_apply,LinearMap.smul_apply,smul_eq_mul,functional_coordinates10 f x,Finset.mul_sum]
    apply Finset.sum_congr rfl
    intro i _
    rw [hz i]
    ring
  have hzn : z ≠ 0 := by
    intro he
    have heq := congrArg (fun F : Module.Dual ℚ (Fin 10 → ℚ) => F (u 9)) hzf
    have hp : f (u 9) = 1 := by simpa using rationalFrameDual42_pairing u hv 9 9
    rw [he] at heq
    simp only [normalForm10_apply,Pi.zero_apply,Int.cast_zero,zero_mul,
      Finset.sum_const_zero,LinearMap.smul_apply,smul_eq_mul,hp,if_pos rfl,mul_one] at heq
    exact hDQ heq.symm
  obtain ⟨g,M,a,hg,hMn,hscale,ha,hprimitive⟩ := primitive_integer10 z hzn
  have hgQ : (g : ℚ) ≠ 0 := by exact_mod_cast hg
  have hzM : normalForm10 z = (g : ℚ) • normalForm10 M := by
    apply LinearMap.ext
    intro x
    rw [normalForm10_apply,LinearMap.smul_apply,normalForm10_apply,smul_eq_mul,Finset.mul_sum]
    apply Finset.sum_congr rfl
    intro i _
    rw [hscale i]
    push_cast
    ring
  have hker : LinearMap.ker (normalForm10 M) = LinearMap.ker f := by
    calc
      _ = LinearMap.ker (normalForm10 z) := by rw [hzM,LinearMap.ker_smul _ _ hgQ]
      _ = _ := by rw [hzf,LinearMap.ker_smul _ _ hDQ]
  have hk : LinearMap.ker (normalForm10 M) = firstNineSpan10 u :=
    hker.trans (lastDual10_kernel u hv)
  refine ⟨M,a,hMn,ha,hprimitive,?_,hk⟩
  intro j
  have hm : u j.castSucc ∈ LinearMap.ker (normalForm10 M) := by
    rw [hk]
    exact Submodule.subset_span (Set.mem_range_self j)
  have hmQ : (∑ i, (M i : ℚ)*(v j.castSucc i : ℚ)) = 0 := by
    exact (normalForm10_apply M (u j.castSucc)).symm.trans hm
  exact_mod_cast hmQ


theorem original_frame_normal_substitution10
    (v : Fin 10 → Fin 10 → ℤ)
    (hv : LinearIndependent ℚ (fun j i => (v j i : ℚ)))
    (P : MvPolynomial (Vars10 1) ℤ) (hP : P ≠ 0) :
    ∃ M : Fin 10 → ℤ, M ≠ 0 ∧ Ideal.span (Set.range M) = ⊤ ∧
      LinearMap.ker (normalForm10 M) = firstNineSpan10 (fun j i => (v j i : ℚ)) ∧
      ∃ (e : Equiv.Perm (Fin 10)) (N : Fin 10 → ℤ)
        (PN : MvPolynomial (Vars10 1) ℚ),
        (∀ k, N k = M (e k)) ∧ N 0 ≠ 0 ∧
        Int.gcd (N 0) (N 1)^9 ≤ (N 0).natAbs^8 ∧ PN ≠ 0 ∧
        forward10 (fun _ => N) (fun _ => 0) PN = P.map (Int.castRingHom ℚ) := by
  obtain ⟨M,a,hn,ha,hp,hann,hker⟩ := primitive_normal10 v hv
  obtain ⟨e,N,PN,he,hdata,hPN,hne,hforward,hclosed,hstrict⟩ :=
    selected_normal_substitution10 (fun _ : Fin 1 => M) (fun _ => hp) P hP
  have hN : N = fun _ : Fin 1 => N 0 := by
    funext h
    have hh : h = 0 := Subsingleton.elim _ _
    subst h
    rfl
  rw [hN] at hforward
  exact ⟨M,hn,hp,hker,e 0,N 0,PN,he 0,(hdata 0).1,(hdata 0).2.2,hne,hforward⟩

end CompoundFeasibility.SchmidtNormal10

end
end M197Literal

namespace M197Literal
open scoped BigOperators Matrix
namespace CompoundFeasibility.OriginalDeformation10
noncomputable section

def gapRadius10 (lam : Fin 10 → ℝ) : ℝ :=
  (∏ i : Fin 10, lam (min i 8)) ^ ((1 : ℝ) / 10)

def gapDeformation10 (lam : Fin 10 → ℝ) (i : Fin 10) : ℝ :=
  gapRadius10 lam / lam (min i 8)

theorem gapRadius10_pos (lam : Fin 10 → ℝ) (hp : ∀ i, 0 < lam i) : 0 < gapRadius10 lam :=
  Real.rpow_pos_of_pos (Finset.prod_pos (fun i _ => hp _)) _

theorem gapRadius10_power (lam : Fin 10 → ℝ) (hp : ∀ i, 0 < lam i) :
    gapRadius10 lam ^ (10 : ℕ) = ∏ i : Fin 10, lam (min i 8) := by
  unfold gapRadius10
  rw [← Real.rpow_natCast, ← Real.rpow_mul (Finset.prod_pos (fun i _ => hp _)).le]
  norm_num

theorem gapRadius10_source_formula (lam : Fin 10 → ℝ) (hp : ∀ i, 0 < lam i) :
    gapRadius10 lam ^ (10 : ℕ) = (∏ i, lam i) * lam 8 / lam 9 := by
  rw [gapRadius10_power lam hp]
  simp only [Fin.prod_univ_succ]
  change lam 0 * (lam 1 * (lam 2 * (lam 3 * (lam 4 * (lam 5 * (lam 6 * (lam 7 * (lam 8 * (lam 8 * 1))))))))) =
    (lam 0 * (lam 1 * (lam 2 * (lam 3 * (lam 4 * (lam 5 * (lam 6 * (lam 7 * (lam 8 * (lam 9 * 1)))))))))) * lam 8 / lam 9
  field_simp [(hp 9).ne']
  <;> ring

theorem gapDeformation10_pos (lam : Fin 10 → ℝ) (hp : ∀ i, 0 < lam i) :
    ∀ i, 0 < gapDeformation10 lam i := fun i => div_pos (gapRadius10_pos lam hp) (hp _)

theorem gapDeformation10_product (lam : Fin 10 → ℝ) (hp : ∀ i, 0 < lam i) :
    (∏ i, gapDeformation10 lam i) = 1 := by
  change (∏ i, gapRadius10 lam / lam (min i 8)) = 1
  rw [Finset.prod_div_distrib]
  simp only [Finset.prod_const, Finset.card_univ, Fintype.card_fin]
  rw [gapRadius10_power lam hp]
  exact div_self (Finset.prod_pos (fun i _ => hp _)).ne'

theorem gapDeformation10_antitone (lam : Fin 10 → ℝ) (hp : ∀ i, 0 < lam i)
    (hm : Monotone lam) : Antitone (gapDeformation10 lam) := by
  intro i j hij
  exact div_le_div_of_nonneg_left (gapRadius10_pos lam hp).le (hp _)
    (hm (min_le_min hij le_rfl))

theorem gapDeformation10_first (lam : Fin 10 → ℝ) (hp : ∀ i, 0 < lam i)
    (i : Fin 10) (hi : i ≤ 8) : gapDeformation10 lam i * lam i = gapRadius10 lam := by
  unfold gapDeformation10
  rw [min_eq_left hi]
  exact div_mul_cancel₀ _ (hp i).ne'

theorem gapDeformation10_last (lam : Fin 10 → ℝ) :
    gapDeformation10 lam 9 * lam 9 = gapRadius10 lam * lam 9 / lam 8 := by
  change (gapRadius10 lam / lam 8) * lam 9 = _
  ring

theorem gapDeformation10_scaled (lam : Fin 10 → ℝ) (hp : ∀ i, 0 < lam i)
    (hm : Monotone lam) : Monotone (fun i => gapDeformation10 lam i * lam i) := by
  have hlast : gapRadius10 lam ≤ gapDeformation10 lam 9 * lam 9 := by
    calc
      _ = (gapRadius10 lam / lam 8) * lam 8 := (div_mul_cancel₀ _ (hp 8).ne').symm
      _ ≤ (gapRadius10 lam / lam 8) * lam 9 :=
        mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (hm (by decide : (8 : Fin 10) ≤ 9))
          (div_pos (gapRadius10_pos lam hp) (hp 8)).le
      _ = _ := rfl
  intro i j hij
  change gapDeformation10 lam i * lam i ≤ gapDeformation10 lam j * lam j
  by_cases hj : j ≤ 8
  · rw [gapDeformation10_first lam hp i (hij.trans hj),gapDeformation10_first lam hp j hj]
  · have hj3 : j = 9 := by omega
    subst j
    by_cases hi : i ≤ 8
    · rw [gapDeformation10_first lam hp i hi]
      exact hlast
    · have hi3 : i = 9 := by omega
      subst i
      exact le_rfl

theorem gapRadius10_quantitative (lam : Fin 10 → ℝ) (hp : ∀ i, 0 < lam i)
    (C Q delta : ℝ) (hQ : 1 < Q) (hprod : (∏ i, lam i) ≤ C)
    (hgap : lam 8 < lam 9 * Q^(-delta)) :
    gapRadius10 lam ^ (10 : ℕ) < C*Q^(-delta) := by
  rw [gapRadius10_source_formula lam hp,mul_div_assoc]
  have hratio : lam 8 / lam 9 < Q^(-delta) := (div_lt_iff₀ (hp 9)).mpr (by simpa only [mul_comm] using hgap)
  exact (mul_lt_mul_of_pos_left hratio (Finset.prod_pos (fun i _ => hp i))).trans_le
    (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hprod (Real.rpow_pos_of_pos (zero_lt_one.trans hQ) _).le)

theorem fourthRoot10_power (C : ℝ) (hC : 0 < C) :
    (C^((1 : ℝ)/10))^(10 : ℕ) = C := by
  rw [← Real.rpow_natCast,← Real.rpow_mul hC.le]
  norm_num

theorem gapRadius10_root_bound (lam : Fin 10 → ℝ) (hp : ∀ i, 0 < lam i)
    (C Q delta : ℝ) (hC : 0 < C) (hQ : 1 < Q)
    (hroot : gapRadius10 lam^(10 : ℕ) < C*Q^(-delta)) :
    gapRadius10 lam < C^((1 : ℝ)/10)*Q^(-delta/10) := by
  have hq : 0 < Q := zero_lt_one.trans hQ
  have hpow : (Q^(-delta/10))^(10 : ℕ) = Q^(-delta) := by
    rw [← Real.rpow_natCast,← Real.rpow_mul hq.le]
    congr 1
    ring
  apply lt_of_pow_lt_pow_left₀ 10
    (mul_pos (Real.rpow_pos_of_pos hC _) (Real.rpow_pos_of_pos hq _)).le
  rw [mul_pow,fourthRoot10_power C hC,hpow]
  exact hroot

def deformedWidths10 (Q : ℝ) (c0 : Fin 10 → ℝ) (d : Fin 10 → ℝ)
    (sigma : Equiv.Perm (Fin 10)) : Fin 10 → ℝ := fun i => Q^(c0 i)/d (sigma i)

def originalDeformedCoordinates10 (L : Matrix (Fin 10) (Fin 10) ℝ) (hL : IsUnit L)
    (Q : ℝ) (hQ : 1 < Q) (c0 : Fin 10 → ℝ) (d : Fin 10 → ℝ)
    (sigma : Equiv.Perm (Fin 10)) (hd : ∀ i, 0 < d i) :
    (Fin 10 → ℝ) ≃ₗ[ℝ] (Fin 10 → ℝ) :=
  davenportCoordinates10 (originalCoordinates10 L hL Q hQ c0) d sigma hd

theorem originalDeformedCoordinates10_apply (L : Matrix (Fin 10) (Fin 10) ℝ) (hL : IsUnit L)
    (Q : ℝ) (hQ : 1 < Q) (c0 : Fin 10 → ℝ) (d : Fin 10 → ℝ)
    (sigma : Equiv.Perm (Fin 10)) (hd : ∀ i, 0 < d i) (x : Fin 10 → ℝ) (i : Fin 10) :
    originalDeformedCoordinates10 L hL Q hQ c0 d sigma hd x i =
      d (sigma i)*(L *ᵥ x) i/Q^(c0 i) := by
  rw [originalDeformedCoordinates10,davenportCoordinates10_apply]
  change d (sigma i)*((L *ᵥ x) i/Q^(c0 i)) = _
  ring

theorem deformedWidths10_pos (Q : ℝ) (hQ : 0 < Q) (c0 : Fin 10 → ℝ)
    (d : Fin 10 → ℝ) (hd : ∀ i, 0 < d i) (sigma : Equiv.Perm (Fin 10)) :
    ∀ i, 0 < deformedWidths10 Q c0 d sigma i := fun i =>
  div_pos (Real.rpow_pos_of_pos hQ _) (hd _)

theorem deformedWidths10_balance (Q : ℝ) (hQ : 0 < Q) (c0 c2 c3 : Fin 10 → ℝ)
    (d : Fin 10 → ℝ) (hp : (∏ i, d i) = 1) (sigma : Equiv.Perm (Fin 10))
    (hb : (∑ i, (c0 i+c2 i+c3 i)) = 0) :
    (∏ i, deformedWidths10 Q c0 d sigma i)*(∏ i, Q^(c2 i+c3 i)) = 1 := by
  simp only [deformedWidths10,Finset.prod_div_distrib,Equiv.prod_comp,hp,div_one]
  rw [← Real.rpow_sum_of_pos hQ,← Real.rpow_sum_of_pos hQ,← Real.rpow_add hQ]
  have he : (∑ i, c0 i)+(∑ i, (c2 i+c3 i)) = 0 := by
    simpa only [Finset.sum_add_distrib,add_assoc] using hb
  rw [he,Real.rpow_zero]

def originalDeformedMinima10 (A2 A3 : Matrix (Fin 10) (Fin 10) ℚ)
    (L : Matrix (Fin 10) (Fin 10) ℝ) (hL : IsUnit L) (Q : ℝ) (hQ : 1 < Q)
    (c0 c2 c3 : Fin 10 → ℝ) (d : Fin 10 → ℝ) (sigma : Equiv.Perm (Fin 10))
    (hd : ∀ i, 0 < d i) (j : Fin 10) : ℝ :=
  successiveRadius10 (sourceRealBasis10 A2 A3
    (fun i => Nat.ceil (JSP000334.qThreshold 2 Q (c2 i)))
    (fun i => Nat.ceil (JSP000334.qThreshold 3 Q (c3 i))))
    (originalDeformedCoordinates10 L hL Q hQ c0 d sigma hd) j

theorem source_original_davenport10 (A2 A3 : Matrix (Fin 10) (Fin 10) ℚ)
    (L : Matrix (Fin 10) (Fin 10) ℝ) (hL : IsUnit L) (Q : ℝ) (hQ : 1 < Q)
    (c0 c2 c3 : Fin 10 → ℝ) (u : Fin 10 → originalSourceGroup10 A2 A3 Q c2 c3)
    (hu : LinearIndependent ℝ (fun i => integerReal (u i : Fin 10 → ℤ)))
    (hatt : ∀ i, coordinateGauge10 (originalCoordinates10 L hL Q hQ c0)
      (integerReal (u i : Fin 10 → ℤ)) = sourceMinima10 A2 A3 L hL Q hQ c0 c2 c3 i) :
    let lam := sourceMinima10 A2 A3 L hL Q hQ c0 c2 c3
    let d := gapDeformation10 lam
    ∃ hd : ∀ i, 0 < d i, (∏ i, d i) = 1 ∧ Antitone d ∧ Monotone (fun i => d i*lam i) ∧
      ∃ sigma : Equiv.Perm (Fin 10),
      (∀ j, d j*lam j/1024 ≤ originalDeformedMinima10 A2 A3 L hL Q hQ c0 c2 c3 d sigma hd j ∧
        originalDeformedMinima10 A2 A3 L hL Q hQ c0 c2 c3 d sigma hd j ≤ 1024*(d j*lam j)) ∧
      MeasureTheory.volume.real (parallelepipedBody10
        (originalDeformedCoordinates10 L hL Q hQ c0 d sigma hd)) =
          MeasureTheory.volume.real (parallelepipedBody10 (originalCoordinates10 L hL Q hQ c0)) ∧
      ∀ j : Fin 10, ∀ z : originalSourceGroup10 A2 A3 Q c2 c3,
        integerReal (z : Fin 10 → ℤ) ∉ minimaFlag10 (fun i => integerReal (u i : Fin 10 → ℤ)) j →
        d j*lam j/1024 ≤ coordinateGauge10 (originalDeformedCoordinates10 L hL Q hQ c0 d sigma hd)
          (integerReal (z : Fin 10 → ℤ)) := by
  dsimp only
  let b := sourceRealBasis10 A2 A3
    (fun i => Nat.ceil (JSP000334.qThreshold 2 Q (c2 i)))
    (fun i => Nat.ceil (JSP000334.qThreshold 3 Q (c3 i)))
  let e := originalCoordinates10 L hL Q hQ c0
  let lam := sourceMinima10 A2 A3 L hL Q hQ c0 c2 c3
  let d := gapDeformation10 lam
  obtain ⟨hp,hm⟩ := sourceMinima10_pos_mono A2 A3 L hL Q hQ c0 c2 c3
  have hd : ∀ i, 0 < d i := gapDeformation10_pos lam hp
  have hprod : (∏ i, d i) = 1 := gapDeformation10_product lam hp
  have hanti : Antitone d := gapDeformation10_antitone lam hp hm
  have hscaled : Monotone (fun i => d i*lam i) := gapDeformation10_scaled lam hp hm
  have hrad (i : Fin 10) : lam i = successiveRadius10 b e i :=
    sourceMinima10_eq_radius A2 A3 L hL Q hQ c0 c2 c3 i
  have hmem (z : originalSourceGroup10 A2 A3 Q c2 c3) : integerReal (z : Fin 10 → ℤ) ∈ lattice10Span b := by
    rw [sourceRealBasis10_span]
    exact ⟨(z : Fin 10 → ℤ),z.property,rfl⟩
  have hatt' (i : Fin 10) : coordinateGauge10 e (integerReal (u i : Fin 10 → ℤ)) =
      successiveRadius10 b e i := (hatt i).trans (hrad i)
  have hscaled' : Monotone (fun i => d i*successiveRadius10 b e i) := by
    simpa only [← hrad] using hscaled
  obtain ⟨sigma,hbounds,hvol,hsep⟩ := davenport_lemma10 b e
    (fun i => integerReal (u i : Fin 10 → ℤ)) hu (fun i => hmem (u i)) hatt' d hd hprod hanti hscaled'
  refine ⟨hd,hprod,hanti,hscaled,sigma,?_,hvol,?_⟩
  · intro j
    change d j*lam j/1024 ≤ successiveRadius10 b (davenportCoordinates10 e d sigma hd) j ∧
      successiveRadius10 b (davenportCoordinates10 e d sigma hd) j ≤ 1024*(d j*lam j)
    rw [hrad]
    exact hbounds j
  · intro j z hout
    change d j*lam j/1024 ≤ coordinateGauge10 (davenportCoordinates10 e d sigma hd) (integerReal (z : Fin 10 → ℤ))
    rw [hrad]
    exact hsep j _ (hmem z) hout


end
end CompoundFeasibility.OriginalDeformation10

end M197Literal

namespace CompoundFeasibility.OriginalSpanTransport10
open OriginalDeformation10

theorem span_gap_bound10 (lam : Fin 10 → ℝ) (hp : ∀ i, 0 < lam i)
    (Q delta : ℝ) (hQ : 1 < Q) (hcut : 1048576 < Q^delta)
    (hgap : lam 8 < lam 9 * Q^(-delta)) :
    1024*gapRadius10 lam < gapDeformation10 lam 9*lam 9/1024 := by
  have hq : 0 < Q := zero_lt_one.trans hQ
  have he : Q^(-delta)*Q^delta = 1 := by
    rw [← Real.rpow_add hq,neg_add_cancel,Real.rpow_zero]
  have hmul : lam 8 * Q^delta < lam 9 := by
    have hh := mul_lt_mul_of_pos_right hgap (Real.rpow_pos_of_pos hq delta)
    simpa only [mul_assoc,he,mul_one] using hh
  have hratio : 1048576*lam 8 < lam 9 := by
    have hh := mul_lt_mul_of_pos_left hcut (hp 8)
    nlinarith
  rw [gapDeformation10_last]
  apply (lt_div_iff₀ (by norm_num : (0 : ℝ) < 1024)).mpr
  apply (lt_div_iff₀ (hp 8)).mpr
  have hr := gapRadius10_pos lam hp
  nlinarith [mul_lt_mul_of_pos_left hratio hr]

theorem davenport_same_top_flag10 (b : Basis (Fin 10) ℝ (Fin 10 → ℝ))
    (e : (Fin 10 → ℝ) ≃ₗ[ℝ] (Fin 10 → ℝ)) (v : Fin 10 → Fin 10 → ℝ)
    (hv : LinearIndependent ℝ v) (hvL : ∀ i, v i ∈ lattice10Span b)
    (hatt : ∀ i, coordinateGauge10 e (v i) = successiveRadius10 b e i)
    (Q delta : ℝ) (hQ : 1 < Q) (hcut : 1048576 < Q^delta)
    (hgap : successiveRadius10 b e 8 < successiveRadius10 b e 9 * Q^(-delta)) :
    let lam := successiveRadius10 b e
    let d := gapDeformation10 lam
    ∃ (hd : ∀ i, 0 < d i) (sigma : Equiv.Perm (Fin 10)) (w : Fin 10 → Fin 10 → ℝ),
      LinearIndependent ℝ w ∧ (∀ i, w i ∈ lattice10Span b) ∧
      (∀ i, coordinateGauge10 (davenportCoordinates10 e d sigma hd) (w i) =
        successiveRadius10 b (davenportCoordinates10 e d sigma hd) i) ∧
      minimaFlag10 v 9 = minimaFlag10 w 9 := by
  dsimp only
  obtain ⟨_, _, _, hp, hm, _, _⟩ := exists_successive_minima10 b e
  let lam := successiveRadius10 b e
  let d := gapDeformation10 lam
  have hd := gapDeformation10_pos lam hp
  obtain ⟨sigma,hcmp,hvol,hsep⟩ := davenport_lemma10 b e v hv hvL hatt d hd
    (gapDeformation10_product lam hp) (gapDeformation10_antitone lam hp hm)
    (gapDeformation10_scaled lam hp hm)
  obtain ⟨w,hw,hwL,hp',hm',hwatt,_⟩ := exists_successive_minima10 b (davenportCoordinates10 e d sigma hd)
  have hstrict := span_gap_bound10 lam hp Q delta hQ hcut hgap
  have hinc : minimaFlag10 w 9 ≤ minimaFlag10 v 9 := by
    apply Submodule.span_le.mpr
    rintro _ ⟨j,rfl⟩
    let i : Fin 10 := Fin.castLE (by omega) j
    by_contra hout
    have hlo := hsep 9 (w i) (hwL i) hout
    have hhi := (hm' (show i ≤ (8 : Fin 10) by change j.val ≤ 8; omega)).trans (hcmp 8).2
    rw [gapDeformation10_first lam hp 8 le_rfl] at hhi
    rw [hwatt] at hlo
    exact (not_lt_of_ge hlo) (hhi.trans_lt hstrict)
  have heq : minimaFlag10 w 9 = minimaFlag10 v 9 :=
    Submodule.eq_of_le_of_finrank_eq hinc (by rw [minimaFlag10_finrank _ hw, minimaFlag10_finrank _ hv])
  exact ⟨hd,sigma,w,hw,hwL,hwatt,heq.symm⟩

theorem source_original_same_top_flag10
    (A2 A3 : Matrix (Fin 10) (Fin 10) ℚ) (L : Matrix (Fin 10) (Fin 10) ℝ)
    (hL : IsUnit L) (Q : ℝ) (hQ : 1 < Q) (c0 c2 c3 : Fin 10 → ℝ)
    (u : Fin 10 → originalSourceGroup10 A2 A3 Q c2 c3)
    (hu : LinearIndependent ℝ (fun i => integerReal (u i : Fin 10 → ℤ)))
    (hatt : ∀ i, coordinateGauge10 (originalCoordinates10 L hL Q hQ c0)
      (integerReal (u i : Fin 10 → ℤ)) = sourceMinima10 A2 A3 L hL Q hQ c0 c2 c3 i)
    (delta : ℝ) (hcut : 1048576 < Q^delta)
    (hgap : sourceMinima10 A2 A3 L hL Q hQ c0 c2 c3 8 <
      sourceMinima10 A2 A3 L hL Q hQ c0 c2 c3 9 * Q^(-delta)) :
    let d := gapDeformation10 (sourceMinima10 A2 A3 L hL Q hQ c0 c2 c3)
    ∃ (hd : ∀ i, 0 < d i) (sigma : Equiv.Perm (Fin 10))
      (w : Fin 10 → originalSourceGroup10 A2 A3 Q c2 c3),
      LinearIndependent ℝ (fun i => integerReal (w i : Fin 10 → ℤ)) ∧
      (∀ i, coordinateGauge10 (originalDeformedCoordinates10 L hL Q hQ c0 d sigma hd)
        (integerReal (w i : Fin 10 → ℤ)) =
          originalDeformedMinima10 A2 A3 L hL Q hQ c0 c2 c3 d sigma hd i) ∧
      minimaFlag10 (fun i => integerReal (u i : Fin 10 → ℤ)) 9 =
        minimaFlag10 (fun i => integerReal (w i : Fin 10 → ℤ)) 9 := by
  dsimp only
  let b := sourceRealBasis10 A2 A3
    (fun i => Nat.ceil (JSP000334.qThreshold 2 Q (c2 i)))
    (fun i => Nat.ceil (JSP000334.qThreshold 3 Q (c3 i)))
  let e := originalCoordinates10 L hL Q hQ c0
  have hrad (i) : sourceMinima10 A2 A3 L hL Q hQ c0 c2 c3 i = successiveRadius10 b e i :=
    sourceMinima10_eq_radius A2 A3 L hL Q hQ c0 c2 c3 i
  have hrfun : sourceMinima10 A2 A3 L hL Q hQ c0 c2 c3 = successiveRadius10 b e := funext hrad
  have hmem (z : originalSourceGroup10 A2 A3 Q c2 c3) : integerReal (z : Fin 10 → ℤ) ∈ lattice10Span b := by
    rw [sourceRealBasis10_span]
    exact ⟨z.val,z.property,rfl⟩
  have hgap' : successiveRadius10 b e 8 < successiveRadius10 b e 9 * Q^(-delta) := by
    simpa only [← hrad] using hgap
  obtain ⟨hd,sigma,v,hv,hvL,hva,heq⟩ := davenport_same_top_flag10 b e
    (fun i => integerReal (u i : Fin 10 → ℤ)) hu (fun i => hmem (u i))
    (fun i => (hatt i).trans (hrad i)) Q delta hQ hcut hgap'
  have hvG (i) : ∃ z : originalSourceGroup10 A2 A3 Q c2 c3, integerReal z.val = v i := by
    have hi := hvL i
    rw [sourceRealBasis10_span] at hi
    obtain ⟨z,hz,he⟩ := hi
    exact ⟨⟨z,hz⟩,he⟩
  choose w hw using hvG
  have hfun : (fun i => integerReal (w i : Fin 10 → ℤ)) = v := funext hw
  rw [hrfun]
  refine ⟨hd,sigma,w,hfun.symm ▸ hv,?_,by simpa only [hfun] using heq⟩
  intro i
  change coordinateGauge10 (davenportCoordinates10 e (gapDeformation10 (successiveRadius10 b e)) sigma hd)
    (integerReal (w i : Fin 10 → ℤ)) = _
  rw [hw i]
  exact hva i

end
end CompoundFeasibility.OriginalSpanTransport10

namespace CompoundFeasibility.SchmidtNormal10
noncomputable section

def realNormalForm10 (M : Fin 10 → ℤ) : Module.Dual ℝ (Fin 10 → ℝ) :=
  ∑ i, (M i : ℝ) • LinearMap.proj i

theorem realNormalForm10_apply (M : Fin 10 → ℤ) (x : Fin 10 → ℝ) :
    realNormalForm10 M x = ∑ i, (M i : ℝ)*x i := by
  simp [realNormalForm10]

theorem real_normal_kernel10 (v : Fin 10 → Fin 10 → ℤ)
    (hv : LinearIndependent ℝ (fun j i => (v j i : ℝ)))
    (M : Fin 10 → ℤ) (hM : M ≠ 0)
    (hann : ∀ j : Fin 9, (∑ i, M i*v j.castSucc i) = 0) :
    LinearMap.ker (realNormalForm10 M) =
      Submodule.span ℝ (Set.range (fun j : Fin 9 => fun i => (v j.castSucc i : ℝ))) := by
  classical
  let u : Fin 10 → Fin 10 → ℝ := fun j i => (v j i : ℝ)
  let b := basisOfPiSpaceOfLinearIndependent hv
  have hb (i : Fin 10) : b i = u i := congrFun (coe_basisOfPiSpaceOfLinearIndependent hv) i
  have hf (j : Fin 9) : realNormalForm10 M (u j.castSucc) = 0 := by
    rw [realNormalForm10_apply]
    change (∑ i, (M i : ℝ)*(v j.castSucc i : ℝ)) = 0
    exact_mod_cast hann j
  have hfn : realNormalForm10 M ≠ 0 := by
    intro hz
    apply hM
    funext i
    have he := congrArg (fun f : Module.Dual ℝ (Fin 10 → ℝ) => f (Pi.single i 1)) hz
    have hi : (M i : ℝ) = 0 := by simpa [realNormalForm10,Pi.single_apply] using he
    exact_mod_cast hi
  have hlast : realNormalForm10 M (u 9) ≠ 0 := by
    intro hz
    apply hfn
    apply b.ext
    intro j
    rw [hb]
    refine Fin.lastCases ?_ (fun i => ?_) j
    · exact hz
    · exact hf i
  have hvalue (x : Fin 10 → ℝ) :
      realNormalForm10 M x = b.repr x 9 * realNormalForm10 M (u 9) := by
    conv_lhs => rw [← b.sum_repr x]
    rw [map_sum,Fin.sum_univ_castSucc]
    simp only [map_smul,hb,hf,smul_zero,Finset.sum_const_zero,zero_add]
    rfl
  apply le_antisymm
  · intro x hx
    have hz : b.repr x 9 = 0 := (mul_eq_zero.mp ((hvalue x).symm.trans hx)).resolve_right hlast
    have he := b.sum_repr x
    rw [Fin.sum_univ_castSucc,show Fin.last 9 = (9 : Fin 10) from rfl,hz,zero_smul,add_zero] at he
    rw [← he]
    apply Submodule.sum_mem
    intro j _
    apply Submodule.smul_mem
    rw [hb]
    exact Submodule.subset_span ⟨j,rfl⟩
  · apply Submodule.span_le.mpr
    rintro _ ⟨j,rfl⟩
    exact hf j


theorem same_real_flag_rational_kernel10 (v w : Fin 10 → Fin 10 → ℤ)
    (hv : LinearIndependent ℝ (fun j => integerReal (v j)))
    (hw : LinearIndependent ℝ (fun j => integerReal (w j)))
    (hflag : minimaFlag10 (fun j => integerReal (v j)) 9 =
      minimaFlag10 (fun j => integerReal (w j)) 9) :
    ∃ M a : Fin 10 → ℤ, M ≠ 0 ∧ (∑ i, a i*M i) = 1 ∧
      Ideal.span (Set.range M) = ⊤ ∧
      LinearMap.ker (normalForm10 M) = firstNineSpan10 (fun j i => (v j i : ℚ)) ∧
      LinearMap.ker (normalForm10 M) = firstNineSpan10 (fun j i => (w j i : ℚ)) ∧
      Module.finrank ℚ (LinearMap.ker (normalForm10 M)) = 9 := by
  let f : (Fin 10 → ℚ) →ₗ[ℚ] (Fin 10 → ℝ) :=
    LinearMap.pi (fun i => (Algebra.linearMap ℚ ℝ).comp (LinearMap.proj i))
  have hvQ : LinearIndependent ℚ (fun j i => (v j i : ℚ)) := by
    apply LinearIndependent.of_comp f
    convert hv.restrict_scalars' ℚ using 1
  have hwQ : LinearIndependent ℚ (fun j i => (w j i : ℚ)) := by
    apply LinearIndependent.of_comp f
    convert hw.restrict_scalars' ℚ using 1
  obtain ⟨M,a,hM,ha,hprim,hann,hker⟩ := primitive_normal10 w hwQ
  have hreal : LinearMap.ker (realNormalForm10 M) = minimaFlag10 (fun j => integerReal (w j)) 9 :=
    real_normal_kernel10 w hw M hM hann
  have hinc : firstNineSpan10 (fun j i => (v j i : ℚ)) ≤ LinearMap.ker (normalForm10 M) := by
    apply Submodule.span_le.mpr
    rintro _ ⟨j,rfl⟩
    have hmem : integerReal (v j.castSucc) ∈ minimaFlag10 (fun j => integerReal (w j)) 9 := by
      rw [← hflag]
      exact Submodule.subset_span ⟨j,rfl⟩
    rw [← hreal] at hmem
    have hz : (∑ i, (M i : ℝ)*(v j.castSucc i : ℝ)) = 0 := hmem
    change (∑ i, (M i : ℚ)*(v j.castSucc i : ℚ)) = 0
    exact_mod_cast hz
  have hvdim : Module.finrank ℚ (firstNineSpan10 (fun j i => (v j i : ℚ))) = 9 := by
    simpa only [firstNineSpan10,Fintype.card_fin] using
      finrank_span_eq_card (hvQ.comp Fin.castSucc (Fin.castSucc_injective 9))
  have hwdim : Module.finrank ℚ (firstNineSpan10 (fun j i => (w j i : ℚ))) = 9 := by
    simpa only [firstNineSpan10,Fintype.card_fin] using
      finrank_span_eq_card (hwQ.comp Fin.castSucc (Fin.castSucc_injective 9))
  have hdim : Module.finrank ℚ (LinearMap.ker (normalForm10 M)) = 9 := by rw [hker]; exact hwdim
  have heq := Submodule.eq_of_le_of_finrank_eq hinc (hvdim.trans hdim.symm)
  exact ⟨M,a,hM,ha,hprim,heq.symm,hker,hdim⟩

end
end CompoundFeasibility.SchmidtNormal10

end M197Literal

namespace M197Literal
open scoped BigOperators Classical Matrix
namespace CompoundFeasibility.SourceNormalHighIndex10
open SourceMultiblock10 SchmidtNormal10
noncomputable section
variable {K : Type*} [Field K]

def homogeneous10 {m : ℕ} {R : Type*} [CommRing R] (r : Fin m → ℕ) (P : MvPolynomial (SourceMultiblock10.Vars10 m) R) : Prop :=
  ∀ d ∈ P.support, ∀ h, ∑ j : Fin 10, d (h,j) = r h

noncomputable def rothSourceDerivativeWeight {m : ℕ} (r : Fin m → ℕ)
    (J : SourceMultiblock10.Vars10 m →₀ ℕ) : ℝ := ∑ h, (∑ i : Fin 10, (J (h,i) : ℝ))/(r h : ℝ)

theorem rothSource_weight_add {m : ℕ} (r : Fin m → ℕ) (J K : SourceMultiblock10.Vars10 m →₀ ℕ) :
    rothSourceDerivativeWeight r (J + K) =
      rothSourceDerivativeWeight r J + rothSourceDerivativeWeight r K := by
  simp [rothSourceDerivativeWeight, Finsupp.add_apply, Nat.cast_add,
    Finset.sum_add_distrib, add_div]

theorem rothSource_weight_single {m : ℕ} (r : Fin m → ℕ) (u : SourceMultiblock10.Vars10 m) :
    rothSourceDerivativeWeight r (Finsupp.single u 1) = 1 / (r u.1 : ℝ) := by
  classical
  rcases u with ⟨h,j⟩
  simp [rothSourceDerivativeWeight, Finsupp.single_apply, Prod.mk.injEq, ite_and,
    Finset.sum_ite_eq, Finset.sum_ite_eq']
  simp [ite_div, Finset.sum_ite_eq', Finset.sum_ite_eq]

theorem derivative10_map {R S : Type*} [CommRing R] [CommRing S] {m : ℕ} (f : R →+* S) (J : SourceMultiblock10.Vars10 m →₀ ℕ)
    (P : MvPolynomial (SourceMultiblock10.Vars10 m) R) :
    (derivative10 J P).map f = derivative10 J (P.map f) := by
  apply MvPolynomial.ext
  intro d
  simp only [MvPolynomial.coeff_map, derivative10_coeff, map_mul, map_prod, map_natCast]

def integerValue {m : ℕ} (J : SourceMultiblock10.Vars10 m →₀ ℕ) (P : MvPolynomial (SourceMultiblock10.Vars10 m) ℤ)
    (v : Fin m → Fin 10 → ℤ) : ℤ :=
  MvPolynomial.eval (fun u => v u.1 u.2) (derivative10 J P)

theorem integerValue_cast {K : Type*} [CommRing K] {m : ℕ}
    (J : SourceMultiblock10.Vars10 m →₀ ℕ) (P : MvPolynomial (SourceMultiblock10.Vars10 m) ℤ) (v : Fin m → Fin 10 → ℤ) :
    (integerValue J P v : K) = MvPolynomial.eval (fun u => (v u.1 u.2 : K))
      (derivative10 J (P.map (Int.castRingHom K))) := by
  rw [← derivative10_map, MvPolynomial.eval_map]
  exact MvPolynomial.eval₂_comp (Int.castRingHom K) _ _

def gridPoint {m : ℕ} (w : Fin m → Fin 9 → Fin 10 → ℤ)
    (a : Fin m → Fin 9 → ℕ) : Fin m → Fin 10 → ℤ := fun h i => ∑ j, (a h j : ℤ)*w h j i

theorem pderiv_coeff {m : ℕ} (u : SourceMultiblock10.Vars10 m)
    (Q : MvPolynomial (SourceMultiblock10.Vars10 m) K) (b : SourceMultiblock10.Vars10 m →₀ ℕ) :
    (MvPolynomial.pderiv u Q).coeff b =
      (b u + 1 : ℕ) * Q.coeff (b + Finsupp.single u 1) := by
  classical
  induction Q using MvPolynomial.induction_on' with
  | add P Q hP hQ => simp only [map_add, MvPolynomial.coeff_add, hP, hQ]; ring
  | monomial a c =>
    by_cases ha : a u = 0
    · have hab : a ≠ b + Finsupp.single u 1 := by
        intro he
        have h := congrArg (fun d : SourceMultiblock10.Vars10 m →₀ ℕ => d u) he
        simp [ha, Finsupp.add_apply, Finsupp.single_apply] at h
      simp [MvPolynomial.pderiv_monomial, ha, MvPolynomial.coeff_monomial, hab]
    · have hle : Finsupp.single u 1 ≤ a := by rw [Finsupp.single_le_iff]; omega
      have heq : a - Finsupp.single u 1 = b ↔ a = b + Finsupp.single u 1 := by
        constructor
        · intro he
          rw [← he]
          exact (tsub_add_cancel_of_le hle).symm
        · intro he
          rw [he, add_tsub_cancel_right]
      simp only [MvPolynomial.pderiv_monomial, MvPolynomial.coeff_monomial, heq]
      split_ifs with he
      · subst a
        simp only [Finsupp.add_apply, Finsupp.single_eq_same, Nat.cast_add, Nat.cast_one]
        ring
      · simp

theorem hermite_sizes10 (eta : ℝ) (heta : 0 < eta) (r : ℕ) (hr : 0 < r) :
    eta⁻¹ < (Nat.floor eta⁻¹+1 : ℕ) ∧
    (r : ℝ)*eta < (Nat.floor ((r : ℝ)*eta)+1 : ℕ) ∧
    (r : ℝ) < (Nat.floor eta⁻¹+1 : ℕ)*(Nat.floor ((r : ℝ)*eta)+1 : ℕ) := by
  have hs := Nat.lt_floor_add_one eta⁻¹
  have ht := Nat.lt_floor_add_one ((r : ℝ)*eta)
  have hs' : eta⁻¹ < ((Nat.floor eta⁻¹+1 : ℕ) : ℝ) := by exact_mod_cast hs
  have ht' : (r : ℝ)*eta < ((Nat.floor ((r : ℝ)*eta)+1 : ℕ) : ℝ) := by exact_mod_cast ht
  refine ⟨hs',ht',?_⟩
  have hr' : (0 : ℝ) < r := by exact_mod_cast hr
  have he : eta⁻¹*((r : ℝ)*eta) = r := by field_simp
  calc
    (r : ℝ) = eta⁻¹*((r : ℝ)*eta) := he.symm
    _ < _ := mul_lt_mul hs' ht'.le (mul_pos hr' heta) (by positivity)

theorem additional_weight10 {m : ℕ} (r : Fin m → ℕ) (hr : ∀ h, 0 < r h)
    (eta : ℝ) (heta : 0 < eta) (K : SourceMultiblock10.Vars10 m →₀ ℕ)
    (hK : ∀ h, (∑ i : Fin 10, K (h,i)) ≤ Nat.floor ((r h : ℝ)*eta)) :
    rothSourceDerivativeWeight r K ≤ (m : ℝ)*eta := by
  unfold rothSourceDerivativeWeight
  calc
    _ ≤ ∑ h : Fin m, eta := by
      apply Finset.sum_le_sum
      intro h _
      apply (div_le_iff₀ (show (0 : ℝ) < r h by exact_mod_cast hr h)).mpr
      have hk : (∑ i : Fin 10, (K (h,i) : ℝ)) ≤ (Nat.floor ((r h : ℝ)*eta) : ℝ) := by
        exact_mod_cast hK h
      exact hk.trans (by simpa [mul_comm] using Nat.floor_le (le_of_lt (mul_pos (by exact_mod_cast hr h) heta)))
    _ = _ := by simp

theorem additional_cutoff10 {m : ℕ} (r : Fin m → ℕ) (hr : ∀ h, 0 < r h)
    (eta : ℝ) (heta : 0 < eta) (J K : SourceMultiblock10.Vars10 m →₀ ℕ)
    (hJ : rothSourceDerivativeWeight r J < (m : ℝ)*eta)
    (hK : ∀ h, (∑ i : Fin 10, K (h,i)) ≤ Nat.floor ((r h : ℝ)*eta)) :
    rothSourceDerivativeWeight r (K+J) < 2*eta*m := by
  rw [rothSource_weight_add]
  have hk := additional_weight10 r hr eta heta K hK
  nlinarith

theorem hasse_grid_composition10 {m : ℕ} (P : MvPolynomial (SourceMultiblock10.Vars10 m) ℤ)
    (J K : SourceMultiblock10.Vars10 m →₀ ℕ) (x : Fin m → Fin 10 → ℤ)
    (hz : integerValue (K+J) P x = 0) :
    integerValue K (derivative10 J P) x = 0 := by
  apply Int.cast_injective (α := ℚ)
  rw [Int.cast_zero,integerValue_cast,derivative10_map,source10_derivative_comp]
  rw [MvPolynomial.smul_eq_C_mul,map_mul,MvPolynomial.eval_C,← integerValue_cast, hz]
  simp


theorem homogeneous10_map {R : Type*} [CommRing R] (f : R →+* K) {m : ℕ}
    (r : Fin m → ℕ) (P : MvPolynomial (SourceMultiblock10.Vars10 m) R) (hP : homogeneous10 r P) :
    homogeneous10 r (P.map f) := by
  intro d hd h
  have hn : P.coeff d ≠ 0 := by
    intro hz
    have hn := MvPolynomial.mem_support_iff.mp hd
    apply hn
    rw [MvPolynomial.coeff_map,hz,map_zero]
  exact hP d (MvPolynomial.mem_support_iff.mpr hn) h

theorem integer_grid_rational_restriction10 {m : ℕ} (r : Fin m → ℕ) (hr : ∀ h, 0 < r h)
    (P : MvPolynomial (SourceMultiblock10.Vars10 m) ℤ) (hP : homogeneous10 r P) (J : SourceMultiblock10.Vars10 m →₀ ℕ)
    (w : Fin m → Fin 9 → Fin 10 → ℤ) (eta : ℝ) (heta : 0 < eta)
    (hgrid : ∀ a : Fin m → Fin 9 → ℕ, (∀ h j, 1 ≤ a h j ∧ a h j ≤ Nat.floor eta⁻¹+1) →
      ∀ B : SourceMultiblock10.Vars10 m →₀ ℕ, (∀ h, (∑ i : Fin 10, B (h,i)) ≤ Nat.floor ((r h : ℝ)*eta)) →
      integerValue B (derivative10 J P) (gridPoint w a) = 0) :
    ∀ y : Fin m → Fin 10 → ℚ,
      (∀ h, y h ∈ Submodule.span ℚ (Set.range (fun j i => (w h j i : ℚ)))) →
      MvPolynomial.eval (fun u => y u.1 u.2) (derivative10 J (P.map (Int.castRingHom ℚ))) = 0 := by
  classical
  let N := Nat.floor eta⁻¹+1
  let S : Fin m → Fin 9 → Finset ℚ := fun _ _ => (Finset.Icc 1 N).image (fun n : ℕ => (n : ℚ))
  have hcard : ∀ h j, (S h j).card = N := by
    intro h j
    dsimp [S]
    rw [Finset.card_image_of_injective _ (Nat.cast_injective)]
    simp
  have hdeg : ∀ d ∈ (derivative10 J (P.map (Int.castRingHom ℚ))).support, ∀ h,
      (∑ i : Fin 10, d (h,i)) ≤ r h :=
    derivative10_block_degree_bound r _ (fun d hd h => (homogeneous10_map _ r P hP d hd h).le) J
  have hsize : ∀ h, r h < N*(Nat.floor ((r h : ℝ)*eta)+1) := by
    intro h
    have he := (hermite_sizes10 eta heta (r h) (hr h)).2.2
    exact_mod_cast he
  apply multiblock_span_hermite10 _ (fun h j i => (w h j i : ℚ)) S (fun _ => N)
    (fun h => Nat.floor ((r h : ℝ)*eta)) r hcard hdeg hsize
  intro a ha B hB
  have hex : ∀ h j, ∃ n : ℕ, n ∈ Finset.Icc 1 N ∧ (n : ℚ) = a h j := by
    intro h j
    exact Finset.mem_image.mp (ha h j)
  choose n hn hna using hex
  have hn' : ∀ h j, 1 ≤ n h j ∧ n h j ≤ Nat.floor eta⁻¹+1 := by
    intro h j
    exact Finset.mem_Icc.mp (hn h j)
  have hz := congrArg (fun z : ℤ => (z : ℚ)) (hgrid n hn' B hB)
  change (integerValue B (derivative10 J P) (gridPoint w n) : ℚ) = 0 at hz
  rw [integerValue_cast,derivative10_map] at hz
  have he : blockFramePoint10 (fun h j i => (w h j i : ℚ)) a =
      (fun u : SourceMultiblock10.Vars10 m => (gridPoint w n u.1 u.2 : ℚ)) := by
    funext u
    rcases u with ⟨h,i⟩
    simp only [blockFramePoint10,gridPoint,Int.cast_sum,Int.cast_mul,Int.cast_natCast]
    apply Finset.sum_congr rfl
    intro j _
    rw [hna h j]
  rw [he]
  exact hz


theorem integer_grid_real_restriction10 {m : ℕ} (r : Fin m → ℕ) (hr : ∀ h, 0 < r h)
    (P : MvPolynomial (SourceMultiblock10.Vars10 m) ℤ) (hP : homogeneous10 r P) (J : SourceMultiblock10.Vars10 m →₀ ℕ)
    (w : Fin m → Fin 9 → Fin 10 → ℤ) (eta : ℝ) (heta : 0 < eta)
    (hgrid : ∀ a : Fin m → Fin 9 → ℕ, (∀ h j, 1 ≤ a h j ∧ a h j ≤ Nat.floor eta⁻¹+1) →
      ∀ B : SourceMultiblock10.Vars10 m →₀ ℕ, (∀ h, (∑ i : Fin 10, B (h,i)) ≤ Nat.floor ((r h : ℝ)*eta)) →
      integerValue B (derivative10 J P) (gridPoint w a) = 0) :
    ∀ y : Fin m → Fin 10 → ℝ,
      (∀ h, y h ∈ Submodule.span ℝ (Set.range (fun j i => (w h j i : ℝ)))) →
      MvPolynomial.eval (fun u => y u.1 u.2) (derivative10 J (P.map (Int.castRingHom ℝ))) = 0 := by
  classical
  let N := Nat.floor eta⁻¹+1
  let S : Fin m → Fin 9 → Finset ℝ := fun _ _ => (Finset.Icc 1 N).image (fun n : ℕ => (n : ℝ))
  have hcard : ∀ h j, (S h j).card = N := by
    intro h j
    dsimp [S]
    rw [Finset.card_image_of_injective _ (Nat.cast_injective)]
    simp
  have hdeg : ∀ d ∈ (derivative10 J (P.map (Int.castRingHom ℝ))).support, ∀ h,
      (∑ i : Fin 10, d (h,i)) ≤ r h :=
    derivative10_block_degree_bound r _ (fun d hd h => (homogeneous10_map _ r P hP d hd h).le) J
  have hsize : ∀ h, r h < N*(Nat.floor ((r h : ℝ)*eta)+1) := by
    intro h
    have he := (hermite_sizes10 eta heta (r h) (hr h)).2.2
    exact_mod_cast he
  apply multiblock_span_hermite10 _ (fun h j i => (w h j i : ℝ)) S (fun _ => N)
    (fun h => Nat.floor ((r h : ℝ)*eta)) r hcard hdeg hsize
  intro a ha B hB
  have hex : ∀ h j, ∃ n : ℕ, n ∈ Finset.Icc 1 N ∧ (n : ℝ) = a h j := by
    intro h j
    exact Finset.mem_image.mp (ha h j)
  choose n hn hna using hex
  have hn' : ∀ h j, 1 ≤ n h j ∧ n h j ≤ Nat.floor eta⁻¹+1 := by
    intro h j
    exact Finset.mem_Icc.mp (hn h j)
  have hz := congrArg (fun z : ℤ => (z : ℝ)) (hgrid n hn' B hB)
  have hz0 : ((0 : ℤ) : ℝ) = (0 : ℝ) := by norm_num
  change (integerValue B (derivative10 J P) (gridPoint w n) : ℝ) = ((0 : ℤ) : ℝ) at hz
  rw [hz0] at hz
  rw [integerValue_cast,derivative10_map] at hz
  have he : blockFramePoint10 (fun h j i => (w h j i : ℝ)) a =
      (fun u : SourceMultiblock10.Vars10 m => (gridPoint w n u.1 u.2 : ℝ)) := by
    funext u
    rcases u with ⟨h,i⟩
    simp only [blockFramePoint10,gridPoint,Int.cast_sum,Int.cast_mul,Int.cast_natCast]
    apply Finset.sum_congr rfl
    intro j _
    rw [hna h j]
  rw [he]
  exact hz


noncomputable def zeroNormalCoordinates10 {m : ℕ} (k : Fin m → Fin 10) :
    MvPolynomial (SourceMultiblock10.Vars10 m) K →+* MvPolynomial (SourceMultiblock10.Vars10 m) K :=
  MvPolynomial.eval₂Hom MvPolynomial.C (fun u => if u.2 = k u.1 then 0 else MvPolynomial.X u)

theorem zeroNormalCoordinates10_monomial {m : ℕ} (k : Fin m → Fin 10)
    (b : SourceMultiblock10.Vars10 m →₀ ℕ) (c : K) :
    zeroNormalCoordinates10 k (MvPolynomial.monomial b c) =
      if (∀ h, b (h,k h) = 0) then MvPolynomial.monomial b c else 0 := by
  classical
  rw [zeroNormalCoordinates10, MvPolynomial.eval₂Hom_monomial]
  rw [Finsupp.prod_fintype _ _ (fun _ => by simp)]
  by_cases hb : ∀ h, b (h,k h) = 0
  · rw [if_pos hb, MvPolynomial.monomial_eq]
    congr 1
    rw [Finsupp.prod_fintype _ _ (fun _ => by simp)]
    apply Finset.prod_congr rfl
    intro u _
    by_cases hu : u.2 = k u.1
    · have he : u = (u.1,k u.1) := Prod.ext rfl hu
      have hz : b u = 0 := (congrArg b he).trans (hb u.1)
      simp [hu, hz]
    · simp [hu]
  · rw [if_neg hb]
    push_neg at hb
    obtain ⟨h, hh⟩ := hb
    have hz : (∏ u : SourceMultiblock10.Vars10 m, (if u.2 = k u.1 then 0 else MvPolynomial.X u) ^ b u) =
        (0 : MvPolynomial (SourceMultiblock10.Vars10 m) K) := by
      apply Finset.prod_eq_zero (Finset.mem_univ (h,k h))
      simp [hh]
    rw [hz, mul_zero]

theorem zeroNormalCoordinates10_coeff {m : ℕ} (k : Fin m → Fin 10)
    (Q : MvPolynomial (SourceMultiblock10.Vars10 m) K) (b : SourceMultiblock10.Vars10 m →₀ ℕ) :
    (zeroNormalCoordinates10 k Q).coeff b = if (∀ h, b (h,k h) = 0) then Q.coeff b else 0 := by
  classical
  induction Q using MvPolynomial.induction_on' with
  | monomial a c =>
    rw [zeroNormalCoordinates10_monomial]
    by_cases ha : ∀ h, a (h,k h) = 0
    · rw [if_pos ha]
      by_cases hb : ∀ h, b (h,k h) = 0
      · simp [hb]
      · have hab : a ≠ b := by intro he; subst a; exact hb ha
        simp [hb, hab]
    · rw [if_neg ha]
      by_cases hb : ∀ h, b (h,k h) = 0
      · have hab : a ≠ b := by intro he; subst a; exact ha hb
        simp [hb, hab]
      · simp [hb]
  | add P Q hP hQ =>
    rw [map_add, MvPolynomial.coeff_add, hP, hQ, MvPolynomial.coeff_add]
    split_ifs <;> simp


theorem normal_zero_restriction10 {m : ℕ} (M : Fin m → Fin 10 → ℤ)
    (k : Fin m → Fin 10) (hk : ∀ h, M h (k h) ≠ 0)
    (P : MvPolynomial (SourceMultiblock10.Vars10 m) ℝ)
    (hzero : ∀ y : Fin m → Fin 10 → ℝ,
      (∀ h, y h ∈ LinearMap.ker (realNormalForm10 (M h))) →
      MvPolynomial.eval (fun u => y u.1 u.2) P = 0) :
    zeroNormalCoordinates10 k (inverse10 M k P) = 0 := by
  classical
  apply MvPolynomial.funext
  intro z
  let H := (zeroNormalCoordinates10 (K := ℝ) k).comp (inverse10 M k)
  let y : Fin m → Fin 10 → ℝ := fun h i => MvPolynomial.eval z (H (MvPolynomial.X (h,i)))
  have he : (MvPolynomial.eval z).comp H = MvPolynomial.eval (fun u => y u.1 u.2) := by
    apply MvPolynomial.ringHom_ext
    · intro c
      simp [H,inverse10,zeroNormalCoordinates10]
    · intro u
      simp [y]
  have hker : ∀ h, y h ∈ LinearMap.ker (realNormalForm10 (M h)) := by
    intro h
    have hn := inverse6_forward10 (K := ℝ) M k hk (MvPolynomial.X (h,k h))
    simp only [forward10,MvPolynomial.eval₂Hom_X',Prod.fst,Prod.snd,ite_true] at hn
    have hz := congrArg (fun Q => MvPolynomial.eval z (zeroNormalCoordinates10 k Q)) hn
    simp only [zeroNormalCoordinates10,MvPolynomial.eval₂Hom_X',Prod.fst,Prod.snd,
      ite_true,map_zero] at hz
    have hp := congrArg (fun f : MvPolynomial (SourceMultiblock10.Vars10 m) ℝ →+* ℝ => f (linearNormal10 (K := ℝ) M h)) he
    change MvPolynomial.eval z (H (linearNormal10 (K := ℝ) M h)) = _ at hp
    change MvPolynomial.eval z (H (linearNormal10 (K := ℝ) M h)) = 0 at hz
    rw [hp] at hz
    simpa [LinearMap.mem_ker,realNormalForm10,linearNormal10,map_sum,map_mul,y] using hz
  have hp := congrArg (fun f : MvPolynomial (SourceMultiblock10.Vars10 m) ℝ →+* ℝ => f P) he
  change MvPolynomial.eval z (zeroNormalCoordinates10 k (inverse10 M k P)) =
    MvPolynomial.eval (fun u => y u.1 u.2) P at hp
  rw [hp,hzero y hker,map_zero]

theorem inverse10_pderiv_X {m : ℕ} (M : Fin m → Fin 10 → ℤ)
    (k : Fin m → Fin 10) (h : Fin m) (u : SourceMultiblock10.Vars10 m) :
    MvPolynomial.pderiv (h,k h) (inverse10 M k (MvPolynomial.X u)) =
      MvPolynomial.C ((M h (k h) : ℝ)⁻¹) * (if (h,k h) = u then 1 else 0) := by
  classical
  rcases u with ⟨g,i⟩
  by_cases hg : g = h
  · subst g
    by_cases hi : i = k h
    · subst i
      have hs : MvPolynomial.pderiv (h,k h)
          (∑ j ∈ Finset.univ.erase (k h),
            MvPolynomial.C (M h j : ℝ) * MvPolynomial.X (h,j)) = 0 := by
        rw [map_sum]
        apply Finset.sum_eq_zero
        intro j hj
        simp [MvPolynomial.pderiv_C_mul,MvPolynomial.pderiv_X,Pi.single_apply,
          Prod.mk.injEq,Ne.symm (Finset.mem_erase.mp hj).1]
      simp only [inverse10,MvPolynomial.eval₂Hom_X',Prod.fst,Prod.snd,ite_true,
        MvPolynomial.pderiv_C_mul,map_sub,hs,MvPolynomial.pderiv_X,Pi.single_eq_same]
      simp
    · simp [inverse10,hi,MvPolynomial.pderiv_X,Pi.single_apply,Prod.mk.injEq,Ne.symm hi]
  · by_cases hi : i = k g <;>
      simp [inverse10,hi,MvPolynomial.pderiv_C_mul,MvPolynomial.pderiv_X,map_sum,
        Pi.single_apply,Prod.mk.injEq,hg,Ne.symm hg]

theorem inverse10_chain {m : ℕ} (M : Fin m → Fin 10 → ℤ)
    (k : Fin m → Fin 10) (h : Fin m) (P : MvPolynomial (SourceMultiblock10.Vars10 m) ℝ) :
    MvPolynomial.pderiv (h,k h) (inverse10 M k P) =
      MvPolynomial.C ((M h (k h) : ℝ)⁻¹) * inverse10 M k (MvPolynomial.pderiv (h,k h) P) := by
  classical
  induction P using MvPolynomial.induction_on with
  | C c => simp [inverse10]
  | add P Q hP hQ => simp only [map_add,hP,hQ,mul_add]
  | mul_X P u hP =>
    rw [map_mul,MvPolynomial.pderiv_mul,hP,inverse10_pderiv_X,
      MvPolynomial.pderiv_mul,map_add,map_mul,MvPolynomial.pderiv_X]
    simp only [Pi.single_apply,apply_ite,map_one,map_zero]
    by_cases hu : u = (h,k h)
    · subst u
      simp
      ring
    · simp [hu,Ne.symm hu]
      ring

def normalCoordinateWeight10 {m : ℕ} (r : Fin m → ℕ) (k : Fin m → Fin 10)
    (b : SourceMultiblock10.Vars10 m →₀ ℕ) : ℝ := ∑ h, (b (h,k h) : ℝ) / r h

theorem normalCoordinateWeight10_add {m : ℕ} (r : Fin m → ℕ) (k : Fin m → Fin 10)
    (b c : SourceMultiblock10.Vars10 m →₀ ℕ) :
    normalCoordinateWeight10 r k (b+c) = normalCoordinateWeight10 r k b + normalCoordinateWeight10 r k c := by
  simp [normalCoordinateWeight10,Finsupp.add_apply,Nat.cast_add,add_div,Finset.sum_add_distrib]

theorem normalCoordinateWeight10_single {m : ℕ} (r : Fin m → ℕ) (k : Fin m → Fin 10) (h : Fin m) :
    normalCoordinateWeight10 r k (Finsupp.single (h,k h) 1) = 1/(r h : ℝ) := by
  have he (g : Fin m) : (Finsupp.single (h,k h) 1 : SourceMultiblock10.Vars10 m →₀ ℕ) (g,k g) =
      if g = h then 1 else 0 := by
    by_cases hg : g = h
    · subst g; simp
    · simp [Finsupp.single_apply,Prod.mk.injEq,Ne.symm hg,hg]
  simp only [normalCoordinateWeight10]
  simp_rw [he]
  simp [ite_div,Finset.sum_ite_eq,Finset.sum_ite_eq']

theorem inverse10_coeff_zero {m : ℕ} (M : Fin m → Fin 10 → ℤ)
    (k : Fin m → Fin 10) (hk : ∀ h, M h (k h) ≠ 0)
    (r : Fin m → ℕ) (c : ℝ) (P : MvPolynomial (SourceMultiblock10.Vars10 m) ℝ)
    (hindex : ∀ J : SourceMultiblock10.Vars10 m →₀ ℕ, rothSourceDerivativeWeight r J < c →
      ∀ y : Fin m → Fin 10 → ℝ, (∀ h, y h ∈ LinearMap.ker (realNormalForm10 (M h))) →
        MvPolynomial.eval (fun u => y u.1 u.2) (derivative10 J P) = 0)
    (J b : SourceMultiblock10.Vars10 m →₀ ℕ)
    (hbad : rothSourceDerivativeWeight r J + normalCoordinateWeight10 r k b < c) :
    (inverse10 M k (derivative10 J P)).coeff b = 0 := by
  classical
  have go : ∀ n : ℕ, ∀ J b : SourceMultiblock10.Vars10 m →₀ ℕ,
      (∑ h : Fin m, b (h,k h)) = n →
      rothSourceDerivativeWeight r J + normalCoordinateWeight10 r k b < c →
      (inverse10 M k (derivative10 J P)).coeff b = 0 := by
    intro n
    induction n using Nat.strong_induction_on with
    | h n ih =>
      intro J b hn hw
      by_cases hnzero : n = 0
      · have hbzero : ∀ h, b (h,k h) = 0 := by
          intro h
          have hle : b (h,k h) ≤ ∑ g : Fin m, b (g,k g) :=
            Finset.single_le_sum (f := fun g : Fin m => b (g,k g)) (fun _ _ => Nat.zero_le _) (Finset.mem_univ h)
          omega
        have hnonneg : 0 ≤ normalCoordinateWeight10 r k b := by
          unfold normalCoordinateWeight10
          positivity
        have hJ : rothSourceDerivativeWeight r J < c := by linarith
        have hz := normal_zero_restriction10 M k hk (derivative10 J P) (hindex J hJ)
        have he := congrArg (MvPolynomial.coeff b) hz
        rw [zeroNormalCoordinates10_coeff,if_pos hbzero,MvPolynomial.coeff_zero] at he
        exact he
      · have hex : ∃ h : Fin m, b (h,k h) ≠ 0 := by
          by_contra! hzero
          have hs : (∑ h : Fin m, b (h,k h)) = 0 := Finset.sum_eq_zero (fun h _ => hzero h)
          omega
        obtain ⟨h,hh⟩ := hex
        let d := b - Finsupp.single (h,k h) 1
        have hle : Finsupp.single (h,k h) 1 ≤ b := by rw [Finsupp.single_le_iff]; omega
        have hdeq : d + Finsupp.single (h,k h) 1 = b := tsub_add_cancel_of_le hle
        have hcount : (∑ g : Fin m, d (g,k g)) + 1 = n := by
          have hs := congrArg (fun b : SourceMultiblock10.Vars10 m →₀ ℕ => ∑ g : Fin m, b (g,k g)) hdeq
          have he (g : Fin m) : (Finsupp.single (h,k h) 1 : SourceMultiblock10.Vars10 m →₀ ℕ) (g,k g) =
              if g = h then 1 else 0 := by
            by_cases hg : g = h
            · subst g; simp
            · simp [Finsupp.single_apply,Prod.mk.injEq,Ne.symm hg,hg]
          simp_rw [Finsupp.add_apply,he] at hs
          simpa [Finset.sum_add_distrib,Finset.sum_ite_eq,Finset.sum_ite_eq',hn] using hs
        have hcoord : normalCoordinateWeight10 r k d + 1/(r h : ℝ) = normalCoordinateWeight10 r k b := by
          rw [← normalCoordinateWeight10_single r k h,← normalCoordinateWeight10_add,hdeq]
        have hz : (inverse10 M k (derivative10 (J+Finsupp.single (h,k h) 1) P)).coeff d = 0 := by
          apply ih (∑ g : Fin m, d (g,k g)) (by omega) _ d rfl
          rw [rothSource_weight_add,rothSource_weight_single]
          linarith
        have hd : (MvPolynomial.pderiv (h,k h) (inverse10 M k (derivative10 J P))).coeff d = 0 := by
          rw [inverse10_chain,source10_pderiv_hasse,map_nsmul,
            MvPolynomial.coeff_C_mul,MvPolynomial.coeff_smul,hz,smul_zero,mul_zero]
        rw [pderiv_coeff,hdeq] at hd
        exact (mul_eq_zero.mp hd).resolve_left (Nat.cast_ne_zero.mpr (by omega))
  exact go _ J b rfl hbad

theorem forward10_monomial {m : ℕ} (M : Fin m → Fin 10 → ℤ)
    (k : Fin m → Fin 10) (b : SourceMultiblock10.Vars10 m →₀ ℕ) (a : ℝ) :
    forward10 M k (MvPolynomial.monomial b a) =
      MvPolynomial.C a * ((∏ h, (linearNormal10 (K := ℝ) M h) ^ b (h,k h)) *
        (∏ h, ∏ i ∈ Finset.univ.erase (k h), MvPolynomial.X (h,i) ^ b (h,i))) := by
  classical
  rw [forward10,MvPolynomial.eval₂Hom_monomial]
  rw [Finsupp.prod_fintype _ _ (fun _ => by simp),Fintype.prod_prod_type,
    ← Finset.prod_mul_distrib]
  congr 1
  apply Finset.prod_congr rfl
  intro h hh
  rw [← Finset.prod_erase_mul _ _ (Finset.mem_univ (k h))]
  simp only [Prod.fst,Prod.snd,ite_true]
  have he : (∏ i ∈ Finset.univ.erase (k h),
      (if i = k h then linearNormal10 (K := ℝ) M h else MvPolynomial.X (h,i)) ^ b (h,i)) =
      ∏ i ∈ Finset.univ.erase (k h), MvPolynomial.X (h,i) ^ b (h,i) := by
    apply Finset.prod_congr rfl
    intro i hi
    rw [if_neg (Finset.mem_erase.mp hi).1]
  rw [he]
  ring

theorem forward10_monomial_mem {m : ℕ} (M : Fin m → Fin 10 → ℤ)
    (k : Fin m → Fin 10) (r : Fin m → ℕ) (c : ℝ)
    (b : SourceMultiblock10.Vars10 m →₀ ℕ) (a : ℝ) (hb : c ≤ normalCoordinateWeight10 r k b) :
    forward10 M k (MvPolynomial.monomial b a) ∈ sourceNormalIdeal10 M r c := by
  have hg : (∏ h, (linearNormal10 (K := ℝ) M h) ^ b (h,k h)) ∈ sourceNormalIdeal10 M r c :=
    Ideal.subset_span ⟨fun h => b (h,k h),hb,rfl⟩
  rw [forward10_monomial]
  have hm := Ideal.mul_mem_left (sourceNormalIdeal10 M r c)
    (MvPolynomial.C a * (∏ h, ∏ i ∈ Finset.univ.erase (k h), MvPolynomial.X (h,i) ^ b (h,i))) hg
  convert hm using 1
  ring

theorem sourceNormalIdeal10_of_derivative_restrictions {m : ℕ}
    (M : Fin m → Fin 10 → ℤ) (hM : ∀ h, M h ≠ 0)
    (r : Fin m → ℕ) (c : ℝ) (P : MvPolynomial (SourceMultiblock10.Vars10 m) ℝ)
    (hindex : ∀ J : SourceMultiblock10.Vars10 m →₀ ℕ, rothSourceDerivativeWeight r J < c →
      ∀ y : Fin m → Fin 10 → ℝ, (∀ h, y h ∈ LinearMap.ker (realNormalForm10 (M h))) →
        MvPolynomial.eval (fun u => y u.1 u.2) (derivative10 J P) = 0) :
    P ∈ sourceNormalIdeal10 M r c := by
  classical
  have hex (h : Fin m) : ∃ i : Fin 10, M h i ≠ 0 := by
    by_contra! hz
    exact hM h (funext hz)
  choose k hk using hex
  have hcoeff (b : SourceMultiblock10.Vars10 m →₀ ℕ) (hb : normalCoordinateWeight10 r k b < c) :
      (inverse10 M k P).coeff b = 0 := by
    have hz := inverse10_coeff_zero M k hk r c P hindex 0 b
      (by simpa [rothSourceDerivativeWeight] using hb)
    simpa only [derivative10_zero_index] using hz
  have hsupport (b) (hb : b ∈ (inverse10 M k P).support) :
      c ≤ normalCoordinateWeight10 r k b := by
    by_contra! hlt
    exact (MvPolynomial.mem_support_iff.mp hb) (hcoeff b hlt)
  have hsum := congrArg (forward10 M k) (inverse10 M k P).as_sum
  rw [forward6_inverse10 M k hk P,map_sum] at hsum
  rw [hsum]
  apply Ideal.sum_mem
  intro b hb
  exact forward10_monomial_mem M k r c b _ (hsupport b hb)

theorem integer_grid_high_normal_index10 {m : ℕ} (r : Fin m → ℕ) (hr : ∀ h, 0 < r h)
    (P : MvPolynomial (SourceMultiblock10.Vars10 m) ℤ) (hP : homogeneous10 r P)
    (w : Fin m → Fin 9 → Fin 10 → ℤ) (M : Fin m → Fin 10 → ℤ)
    (hM : ∀ h, M h ≠ 0)
    (hQ : ∀ h, LinearMap.ker (normalForm10 (M h)) =
      Submodule.span ℚ (Set.range (fun j i => (w h j i : ℚ))))
    (hR : ∀ h, LinearMap.ker (realNormalForm10 (M h)) =
      Submodule.span ℝ (Set.range (fun j i => (w h j i : ℝ))))
    (eta : ℝ) (heta : 0 < eta)
    (hgrid : ∀ a : Fin m → Fin 9 → ℕ, (∀ h j, 1 ≤ a h j ∧ a h j ≤ Nat.floor eta⁻¹+1) →
      ∀ J : SourceMultiblock10.Vars10 m →₀ ℕ, rothSourceDerivativeWeight r J < 2*eta*m →
        integerValue J P (gridPoint w a) = 0) :
    (∀ J : SourceMultiblock10.Vars10 m →₀ ℕ, rothSourceDerivativeWeight r J < (m : ℝ)*eta →
      (∀ y : Fin m → Fin 10 → ℚ, (∀ h, y h ∈ LinearMap.ker (normalForm10 (M h))) →
        MvPolynomial.eval (fun u => y u.1 u.2) (derivative10 J (P.map (Int.castRingHom ℚ))) = 0) ∧
      (∀ y : Fin m → Fin 10 → ℝ, (∀ h, y h ∈ LinearMap.ker (realNormalForm10 (M h))) →
        MvPolynomial.eval (fun u => y u.1 u.2) (derivative10 J (P.map (Int.castRingHom ℝ))) = 0)) ∧
    P.map (Int.castRingHom ℝ) ∈ sourceNormalIdeal10 M r ((m : ℝ)*eta) := by
  have hrestr : ∀ J : SourceMultiblock10.Vars10 m →₀ ℕ, rothSourceDerivativeWeight r J < (m : ℝ)*eta →
      (∀ y : Fin m → Fin 10 → ℚ, (∀ h, y h ∈ LinearMap.ker (normalForm10 (M h))) →
        MvPolynomial.eval (fun u => y u.1 u.2) (derivative10 J (P.map (Int.castRingHom ℚ))) = 0) ∧
      (∀ y : Fin m → Fin 10 → ℝ, (∀ h, y h ∈ LinearMap.ker (realNormalForm10 (M h))) →
        MvPolynomial.eval (fun u => y u.1 u.2) (derivative10 J (P.map (Int.castRingHom ℝ))) = 0) := by
    intro J hJ
    have hc : ∀ a : Fin m → Fin 9 → ℕ, (∀ h j, 1 ≤ a h j ∧ a h j ≤ Nat.floor eta⁻¹+1) →
        ∀ B : SourceMultiblock10.Vars10 m →₀ ℕ, (∀ h, (∑ i : Fin 10, B (h,i)) ≤ Nat.floor ((r h : ℝ)*eta)) →
          integerValue B (derivative10 J P) (gridPoint w a) = 0 := by
      intro a ha B hB
      exact hasse_grid_composition10 P J B _
        (hgrid a ha (B+J) (additional_cutoff10 r hr eta heta J B hJ hB))
    constructor
    · intro y hy
      apply integer_grid_rational_restriction10 r hr P hP J w eta heta hc y
      intro h
      rw [← hQ h]
      exact hy h
    · intro y hy
